# Current integration contract reference

For current module interfaces, state ownership, coordinate conventions, persistence invariants and regression notes, read `handoff/PROJECT_HANDOFF.md` and the module README files, then inspect the implementation. `CLAUDE.md` supplies continuation guidance.

The historical parallel-agent assignment contract is intentionally omitted: its folder ownership and coordination restrictions no longer apply. This export is a standalone development snapshot.

===== BEGIN FILE: README.md =====
# Deadfall

An original, browser-native isometric survival sandbox inspired by the systems of Project Zomboid. The game opens directly into a new survivor or resumes the survivor stored in this browser. No installation, account, CDN, runtime package, or external asset download is required.

This release is a playable foundation, not a recreation of Project Zomboid's content or complete simulation. Survivor imagery is extracted from the lighting study supplied with the project; environment artwork is original procedural drawing.

## Play

[Play the hosted game](https://deadfall-cwdknte.solid.site) in a desktop browser. Click the world to focus it.

| Control | Action |
| --- | --- |
| WASD / arrow keys | Move in screen directions |
| Shift | Run (uses stamina and attracts zombies) |
| Z | Sneak |
| Mouse click / Space / 1 | Attack |
| E | Search nearby supplies / open or close an opening |
| F | Collect nearby supplies |
| B | Barricade an opening / remove its plank |
| V | Build a wooden barrier in the facing direction |
| X | Dismantle a nearby structure |
| R | Rest; moving or a nearby zombie interrupts |
| 2 / 3 / 4 | Drink / eat / bandage |
| I / 5 | Inventory |
| C / K / M | Crafting / skills / map |
| H | Controls |
| Escape / P | Pause |
| Mouse wheel / + / − | Zoom |

Start by searching the nearby supply crate. Find the safehouse door, then explore for materials, medical supplies, and weapons. Running and gunfire are loud. Zombies can see and hear you, and route around obstacles. Barricade openings or build a barrier to buy time. Food, water, fatigue, bleeding, and stamina matter. Death ends the current survivor; a new survivor resets the world.

## Saves

The game saves periodically and after important actions. It also attempts a save when the tab is hidden or closed. The same browser and site automatically resume the latest successful save. Pause provides explicit save, export, and import actions. IndexedDB is the primary store with a localStorage fallback. Saves are versioned and validated; unreadable existing saves are preserved instead of silently replaced.

Saves are local to this browser profile and origin. Export JSON to move a survivor to another device or keep a backup. There is no cloud account or cloud synchronization in this release. Closing a tab abruptly can lose changes since the last completed save.

## Run from source

Serve the project directory with any static HTTP server. For example:

```sh
python3 -m http.server 8080
```

Open `http://localhost:8080`. Module workers require HTTP(S); opening index.html with a file URL is not supported. The optional tests use Node 20 or newer:

```sh
node --test tests/*.test.mjs save/*.test.mjs ai/*.test.mjs world/*.test.mjs render/*.test.mjs
```

No npm install is needed to play or serve the game. See `build/optimized/` for the static release packaging script, `tests/RESULTS.md` for executed checks and measured performance limitations, and `ROADMAP.md` for expansion milestones.

## Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart LR
  Input[Keyboard and pointer] --> Game[Game orchestration]
  Game --> ECS[Entity and component registry]
  Game --> World[Seeded chunk world]
  Game --> Systems[Needs, time, weather, combat]
  Game --> AI[AI controller]
  AI <-->|Snapshots and decisions| Worker[Worker perception and A*]
  World --> Renderer[Isometric Canvas renderer]
  ECS --> Renderer
  Systems --> Renderer
  Game --> UI[DOM HUD and panels]
  Game <-->|Versioned snapshots| Save[IndexedDB and fallback storage]
```

- `main.js` boots the game and presents actionable startup errors.
- `game.js` owns integration, inputs, inventory actions, construction, entity registration, and save timing.
- `engine/` provides a fixed simulation timestep, bounded catch-up, and normalized browser input.
- `ecs/` contains sparse component stores. The game registers the survivor and zombies as entities; state views share their mutable components with simulation systems.
- `world/` deterministically generates tile chunks and structures. Mutations are recorded by stable prop IDs and survive chunk unloading and saves.
- `ai/` manages worker snapshots and applies returned movement. `workers/` performs perception, behavior decisions, and pathfinding away from the rendering thread.
- `systems/` implements melee/ranged combat, player needs, weather, and the accelerated day/night clock.
- `render/` draws tiles, depth-sorted props and characters, lighting, particles, and weather. `assets/` contains the supplied character facings and art pipeline files.
- `ui/` owns the accessible DOM HUD, inventory, crafting, health, skills, map, and pause/save controls.
- `save/` validates and stores versioned state, with export/import and fallback handling.
- `perf/`, `tests/`, and `build/optimized/` provide diagnostics, automated verification, and release packaging.

The current renderer uses Canvas 2D for a small, dependency-free download. A WebGL lighting pipeline, large population simulation, multistorey buildings, and the deeper systems needed for full PZ scale belong to later milestones in the roadmap.

===== END FILE: README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: ROADMAP.md =====
# Deadfall prototype roadmap

Deadfall is an original browser survival foundation. Its intended scope is a
single survivor exploring a procedurally generated town, gathering supplies,
fighting or avoiding zombies, crafting simple tools and securing a shelter.
It is not a feature-complete recreation of a commercial survival game.

## Prototype scope

- Native browser ES modules and Canvas2D rendering with no runtime packages.
- Deterministic chunked terrain, readable interiors, doors, windows and loot.
- Fixed-step movement, collision, melee/ranged combat, survival needs and weather.
- Worker-owned zombie sensing and A* pathfinding with bounded per-update work.
- Inventory, crafting, barricading, wooden barrier placement/dismantling,
  pause/help and a survival journal.
- Versioned local saves, validated JSON import/export and a storage fallback.

Actual verification results and any open failures are recorded separately in
`tests/RESULTS.md`. A listed feature does not imply production-scale coverage,
mobile control support, or sustained performance on every device.

## Next playable improvements

1. Longer play sessions: tune depletion, loot, damage, infection and progression
   from recorded playtests; add more clear early objectives and contextual help.
2. Interaction depth: explicit target selection when loot and doors overlap,
   multiple barricade layers, advanced construction, furniture disassembly and
   weapon wear. Basic wooden barrier placement and dismantling already work.
3. Persistence resilience: cross-tab ownership, save-slot selection, backups and
   user-facing recovery when a browser clears storage. Cloud sync needs a server.
4. Presentation: richer animation, more original environmental art, spatial
   sound, controller rebinding and reduced-motion/high-contrast options.

## Expansion requires additional systems

- Large hordes: actor interest management, sleeping simulation outside the
  active area, worker transfer profiling and systematic CPU/memory budgets.
- Larger settlements: navigation regions, asynchronous chunk generation,
  authored landmarks, streaming stress tests and persistent world migration.
- Rendering scale: a WebGL renderer, GPU sprite batching, adaptive quality and
  representative hardware testing would be additional work. The current
  renderer uses Canvas2D with cached sprite and static-ground layers.
- Deeper survival: farming, cooking, power/water networks, construction,
  vehicles, advanced injuries and NPC schedules are separate projects.
- Multiplayer: authoritative networking, reconciliation, security and hosting
  must be designed before shared-state gameplay can be promised.

## Release gate for a broader public build

Maintain passing unit and integration tests; verify actual IndexedDB reloads,
worker failure handling and world changes across unloading; complete extended
survival sessions on representative laptops; repeat browser checks in current
Chromium, Firefox and Safari; measure draw time, worker latency, actor counts,
chunk retention and save sizes; and publish the tested hardware/browser matrix.
Headless Chromium measurements alone are not a performance guarantee.

===== END FILE: ROADMAP.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: ai/README.md =====
# AI and simulation integration

Public exports follow `CONTRACT.md`:

- `AIController` from `ai/controller.js`: construct with the live World; call `update(state, dt)` with seconds; call `destroy()` on restart/disposal.
- `updateNeeds(state, dt)` from `systems/needs.js`.
- `updateWeather(state, dt)` from `systems/weather.js`.
- `attack(state, world, target?)` and `updateCombat(state, dt)` from `systems/combat.js`. An optional attack target is `{x, y}` in world coordinates. `WEAPONS` is also exported.

All functions leave paused/dead simulation unchanged. Normal integration order is input and player movement, world update, weather, needs, AI, combat. The AI never moves the player. The game owns `state.elapsed`, spawning, inventory use, rest energy/health bonuses, notifications, persistence, and corpse cleanup.

## Worker behavior

The canonical native module-worker entry is `workers/aiWorker.js`; `AIController` loads that entry with `{type:'module'}`. It imports the existing `workers/ai-worker.js` handler, which remains available for compatibility. The worker receives plain actor snapshots every 0.125 seconds and a transferred 65×65 collision grid every 0.5 seconds or after significant player movement. Grid occupancy is sampled at tile centers using `world.isBlocked`. No main-thread A* is imported or executed. Only living zombies within 30 tiles are active. Live corner collision and attack rays are revalidated on the main thread, so old worker results cannot walk or attack through a recently closed door.

The worker uses eight-direction A* with no diagonal corner cutting, a binary priority heap, a 2,400-node search limit, cached paths, and a maximum of seven searches per snapshot. Priority rotates through the crowd. Visual perception includes facing, close-range awareness, occlusion, daylight, and fog. Sound attracts nearby zombies through cover, rain reduces hearing, visible alerted neighbors draw groups into pursuit, and last-known positions expire. Idle actors wander. Local steering separates crowds. Pursuit moves slower than the walking survivor to make positioning and stamina meaningful.

`state.ai` exposes `{worker, active, paths, error?}` for diagnostics. If workers cannot start or stop responding, direct sight-based steering keeps movement playable; obstacle pathfinding and worker perception are unavailable in that fallback. Actors still obey live collision. There is no door-breaking behavior.

The simulation covers local survival encounters. Zombies beyond the active radius remain dormant; there is no town-wide migration, persistent offscreen pursuit, or scent tracking. Hearing currently uses the survivor's position and a scalar noise radius rather than independent world sound sources. Search limits bound the work for crowds, so actors can briefly wait for a route when many need new paths at once.

`workers/simulation.js` exports pure `ZombieSimulation.step(snapshot)` for tests. `workers/pathfinding.js` exports `findPath`, `blocked`, `lineOfSight`, and `clearPassage`. The worker protocol is `{type:'step', seq, dt, grid?, player:{x,y}, zombies, time, noise, weather}` → `{type:'result', seq, actors, paths}`. Grid is `{x,y,width,height,cells:Uint8Array}`, with 0 walkable and 1 blocked. Returned actors have `{id,mode,vx,vy,dirX,dirY,canAttack}`.

## Simulation ownership and feedback

- Needs owns hunger, thirst, energy depletion, stamina drain/recovery, bleeding and infection damage, and death from those causes. Set `player.moving`, `player.running`, and `player.resting`. Rest stamina recovers faster; rest energy and health bonuses remain in `game.js`.
- Weather advances five game minutes per second, handles midnight/multiple-day rollover, and stores deterministic six-hour weather periods in `weather.period`. Intensity and temperature ease smoothly. `weather.daylight` and `weather.wind` are available for rendering.
- Combat owns attack/hurt cooldowns, enemy strikes, particle physics/lifetimes, and noise decay. Noise is an approximate audible radius in tiles: bat 6, axe 7, pistol 32. Movement and interactions can raise `state.noise` with `Math.max`.
- Bats and axes sweep a forward cone and can hit two targets, with reduced secondary damage. Pistols consume ammunition, hit the nearest aimed target, and obey walls/range. All attacks cost stamina and obey cooldowns. Damage, knockback, skill gains, kill counts, bleeding, infection chances, death, and short invulnerability are resolved here.
- Player visual fields: `attackTimer`, `attackDuration`, `attackWeapon`, `hurtTimer`. Zombie fields: `hitTimer`, `attackTimer`, `knockbackX`, `knockbackY`, `deathTimer`, `deathDirection`. Dead zombies retain `health:0` for the game's ECS cleanup.
- `state.lastAttack` is `{x,y,dirX,dirY,weapon,range,ttl,duration}`. Particles are `{type,x,y,z,vx,vy,vz,life,maxLife,size,color}`. Seconds are used for all timers. The transient particle count is capped at 220.
- Needs/combat set `state.dead=true` on lethal damage. The game should detect the transition with its own flag if it needs a death notification or immediate save.

## Validation

Run `node --test ai/simulation.test.mjs tests/ai.test.mjs tests/systems.test.mjs` from the project root. The owned suite exercises combat, needs, clock/weather, full pursuit around an obstacle, shared group awareness, fallback collision, and stale-worker collision. QA's suite covers A* legality, supercover corner blocking, hearing, and a real Node worker bridge executing the browser worker protocol.

An integration probe using the generated world confirmed that the closed safehouse east door blocks sight and routing; opening its live prop enables both sight and the six-tile route to the spawn room. Browser validation is coordinated separately; the Node worker check is not a browser test.

===== END FILE: ai/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: ai/controller.js =====
const STEP = 0.125;
const ACTIVE_RADIUS = 30;

/** Worker computes decisions; the main thread integrates validated movement. */
export class AIController {
  constructor(world) {
    this.world = world;
    this.worker = null;
    this.commands = new Map();
    this.pending = false;
    this.pendingFor = 0;
    this.sinceStep = STEP;
    this.sinceGrid = 1;
    this.sequence = 0;
    this.lastApplied = 0;
    this.gridCenter = null;
    this.destroyed = false;
    this.workerError = null;
    try {
      if (typeof Worker !== 'undefined') {
        this.worker = new Worker(new URL('../workers/aiWorker.js', import.meta.url), { type: 'module', name: 'deadfall-ai' });
        this.worker.onmessage = ({ data }) => {
          this.pending = false; this.pendingFor = 0;
          if (data.type === 'error') { this.failWorker(data.message); return; }
          if (data.type !== 'result' || data.seq < this.lastApplied || this.destroyed) return;
          this.lastApplied = data.seq;
          this.commands = new Map(data.actors.map(actor => [actor.id, actor]));
          this.paths = data.paths;
        };
        this.worker.onerror = event => { event.preventDefault?.(); this.failWorker(event.message || 'Worker unavailable'); };
        this.worker.onmessageerror = () => this.failWorker('Invalid AI worker response');
      }
    } catch (error) { this.failWorker(error.message); }
  }

  failWorker(message) {
    this.worker?.terminate(); this.worker = null;
    this.pending = false; this.commands.clear(); this.workerError = message;
    // Direct collision-aware steering is still playable where module workers are blocked.
    // This fallback does not perform pathfinding on the main thread.
  }

  rasterize(player) {
    const radius = 32, width = radius * 2 + 1;
    const x = Math.floor(player.x) - radius, y = Math.floor(player.y) - radius;
    const cells = new Uint8Array(width * width);
    for (let row = 0; row < width; row++) for (let col = 0; col < width; col++) {
      cells[row * width + col] = this.world.isBlocked(x + col + 0.5, y + row + 0.5) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    this.gridCenter = { x: player.x, y: player.y };
    this.sinceGrid = 0;
    return { x, y, width, height: width, cells };
  }

  clear(x, y, radius = 0.2) {
    return !this.world.isBlocked(x - radius, y - radius) && !this.world.isBlocked(x + radius, y - radius) &&
      !this.world.isBlocked(x - radius, y + radius) && !this.world.isBlocked(x + radius, y + radius);
  }

  rayClear(ax, ay, bx, by) {
    const steps = Math.ceil(Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay) * 5);
    for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
      if (!this.clear(ax + (bx - ax) * i / steps, ay + (by - ay) * i / steps, 0.08)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  fallback(state, zombies) {
    const p = state.player;
    for (const z of zombies) {
      const dx = p.x - z.x, dy = p.y - z.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      const aware = d < 9 || d < (state.noise || 0);
      const clear = aware && this.rayClear(z.x, z.y, p.x, p.y);
      const attack = clear && d < 0.96;
      this.commands.set(z.id, { id: z.id, mode: attack ? 'attack' : clear ? 'chase' : 'idle',
        vx: clear && !attack ? dx / (d || 1) * 0.72 : 0,
        vy: clear && !attack ? dy / (d || 1) * 0.72 : 0,
        dirX: dx / (d || 1), dirY: dy / (d || 1), canAttack: attack });
    }
  }

  update(state, dt) {
    if (this.destroyed || state.paused || state.dead || !Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0) return;
    dt = Math.min(dt, 0.1);
    const p = state.player;
    const active = state.zombies.filter(z => z.health > 0 && Math.hypot(z.x - p.x, z.y - p.y) <= ACTIVE_RADIUS);
    this.sinceStep += dt; this.sinceGrid += dt;
    if (this.pending) {
      this.pendingFor += dt;
      if (this.pendingFor > 3) this.failWorker('AI worker response timed out');
    }
    if (this.sinceStep >= STEP && !this.pending) {
      const stepDt = Math.min(this.sinceStep, 0.5);
      this.sinceStep = 0;
      if (this.worker) {
        const moved = this.gridCenter && Math.hypot(p.x - this.gridCenter.x, p.y - this.gridCenter.y) > 3;
        const grid = !this.gridCenter || this.sinceGrid >= 0.5 || moved ? this.rasterize(p) : undefined;
        const snapshot = { type: 'step', seq: ++this.sequence, dt: stepDt, grid,
          player: { x: p.x, y: p.y }, time: state.time, noise: state.noise || 0,
          weather: { type: state.weather?.type },
          zombies: active.map(z => ({ id: z.id, x: z.x, y: z.y, health: z.health,
            mode: z.mode, dirX: z.dirX, dirY: z.dirY })) };
        this.pending = true; this.pendingFor = 0;
        try { this.worker.postMessage(snapshot, grid ? [grid.cells.buffer] : []); }
        catch (error) { this.failWorker(error.message); this.fallback(state, active); }
      } else this.fallback(state, active);
    }
    for (const z of state.zombies) {
      if (z.health <= 0) { z.vx = z.vy = 0; z.canAttack = false; continue; }
      if (Math.hypot(z.x - p.x, z.y - p.y) > ACTIVE_RADIUS) { z.canAttack = false; z.vx = z.vy = 0; continue; }
      const command = this.commands.get(z.id);
      if (!command) continue;
      z.mode = command.mode;
      // Revalidate close attacks against the current door state, between worker snapshots.
      z.canAttack = command.canAttack && this.rayClear(z.x, z.y, p.x, p.y);
      const stagger = z.hitTimer > 0 ? 0.18 : 1;
      z.vx = command.vx * stagger; z.vy = command.vy * stagger;
      const kx = z.knockbackX || 0, ky = z.knockbackY || 0;
      const dx = (z.vx + kx) * dt, dy = (z.vy + ky) * dt;
      // Axis separation permits sliding along walls; small substeps prevent tunneling.
      const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx, dy) / 0.12));
      for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
        if (this.clear(z.x + dx / steps, z.y)) z.x += dx / steps;
        if (this.clear(z.x, z.y + dy / steps)) z.y += dy / steps;
      }
      z.knockbackX = kx * Math.exp(-12 * dt); z.knockbackY = ky * Math.exp(-12 * dt);
      if (Math.hypot(command.dirX, command.dirY) > 0.1) { z.dirX = command.dirX; z.dirY = command.dirY; }
    }
    state.ai = { worker: !!this.worker, active: active.length, paths: this.paths || 0,
      ...(this.workerError ? { error: this.workerError } : {}) };
  }

  destroy() {
    this.destroyed = true;
    this.worker?.terminate(); this.worker = null;
    this.commands.clear(); this.pending = false;
  }
}

===== END FILE: ai/controller.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: ai/simulation.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { AIController } from './controller.js';
import { ZombieSimulation } from '../workers/simulation.js';
import { attack, updateCombat } from '../systems/combat.js';
import { updateNeeds } from '../systems/needs.js';
import { updateWeather } from '../systems/weather.js';

function state() {
  return { seed: 74219, day: 1, time: 540, elapsed: 0, paused: false, dead: false,
    weather: { type: 'overcast', intensity: 0.25, temperature: 16 }, noise: 0,
    player: { id: 1, x: 2.5, y: 2.5, health: 100, dirX: 1, dirY: 0,
      stamina: 100, energy: 100, hunger: 86, thirst: 82, bleeding: 0, infection: 0,
      equipped: 'bat', inventory: [{ id: 'bat', qty: 1 }], skills: { strength: 0, fitness: 0 }, kills: 0 },
    zombies: [], particles: [] };
}
function zombie(overrides = {}) {
  return { id: 101, x: 3.5, y: 2.5, dirX: -1, dirY: 0, health: 80, mode: 'idle', attackCooldown: 0, ...overrides };
}
const openWorld = { isBlocked: () => false };

test('melee sweep obeys facing, reach, cooldown and one-time kill credit', () => {
  const s = state();
  const front = zombie({ health: 1 }), back = zombie({ id: 102, x: 1.5 }), far = zombie({ id: 103, x: 8 });
  s.zombies = [front, back, far];
  assert.deepEqual(attack(s, openWorld), { hit: true, killed: 1, message: 'Infected put down.' });
  assert.equal(back.health, 80); assert.equal(far.health, 80); assert.equal(s.player.kills, 1);
  const stamina = s.player.stamina;
  assert.equal(attack(s, openWorld).hit, false); assert.equal(s.player.stamina, stamina);
  updateCombat(s, 1);
  assert.equal(attack(s, openWorld).hit, false); assert.equal(s.player.kills, 1);
});

test('a wall prevents both melee and pistol hits', () => {
  const world = { isBlocked: (x, y) => Math.floor(x) === 3 && Math.floor(y) === 2 };
  for (const weapon of ['bat', 'pistol']) {
    const s = state(); s.player.equipped = weapon;
    s.player.inventory = [{ id: weapon, qty: 1 }, { id: 'ammo', qty: 3 }];
    s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 4.1 })];
    assert.equal(attack(s, world).hit, false); assert.equal(s.zombies[0].health, 80);
  }
});

test('pistol consumes one round, hits nearest target, and makes loud noise', () => {
  const s = state(); s.player.equipped = 'pistol';
  s.player.inventory = [{ id: 'pistol', qty: 1 }, { id: 'ammo', qty: 1 }];
  s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 6 }), zombie({ id: 102, x: 9 })];
  assert.equal(attack(s, openWorld).hit, true);
  assert.ok(s.zombies[0].health < 80); assert.equal(s.zombies[1].health, 80);
  assert.equal(s.player.inventory.some(item => item.id === 'ammo'), false);
  assert.equal(s.noise, 32);
  updateCombat(s, 1);
  assert.match(attack(s, openWorld).message, /ammunition/);
});

test('explicit aim changes direction while stamina prevents exhausted attacks', () => {
  const s = state(); s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 2.5, y: 3.5 })];
  assert.equal(attack(s, openWorld, { x: 2.5, y: 6 }).hit, true);
  assert.equal(s.player.dirX, 0); assert.equal(s.player.dirY, 1);
  updateCombat(s, 1); s.player.stamina = 0;
  const health = s.zombies[0].health;
  assert.match(attack(s, openWorld).message, /exhausted/); assert.equal(s.zombies[0].health, health);
});

test('enemy strikes require range and attack permission and respect invulnerability', () => {
  const s = state();
  s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 3.1, mode: 'attack', canAttack: false })];
  updateCombat(s, 0.016); assert.equal(s.player.health, 100);
  s.zombies[0].canAttack = true;
  s.zombies.push(zombie({ id: 102, x: 2.9, mode: 'attack', canAttack: true }));
  updateCombat(s, 0.016);
  assert.ok(s.player.health >= 88 && s.player.health <= 93);
  const health = s.player.health;
  updateCombat(s, 0.016); assert.equal(s.player.health, health);
});

test('fatal enemy damage sets death and expired feedback is removed', () => {
  const s = state(); s.player.health = 1;
  s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 3.1, mode: 'attack', canAttack: true })];
  updateCombat(s, 0.016); assert.equal(s.dead, true); assert.equal(s.player.health, 0);
  const t = state(); t.particles = [{ x: 0, y: 0, life: 0.1 }]; t.lastAttack = { ttl: 0.1 };
  updateCombat(t, 0.2); assert.equal(t.particles.length, 0); assert.equal(t.lastAttack, undefined);
});

test('running drains stamina and hydration faster; standing restores stamina', () => {
  const rest = state(), run = state(); run.player.moving = true; run.player.running = true;
  updateNeeds(rest, 5); updateNeeds(run, 5);
  assert.ok(run.player.stamina < rest.player.stamina);
  assert.ok(run.player.thirst < rest.player.thirst);
  run.player.running = false; run.player.moving = false;
  const stamina = run.player.stamina; updateNeeds(run, 1); assert.ok(run.player.stamina > stamina);
  assert.ok(run.player.stamina <= 100);
});

test('bleeding and unmet needs are harmful and pause freezes all systems', () => {
  const s = state(); s.player.bleeding = 30; s.player.hunger = s.player.thirst = 0;
  updateNeeds(s, 5); assert.ok(s.player.health < 100);
  s.paused = true;
  const before = JSON.stringify(s);
  updateNeeds(s, 10); updateCombat(s, 10); updateWeather(s, 10); attack(s, openWorld);
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(s), before);
});

test('needs do not duplicate rest health/energy bonuses and always bound values', () => {
  const s = state(); Object.assign(s.player, { resting: true, health: 70, energy: 60, stamina: 90 });
  updateNeeds(s, 10);
  assert.equal(s.player.health, 70); assert.equal(s.player.energy, 60); assert.equal(s.player.stamina, 100);
  s.player.bleeding = 100; s.player.health = 0.01; updateNeeds(s, 1);
  assert.equal(s.dead, true); assert.equal(s.player.health, 0);
});

test('clock rolls across multiple days and deterministic weather survives serialization', () => {
  const a = state(); a.time = 1435;
  updateWeather(a, 2); assert.equal(a.time, 5); assert.equal(a.day, 2);
  const b = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a));
  updateWeather(a, 600); updateWeather(b, 600);
  assert.deepEqual(a.weather, b.weather); assert.equal(a.day, 4); assert.equal(a.time, 125);
  assert.ok(a.weather.intensity >= 0 && a.weather.intensity <= 1);
});

test('noise investigation routes around a closed wall and uses the opening', () => {
  const grid = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 12, height: 12, cells: new Uint8Array(144) };
  for (let y = 0; y < 12; y++) if (y !== 8) grid.cells[y * 12 + 5] = 1;
  const simulation = new ZombieSimulation();
  const z = zombie({ x: 3.5, y: 5.5, dirX: 1 });
  const first = simulation.step({ seq: 1, dt: 0.125, grid, player: { x: 7.5, y: 5.5 }, zombies: [z], time: 720, noise: 16 });
  assert.equal(first.actors[0].mode, 'wander'); assert.equal(first.actors[0].canAttack, false);
  assert.ok(first.paths > 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
    const command = simulation.step({ seq: i + 2, dt: 0.125, player: { x: 7.5, y: 5.5 }, zombies: [z], time: 720, noise: 16 }).actors[0];
    z.mode = command.mode; z.dirX = command.dirX; z.dirY = command.dirY;
    z.x += command.vx * 0.125; z.y += command.vy * 0.125;
    assert.equal(grid.cells[Math.floor(z.y) * 12 + Math.floor(z.x)], 0, 'route enters a blocked tile');
  }
  assert.ok(Math.hypot(z.x - 7.5, z.y - 5.5) < 1.1);
});

test('controller fallback preserves player position and respects a closing door', () => {
  let closed = false;
  const world = { isBlocked: (x, y) => closed && Math.floor(x) === 3 && Math.floor(y) === 2 };
  const ai = new AIController(world), s = state();
  s.player.x = 4.5; s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 2.5 })];
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) ai.update(s, 0.1);
  assert.ok(s.zombies[0].x > 2.5); assert.equal(s.player.x, 4.5);
  closed = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) ai.update(s, 0.1);
  assert.ok(s.zombies[0].x < 2.81); assert.equal(s.zombies[0].canAttack, false);
  ai.destroy(); const x = s.zombies[0].x; ai.update(s, 1); assert.equal(s.zombies[0].x, x);
});

test('stale worker decisions cannot move through a newly closed door', () => {
  const s = state(); s.player.x = 5.5; s.zombies = [zombie({ x: 2.7 })];
  const world = { isBlocked: x => Math.floor(x) === 3 };
  const ai = new AIController(world);
  ai.worker = { postMessage() {}, terminate() {} }; ai.sinceStep = 0; ai.sinceGrid = 0;
  ai.commands.set(101, { id: 101, mode: 'chase', vx: 0.9, vy: 0, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, canAttack: false });
  for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) ai.update(s, 0.1);
  assert.ok(s.zombies[0].x < 2.81); assert.equal(s.player.x, 5.5);
  ai.destroy();
});

test('a visible alerted neighbor recruits a zombie facing away from the survivor', () => {
  const grid = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 12, height: 12, cells: new Uint8Array(144) };
  const player = { x: 8.5, y: 5.5 };
  const facingAway = zombie({ id: 101, x: 3.5, y: 5.5, dirX: -1 });
  const lookout = zombie({ id: 102, x: 5.5, y: 5.5, dirX: 1 });
  const alone = new ZombieSimulation().step({ seq: 1, grid, player, zombies: [facingAway], time: 720, noise: 0 });
  assert.notEqual(alone.actors[0].mode, 'chase');
  const group = new ZombieSimulation().step({ seq: 1, grid, player, zombies: [facingAway, lookout], time: 720, noise: 0 });
  assert.equal(group.actors.find(z => z.id === 101).mode, 'chase');
});

test('pursuit memory expires after losing both sight and sound', () => {
  const grid = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 20, height: 20, cells: new Uint8Array(400) };
  const sim = new ZombieSimulation(), z = zombie({ x: 3.5, y: 5.5, dirX: 1 });
  const first = sim.step({ seq: 1, grid, player: { x: 8.5, y: 5.5 }, zombies: [z], time: 720, noise: 0 });
  assert.equal(first.actors[0].mode, 'chase');
  let result;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) result = sim.step({ seq: i + 2, dt: 1, player: { x: 18.5, y: 18.5 }, zombies: [z], time: 720, noise: 0 });
  assert.notEqual(result.actors[0].mode, 'chase');
  assert.equal(result.actors[0].canAttack, false);
});

===== BEGIN FILE: assets/README.md =====
# Deadfall art provenance

All terrain textures, isometric furniture, walls, cars, trees, fallback infected figures, visual effects and lighting are original Canvas 2D drawing code in `render/`. They are generated locally; the game never loads assets from a CDN or image service.

`survivor/face-0.png` through `face-7.png` are the eight survivor views embedded in the user-supplied `/workspace/references/sprite-lighting.html`. They were extracted without modification for this project, as authorized in the project contract. The source facings are SE, SW, NW, NE, S, N, E, W. The renderer also derives cached desaturated, gray-green and bloodied infected variants from these views at runtime, retaining the same human proportions. No commercial game assets are included.

The three user reference images informed the warm lamp lighting, worn timber, muted olive palette, boarded windows, and cutaway interior composition. They are not embedded as game backgrounds.

===== END FILE: assets/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: build/optimized/build.mjs =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Reproducible static package. Run: node build/optimized/build.mjs */
import { readFile, writeFile, readdir, mkdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, resolve, relative, extname, join } from 'node:path';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { gzipSync, brotliCompressSync, constants } from 'node:zlib';

const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
const output = resolve(root, 'build/optimized/site');
const directories = ['data', 'engine', 'ecs', 'render', 'assets', 'world', 'ai', 'workers', 'systems', 'ui', 'save', 'perf'];
const entryFiles = ['index.html', 'main.js', 'game.js'];
const mime = { '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.css': 'text/css', '.json': 'application/json', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.png': 'image/png', '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.webp': 'image/webp', '.woff2': 'font/woff2' };
const compressible = new Set(['.html', '.js', '.css', '.json', '.svg', '.txt']);

async function walk(folder) {
  let entries;
  try { entries = await readdir(folder, { withFileTypes: true }); }
  catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return []; throw error; }
  const files = [];
  for (const e of entries.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
    if (e.name.startsWith('.') || e.name.endsWith('.md') || e.name.endsWith('.mjs') || e.name.endsWith('-results.json') || /\.test\.[cm]?js$/.test(e.name)) continue;
    const path = join(folder, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) files.push(...await walk(path));
    else if (e.isFile()) files.push(path);
  }
  return files;
}

const sources = new Map();
for (const path of [...entryFiles.map(f => resolve(root, f)), ...(await Promise.all(directories.map(d => walk(resolve(root, d))))).flat()]) {
  sources.set(relative(root, path).split('\\').join('/'), await readFile(path));
}
// Validate module graph before replacing a previously usable package.
for (const match of sources.get('index.html').toString().matchAll(/(?:src|href)\s*=\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]/g)) {
  const reference = match[1];
  if (/^(data:|#)/.test(reference)) continue;
  if (/^https?:|^\/\//.test(reference)) throw new Error(`External runtime resource in index.html: ${reference}`);
  const path = reference.replace(/^\.?\//, '').split(/[?#]/)[0];
  if (!sources.has(path)) throw new Error(`Missing HTML resource: ${reference}`);
}
for (const [name, data] of sources) {
  if (extname(name) !== '.js') continue;
  const specifiers = [...data.toString().matchAll(/(?:\bfrom\s*|\bimport\s*\(?\s*|new\s+URL\s*\(\s*)['"]([^'"]+)['"]/g)].map(m => m[1]);
  for (const specifier of specifiers) {
    if (/^(https?:)?\/\//.test(specifier)) throw new Error(`External runtime dependency in ${name}: ${specifier}`);
    if (!specifier.startsWith('.')) continue;
    const target = relative(root, resolve(root, dirname(name), specifier)).split('\\').join('/');
    if (!sources.has(target)) throw new Error(`Missing module or worker: ${name} → ${specifier}`);
  }
}
await rm(output, { recursive: true, force: true });
await mkdir(output, { recursive: true });
const manifest = { format: 1, files: {}, totals: { rawBytes: 0, gzipBytes: 0, brotliBytes: 0 } };
for (const [name, data] of [...sources.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
  const dest = resolve(output, name);
  await mkdir(dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(dest, data);
  const entry = { bytes: data.length, sha256: createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex'), contentType: mime[extname(name)] || 'application/octet-stream' };
  if (compressible.has(extname(name))) {
    const gz = gzipSync(data, { level: 9 });
    const br = brotliCompressSync(data, { params: { [constants.BROTLI_PARAM_QUALITY]: 11 } });
    await writeFile(dest + '.gz', gz);
    await writeFile(dest + '.br', br);
    entry.gzipBytes = gz.length;
    entry.brotliBytes = br.length;
  }
  manifest.files[name] = entry;
  manifest.totals.rawBytes += data.length;
  manifest.totals.gzipBytes += entry.gzipBytes ?? data.length;
  manifest.totals.brotliBytes += entry.brotliBytes ?? data.length;
}
await writeFile(resolve(output, 'manifest.json'), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + '\n');
console.log(JSON.stringify({ output, files: sources.size, ...manifest.totals }, null, 2));

===== END FILE: build/optimized/build.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: deploy/server.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Dependency-free static hosting with precompressed release assets."""
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, SimpleHTTPRequestHandler
from pathlib import Path
import os

class StaticHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'
    extensions_map = {**SimpleHTTPRequestHandler.extensions_map, '.js': 'text/javascript', '.json': 'application/json', '.wasm': 'application/wasm', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml'}
    def end_headers(self):
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
        self.send_header('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff')
        self.send_header('Referrer-Policy', 'same-origin')
        super().end_headers()
    def send_head(self):
        path = Path(self.translate_path(self.path))
        if path.is_dir():
            path = path / 'index.html'
        accepted = {}
        for part in self.headers.get('Accept-Encoding', '').split(','):
            pieces = part.strip().split(';')
            quality = 1.0
            for option in pieces[1:]:
                if option.strip().startswith('q='):
                    try: quality = float(option.strip()[2:])
                    except ValueError: quality = 0
            accepted[pieces[0]] = quality
        if path.is_file():
            for encoding, suffix in [('br','.br'),('gzip','.gz')]:
                compressed = Path(str(path) + suffix)
                if accepted.get(encoding, 0) > 0 and compressed.is_file():
                    data = compressed.open('rb')
                    self.send_response(200)
                    self.send_header('Content-Type', self.guess_type(str(path)))
                    self.send_header('Content-Encoding', encoding)
                    self.send_header('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding')
                    self.send_header('Content-Length', str(compressed.stat().st_size))
                    self.end_headers()
                    return data
        return super().send_head()
    def list_directory(self, path):
        self.send_error(404)
        return None

if __name__ == '__main__':
    os.chdir(os.environ.get('DEADFALL_ROOT','/workspace/deadfall'))
    ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1',8080),StaticHandler).serve_forever()

===== END FILE: deploy/server.py =====


===== BEGIN FILE: ecs/world.js =====
/**
 * Sparse component storage. Components remain ordinary game-owned JSON values.
 *
 * const actors = new ECS();
 * const id = actors.create({ position: { x: 7.5, y: 7.5 }, health: 100 });
 * actors.add(id, 'velocity', { x: 0, y: 0 });
 * for (const [id, position, velocity] of actors.query('position', 'velocity')) {}
 */
export class ECS {
  constructor() {
    this.entities = new Set();
    this.components = new Map();
    this._nextId = 1;
  }

  get size() { return this.entities.size; }

  /** Create an entity with an automatically allocated numeric ID. */
  create(components = {}) {
    while (this.entities.has(this._nextId)) this._nextId += 1;
    const id = this._nextId++;
    return this.add(id, components);
  }

  /**
   * add(id, name, value) sets a component, creating the entity if necessary.
   * add(id, { name: value, ... }) adds a batch of components.
   * add({ name: value, ... }) is shorthand for create(components).
   */
  add(id, component, value) {
    if (id !== null && typeof id === 'object' && !Array.isArray(id)) return this.create(id);
    this._checkId(id);
    let entries;
    if (component === undefined) entries = [];
    else if (typeof component === 'string') entries = [[component, value]];
    else if (component !== null && typeof component === 'object' && !Array.isArray(component)) {
      entries = Object.entries(component);
    } else throw new TypeError('Components must be named with a string or supplied as an object.');
    for (const [name] of entries) this._checkName(name);
    this.entities.add(id);
    for (const [name, data] of entries) {
      let store = this.components.get(name);
      if (!store) this.components.set(name, store = new Map());
      store.set(id, data);
    }
    return id;
  }

  get(id, component) {
    return this.components.get(component)?.get(id);
  }

  has(id, component) {
    return component === undefined ? this.entities.has(id) : Boolean(this.components.get(component)?.has(id));
  }

  /** Remove one component, or remove the entire entity when no name is supplied. */
  remove(id, component) {
    if (component !== undefined) {
      const store = this.components.get(component);
      if (!store) return false;
      const removed = store.delete(id);
      if (!store.size) this.components.delete(component);
      return removed;
    }
    if (!this.entities.delete(id)) return false;
    for (const [name, store] of this.components) {
      store.delete(id);
      if (!store.size) this.components.delete(name);
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Iterate [entityId, ...componentValues] in the requested component order.
   * Uses the smallest store as the candidate set. The query is live: component
   * changes are visible on subsequent iterations. query() visits every entity.
   */
  *query(...names) {
    if (names.length === 1 && Array.isArray(names[0])) names = names[0];
    if (!names.length) {
      for (const id of this.entities) yield [id];
      return;
    }
    let candidates;
    const stores = [];
    for (const name of names) {
      this._checkName(name);
      const store = this.components.get(name);
      if (!store?.size) return;
      stores.push(store);
      if (!candidates || store.size < candidates.size) candidates = store;
    }
    for (const id of candidates.keys()) {
      if (this.entities.has(id) && stores.every(store => store.has(id))) {
        yield [id, ...stores.map(store => store.get(id))];
      }
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.entities.clear();
    this.components.clear();
    // Do not recycle IDs: callers may still hold references to deleted entities.
  }

  _checkId(id) {
    if ((typeof id === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(id) && id >= 0) ||
        (typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0)) return;
    throw new TypeError('Entity IDs must be nonnegative safe integers or nonempty strings.');
  }

  _checkName(name) {
    if (typeof name !== 'string' || name.length === 0) {
      throw new TypeError('Component names must be nonempty strings.');
    }
  }
}

export default ECS;

===== END FILE: ecs/world.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: engine/README.md =====
# Deadfall engine and actor storage

The browser loads `main.js`, waits for the document, dynamically imports `Game`,
awaits `game.init()`, exposes `window.game`, and calls `game.start()`. Loading and
runtime failures show a readable recovery panel. Dynamic import allows missing
or invalid game modules to reach that panel too. Native ES modules are served
as static files; there are no runtime package dependencies.

## Simulation loop

```js
import { Engine } from './engine/loop.js';
const engine = new Engine({
  update: dt => game.update(dt),
  render: alpha => game.render(alpha),
});
engine.start();
```

`update` receives seconds at a fixed 1/60 step. `render` receives interpolation
alpha in `[0, 1]` once per animation frame. At most five updates run per frame;
excess elapsed time is discarded and counted in `engine.droppedTime`. This
keeps a slow or suspended browser from running an unbounded simulation backlog.
Hidden documents do not update or render; visibility changes reset elapsed time.

`start()` and `stop()` are idempotent. `resetClock()` discards the accumulator;
`destroy()` also removes the visibility listener and prevents future restarts.
Public diagnostics are `running`, `fps` (smoothed), `frame`, and `droppedTime`.
Constructor options `step`, `maxSteps`, and `onError(error)` are optional.
Callback failures stop the loop; without `onError`, they propagate to the
browser error handler. Game pause and death rules remain in `Game` so menus
can continue rendering while simulation actions are paused.

## Input

`new Input(canvas)` exposes lowercase held keyboard `keys`, `movement()` and
`consume(key)`. Movement is normalized in screen directions: up is `(0, -1)`;
`Game` converts to world directions before collision checks. Space is `' '`.
A press edge survives key release until consumed. Repeated keydown events do
not retrigger a consumed edge. Call `clear()` when dismissing a modal or
restarting so gameplay commands pressed during a pause do not execute later.

Typing, text composition, and Ctrl/Meta/Alt shortcuts are ignored. The game uses
Z for sneak, keeping browser shortcuts available. Blur, hidden-tab transitions,
and entering an editable field clear held controls and queued press edges.

`pointer.x/y` are CSS pixels relative to the canvas bounding rectangle, independent
of the canvas backing resolution. `pointer.down` means primary-button held;
`buttons`, `button`, and `inside` supply additional state. Pointer capture keeps
releases reliable outside the canvas. Cancellation and lost capture stop held
attacks. `consume('mouse0')` reads a primary-click edge; other buttons follow
`mouse1`, `mouse2`, etc. `consumeWheel()` returns signed wheel steps (positive
means zoom out). Only the canvas intercepts wheel scrolling and context menus.

`destroy()` removes every listener and restores the canvas's previous tab index
and touch action. Input never owns gameplay actions or camera projection.

## Entity/component storage

```js
import { ECS } from './ecs/world.js';
const actors = new ECS();
const id = actors.create({ survivor: state.player, position: state.player });
actors.add(id, 'tag', 'player');
for (const [id, survivor, position] of actors.query('survivor', 'position')) {
  // Component values are the same objects supplied by Game.
}
```

`create(components)` allocates an ID. `add(id, name, value)` sets a component;
`add(id, components)` adds a batch, and `add(components)` allocates an entity.
`get(id, name)` retrieves a value. `has(id, name?)` tests a component or entity.
`remove(id, name?)` removes a component or the complete entity and returns a
boolean. `clear()` empties the registry without recycling automatically issued
IDs. `size`, `entities` (Set), and `components` (Map of component Maps) are public.

`query(...names)` yields `[id, ...values]` in the requested component order,
using the smallest component store as its candidate set. `query()` visits all
entities; `query(['a', 'b'])` is also supported. Queries are live and deletion of
the current entity is safe. The game owns the plain JSON component values, so
player/zombie mutations are immediately visible to systems and persistence.

## Checks and packaging

Run `node --test tests/*.test.mjs save/*.test.mjs` (or `npm test` when npm is installed). QA owns
the repository tests. `npm run check` checks the engine entry-point syntax.
`node build/optimized/build.mjs` validates the static module graph and prepares
compressed assets; QA owns that packager. `python3 -m http.server 8080` serves a
local development copy. Production deployment belongs to the coordinator.

===== END FILE: engine/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: engine/input.js =====
const SCROLL_KEYS = new Set([' ', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright']);
const normalize = key => key === 'Spacebar' || key === 'Space' ? ' ' : String(key).toLowerCase();

function isEditable(target) {
  return Boolean(target?.isContentEditable || target?.closest?.(
    'input, textarea, select, [contenteditable=""], [contenteditable="true"], [role="textbox"]'
  ));
}

/** Keyboard edges and pointer positions in CSS pixels, independent of canvas DPR. */
export class Input {
  constructor(canvas) {
    if (!canvas?.addEventListener || !canvas?.getBoundingClientRect) {
      throw new TypeError('Input requires a canvas element.');
    }
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.keys = new Set();
    this.pointer = { x: 0, y: 0, down: false, button: -1, buttons: 0, inside: false };
    this._pressed = new Set();
    this._codes = new Map();
    this._listeners = [];
    this._wheel = 0;
    this._pointerId = null;
    this._document = canvas.ownerDocument ?? globalThis.document;
    this._window = this._document?.defaultView ?? globalThis.window;
    this._oldTabIndex = canvas.getAttribute('tabindex');
    this._oldTouchAction = canvas.style.touchAction;
    if (this._oldTabIndex === null) canvas.tabIndex = 0;
    canvas.style.touchAction = 'none';

    this._listen(this._window, 'keydown', event => {
      if (isEditable(event.target) || event.isComposing || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.altKey) return;
      const key = normalize(event.key);
      const interactive = event.target?.closest?.('button, a[href], [role="button"]');
      if (interactive && (key === ' ' || key === 'enter')) return;
      if (SCROLL_KEYS.has(key)) event.preventDefault();
      if (!this.keys.has(key) && !event.repeat) this._pressed.add(key);
      this.keys.add(key);
      if (event.code) this._codes.set(event.code, key);
    });
    this._listen(this._window, 'keyup', event => {
      const key = this._codes.get(event.code) ?? normalize(event.key);
      if (event.code) this._codes.delete(event.code);
      if (![...this._codes.values()].includes(key)) this.keys.delete(key);
    });
    this._listen(this._window, 'blur', () => this.clear());
    this._listen(this._document, 'visibilitychange', () => this.clear());
    this._listen(this._document, 'focusin', event => {
      if (isEditable(event.target)) this.clear();
    });
    this._listen(canvas, 'contextmenu', event => event.preventDefault());
    this._listen(canvas, 'pointerdown', event => {
      if (this._pointerId !== null && this._pointerId !== event.pointerId) return;
      this._pointerId = event.pointerId;
      this._position(event);
      this.pointer.button = event.button;
      this.pointer.buttons = event.buttons;
      this.pointer.down = Boolean(event.buttons & 1);
      this._pressed.add(`mouse${event.button}`);
      canvas.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      try { canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); } catch { /* Pointer may have ended. */ }
    });
    this._listen(this._window, 'pointermove', event => {
      if (this._pointerId !== null && this._pointerId !== event.pointerId) return;
      if (event.pointerType === 'touch' && this._pointerId === null) return;
      this._position(event);
      if (this._pointerId !== null) {
        this.pointer.buttons = event.buttons;
        this.pointer.down = Boolean(event.buttons & 1);
      }
    });
    this._listen(this._window, 'pointerup', event => {
      if (this._pointerId !== event.pointerId) return;
      this._position(event);
      this.pointer.buttons = event.buttons;
      this.pointer.down = Boolean(event.buttons & 1);
      if (!event.buttons) this._releasePointer();
    });
    this._listen(this._window, 'pointercancel', event => {
      if (this._pointerId === event.pointerId) this._releasePointer();
    });
    this._listen(canvas, 'lostpointercapture', event => {
      if (this._pointerId === event.pointerId) this._releasePointer();
    });
    this._listen(canvas, 'pointerleave', () => { this.pointer.inside = false; });
    this._listen(canvas, 'wheel', event => {
      if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      this._wheel += Math.sign(event.deltaY);
    }, { passive: false });
  }

  _listen(target, type, listener, options) {
    target.addEventListener(type, listener, options);
    this._listeners.push([target, type, listener, options]);
  }

  _position(event) {
    const bounds = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.pointer.x = event.clientX - bounds.left;
    this.pointer.y = event.clientY - bounds.top;
    this.pointer.inside = this.pointer.x >= 0 && this.pointer.y >= 0 &&
      this.pointer.x < bounds.width && this.pointer.y < bounds.height;
  }

  _releasePointer() {
    const id = this._pointerId;
    this._pointerId = null;
    this.pointer.down = false;
    this.pointer.buttons = 0;
    this.pointer.button = -1;
    try {
      if (id !== null && this.canvas.hasPointerCapture?.(id)) this.canvas.releasePointerCapture(id);
    } catch { /* Browsers may release capture before the event is delivered. */ }
  }

  /** True exactly once per press; a short tap survives until consumed. */
  consume(key) {
    return this._pressed.delete(normalize(key));
  }

  /** Screen directions; the game applies the isometric world transform. */
  movement() {
    const x = Number(this.keys.has('d') || this.keys.has('arrowright')) -
      Number(this.keys.has('a') || this.keys.has('arrowleft'));
    const y = Number(this.keys.has('s') || this.keys.has('arrowdown')) -
      Number(this.keys.has('w') || this.keys.has('arrowup'));
    const length = Math.hypot(x, y);
    return length ? { x: x / length, y: y / length } : { x: 0, y: 0 };
  }

  /** Signed wheel steps since the last call; positive means zoom out. */
  consumeWheel() {
    const delta = this._wheel;
    this._wheel = 0;
    return delta;
  }

  clear() {
    this.keys.clear();
    this._codes.clear();
    this._pressed.clear();
    this._wheel = 0;
    this._releasePointer();
  }

  destroy() {
    this.clear();
    for (const [target, type, listener, options] of this._listeners) {
      target.removeEventListener(type, listener, options);
    }
    this._listeners.length = 0;
    if (this._oldTabIndex === null) this.canvas.removeAttribute('tabindex');
    else this.canvas.setAttribute('tabindex', this._oldTabIndex);
    this.canvas.style.touchAction = this._oldTouchAction;
  }
}

export default Input;

===== END FILE: engine/input.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: engine/loop.js =====
/** Fixed-step simulation with interpolated rendering and bounded catch-up. */
export class Engine {
  constructor({ update, render, step = 1 / 60, maxSteps = 5, onError } = {}) {
    if (typeof update !== 'function' || typeof render !== 'function') {
      throw new TypeError('Engine requires update(dt) and render(alpha) callbacks.');
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(step) || step <= 0 || !Number.isInteger(maxSteps) || maxSteps < 1) {
      throw new RangeError('Engine step must be positive and maxSteps must be a positive integer.');
    }
    this.update = update;
    this.render = render;
    this.step = step;
    this.maxSteps = maxSteps;
    this.onError = onError;
    this.running = false;
    this.fps = 0;
    this.frame = 0;
    this.droppedTime = 0;
    this._request = null;
    this._lastTime = null;
    this._accumulator = 0;
    this._destroyed = false;
    this._document = globalThis.document;
    this._tick = this._tick.bind(this);
    this._visibilityChanged = () => this.resetClock();
    this._document?.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this._visibilityChanged);
  }

  start() {
    if (this._destroyed) throw new Error('A destroyed Engine cannot be restarted.');
    if (this.running) return this;
    if (typeof globalThis.requestAnimationFrame !== 'function') {
      throw new Error('Animation frames are unavailable in this environment.');
    }
    this.running = true;
    this.resetClock();
    this._request = globalThis.requestAnimationFrame(this._tick);
    return this;
  }

  stop() {
    this.running = false;
    if (this._request !== null) globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame(this._request);
    this._request = null;
    this.resetClock();
    return this;
  }

  /** Discard elapsed wall time after a pause, visibility change, or load. */
  resetClock() {
    this._lastTime = null;
    this._accumulator = 0;
    this.fps = 0;
  }

  _tick(timestamp) {
    this._request = null;
    if (!this.running) return;
    if (this._document?.hidden) {
      this.resetClock();
      this._schedule();
      return;
    }
    try {
      const elapsed = this._lastTime === null ? 0 : Math.max(0, (timestamp - this._lastTime) / 1000);
      this._lastTime = timestamp;
      if (elapsed > 0) {
        const measured = 1 / elapsed;
        this.fps = this.fps ? this.fps + (measured - this.fps) * 0.08 : measured;
      }
      // At most maxSteps updates per frame. A suspended tab cannot drain needs.
      const budget = this.step * this.maxSteps;
      const delta = Math.min(elapsed, budget);
      this.droppedTime += elapsed - delta;
      this._accumulator += delta;
      let steps = 0;
      while (this._accumulator + 1e-10 >= this.step && steps < this.maxSteps && this.running) {
        this._accumulator = Math.max(0, this._accumulator - this.step);
        this.update(this.step);
        steps += 1;
      }
      if (!this.running) return;
      if (this._accumulator >= this.step) {
        const remainder = this._accumulator % this.step;
        this.droppedTime += this._accumulator - remainder;
        this._accumulator = remainder;
      }
      this.render(Math.min(1, this._accumulator / this.step));
      this.frame += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      this.stop();
      if (typeof this.onError === 'function') this.onError(error);
      else throw error;
      return;
    }
    this._schedule();
  }

  _schedule() {
    if (this.running && this._request === null) {
      this._request = globalThis.requestAnimationFrame(this._tick);
    }
  }

  destroy() {
    this.stop();
    this._document?.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this._visibilityChanged);
    this._destroyed = true;
  }
}

export default Engine;

===== END FILE: engine/loop.js =====

import {Engine} from './engine/loop.js';

export function createState(){
 return {version:1,seed:74219,time:9*60,day:1,elapsed:0,paused:false,dead:false,
  weather:{type:'overcast',intensity:.25,temperature:16},
  player:{id:1,x:7.5,y:7.5,dirX:1,dirY:0,health:100,hunger:86,thirst:82,energy:100,stamina:100,bleeding:0,infection:0,inventory:[{id:'bat',qty:1},{id:'water',qty:2},{id:'beans',qty:2},{id:'bandage',qty:2},{id:'plank',qty:4},{id:'nails',qty:8},{id:'hammer',qty:1}],equipped:'bat',skills:{strength:0,fitness:0,crafting:0},kills:0},
  zombies:[],particles:[],messages:[],worldChanges:{},objectives:{looted:0,crafted:0,barricaded:0},stats:{distance:0},settings:{sound:true},noise:0,saveStatus:'Preparing save…'};
}
const distance=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);
export class Game{
 constructor(){this.state=createState();this.lastSave=0;this.lastFrame=performance.now();this.saveBusy=false;this.attackHeld=0;this.spawned=new Set();this.audio=null;this.resting=false;this._listeners=[];}
 async init(){
  this.canvas=document.getElementById('game-canvas');
  if(!this.canvas){this.canvas=document.createElement('canvas');this.canvas.id='game-canvas';document.body.prepend(this.canvas);}
  let saved=null;
  try{saved=await loadGame();}catch(e){console.warn('Load failed',e);this.persistenceBlocked=true;this.state.saveStatus='Existing save could not be read';}
  if(saved)this.restore(saved);
  this.world=new World(this.state.seed,this.state.worldChanges);this.world.update(this.state.player.x,this.state.player.y);
  this.renderer=new Renderer(this.canvas,this.world);this.input=new Input(this.canvas);this.ai=new AIController(this.world);
  if(!saved)this.spawnInitial();
  this.initEntities();
  this.ui=new UI(this);this.ui.mount();
  this.engine=new Engine({update:dt=>this.update(dt),render:alpha=>this.render(alpha)});
  const onResize=()=>this.renderer.resize();window.addEventListener('resize',onResize);this.renderer.resize();
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden){if(!this.state.paused&&!this.state.dead)this.action('pause');this.save();this.ui.update(this.state);}});
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>this.save());
  this.notify(saved?'Survivor restored. Your story continues.':'Find supplies. Secure a shelter. Survive the night.');
  if(this.persistenceBlocked)this.notify('Existing save could not be read and has been preserved. Start a new survivor or import a backup to resume saving.');
  else if(!saved)await this.save();else this.state.saveStatus='Survivor restored';
  this.updateNearby();this.ui.update(this.state);
 }
 restore(saved){
  const fresh=createState();
  this.state={...fresh,...saved,player:{...fresh.player,...saved.player,skills:{...fresh.player.skills,...saved.player?.skills}},objectives:{...fresh.objectives,...saved.objectives},stats:{...fresh.stats,...saved.stats},settings:{...fresh.settings,...saved.settings},messages:[],particles:[],paused:false};
  this.state.dead=this.state.player.health<=0;this.state.nearby=null;this._deathHandled=this.state.dead;
  this.spawned=new Set(this.state.spawnedChunks||[]);
 }
 start(){this.render(0);this.engine.start();}
 initEntities(){
  this.ecs=new ECS();this.entityIds=new Map();
  this.ecs.create({survivor:this.state.player,position:this.state.player});
  this.syncEntities();
 }
 syncEntities(){
  for(const z of this.state.zombies){if(!this.entityIds.has(z.id)){this.entityIds.set(z.id,this.ecs.create({zombie:z,position:z}));}}
  const actors=[];
  for(const [id,z] of this.ecs.query('zombie')){
   if(z.health<=0&&distance(z,this.state.player)>42){this.ecs.remove(id);this.entityIds.delete(z.id);}
   else actors.push(z);
  }
  this.state.zombies=actors;
 }
 getItem(id){return ITEMS[id]||{name:id,category:'Supply',weight:.1,description:''};}
 count(id){return this.state.player.inventory.find(i=>i.id===id)?.qty||0;}
 addItem(id,qty=1){const a=this.state.player.inventory.find(i=>i.id===id);if(a)a.qty+=qty;else this.state.player.inventory.push({id,qty});}
 removeItem(id,qty=1){const a=this.state.player.inventory.find(i=>i.id===id);if(!a||a.qty<qty)return false;a.qty-=qty;this.state.player.inventory=this.state.player.inventory.filter(i=>i.qty>0);return true;}
 getRecipes(){return RECIPES.map(r=>({...r,canCraft:r.ingredients.every(i=>this.count(i.id)>=i.qty)}));}
 canReach(prop){
  const p=this.state.player,tx=prop.x+.5,ty=prop.y+.5,d=Math.hypot(tx-p.x,ty-p.y);
  for(let t=.2;t<d-.6;t+=.2){if(this.world.isBlocked(p.x+(tx-p.x)*t/d,p.y+(ty-p.y)*t/d))return false;}
  return true;
 }
 getNearbyLoot(){const p=this.findLoot();return p?.loot?.filter(i=>i.qty>0)||[];}
 findLoot(){return this.world.getNearby(this.state.player.x,this.state.player.y,2.2).filter(p=>p.loot?.some(i=>i.qty>0)&&!p.looted&&this.canReach(p)).sort((a,b)=>distance(a,this.state.player)-distance(b,this.state.player))[0];}
 nearestOpening(){return this.world.getNearby(this.state.player.x,this.state.player.y,2.4).filter(p=>(p.type==='door'||p.type==='window')&&this.canReach(p)).sort((a,b)=>distance(a,this.state.player)-distance(b,this.state.player))[0];}
 notify(text){if(!text)return;this.state.messages.push({text,at:performance.now()});this.state.messages=this.state.messages.slice(-5);this.ui?.notify?.(text);}
 spawnInitial(){
  const places=[[17.5,10.5],[20.5,13.5],[21.5,8.5],[25.5,18.5],[27.5,16.5],[13.5,24.5],[29.5,7.5],[7.5,25.5],[-5.5,16.5]];
  this.state.zombies=places.filter(([x,y])=>!this.world.isBlocked(x,y)).map(([x,y],i)=>({id:100+i,x,y,health:80,mode:'idle',dirX:0,dirY:1,attackCooldown:0}));
  for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)this.spawned.add(x+','+y);
 }
 spawnExplored(){
  const p=this.state.player,cx=Math.floor(p.x/16),cy=Math.floor(p.y/16);
  const key=cx+','+cy;if(this.spawned.has(key))return;this.spawned.add(key);
  let seed=(cx*73856093^cy*19349663^this.state.seed)>>>0;
  const random=()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return seed/4294967296;};
  for(let i=0;i<6&&this.state.zombies.filter(z=>z.health>0).length<100;i++){
   const x=cx*16+random()*28-6,y=cy*16+random()*28-6;
   if(!this.world.isBlocked(x,y)&&Math.hypot(x-p.x,y-p.y)>9)this.state.zombies.push({id:Date.now()+i,x,y,health:80,mode:'idle',dirX:1,dirY:0,attackCooldown:0});
  }
 }
 updateNearby(){
  const p=this.state.player;
  this.state.nearby=this.findLoot()||this.nearestOpening()||this.world.getNearby(p.x,p.y,1.8).find(o=>o.type==='bed')||null;
  this.state.location=this.world.getLocation?.(p.x,p.y)||this.world.getTile(p.x,p.y).room||'Cedar County outskirts';
 }
 update(dt){
  const s=this.state,p=s.player;
  if(this.input.consume('escape')){if(s.paused){this.ui.toggle?.('close');s.paused=false;}else this.action('pause');}
  for(const [key,action] of [['i','inventory'],['c','crafting'],['m','map'],['k','skills'],['h','help']])if(this.input.consume(key))this.action(action);
  if(this.input.consume('p')){if(s.paused){this.ui.toggle('close');this.action('resume');}else this.action('pause');}
  const wheel=this.input.consumeWheel?.()||0;if(wheel)this.action(wheel>0?'zoomOut':'zoomIn');
  if(this.input.consume('+')||this.input.consume('='))this.action('zoomIn');if(this.input.consume('-'))this.action('zoomOut');
  if(s.paused||s.dead){if(s.dead)this.ui.update(s);return;}
  s.elapsed+=dt;
  const move=this.input.movement();
  let mx=move.x+move.y,my=move.y-move.x,mag=Math.hypot(mx,my);
  const running=this.input.keys.has('shift')&&p.stamina>8;
  const sneaking=this.input.keys.has('z');
  s.sneaking=sneaking;s.running=running&&mag>0;p.running=s.running;p.sneaking=sneaking;p.resting=this.resting;
  if(mag>0){
   this.resting=false;mx/=mag;my/=mag;
   const speed=(running?3.5:sneaking?1.05:2.05)*(p.energy<15?.7:1);
   const dx=mx*speed*dt,dy=my*speed*dt,oldX=p.x,oldY=p.y;
   if(this.canMove(p.x+dx,p.y))p.x+=dx;
   if(this.canMove(p.x,p.y+dy))p.y+=dy;
   p.dirX=mx;p.dirY=my;p.moving=true;
   s.stats.distance+=Math.hypot(p.x-oldX,p.y-oldY);
   s.noise=Math.max(s.noise,running?9:sneaking?.6:3);
   if(running)p.skills.fitness+=dt*.025;
  }else p.moving=false;
  if(this.resting){
   if(s.zombies.some(z=>z.health>0&&distance(z,p)<5)){this.resting=false;this.notify('Too dangerous to rest.');}
   else{p.energy=Math.min(100,p.energy+dt*1.6);p.health=Math.min(100,p.health+dt*.1);}
  }
  this.world.update(p.x,p.y);this.spawnExplored();this.syncEntities();this.updateNearby();
  if(this.input.consume('e'))this.action('interact');
  if(this.input.consume('f'))this.action('loot');
  if(this.input.consume('b'))this.action('barricade');
  if(this.input.consume('v'))this.action('build');
  if(this.input.consume('x'))this.action('dismantle');
  if(this.input.consume('r'))this.action('rest');
  if(this.input.consume('5'))this.action('inventory');
  for(const [key,id] of [['1',p.equipped],['2','water'],['3','beans'],['4','bandage']])if(this.input.consume(key)){if(key==='1')this.doAttack();else this.action('use',id);}
  if(this.input.consume(' '))this.doAttack();
  this.attackHeld-=dt;
  if(this.input.pointer.down&&this.attackHeld<=0){
   const target=this.renderer.screenToWorld(this.input.pointer.x,this.input.pointer.y);
   const dx=target.x-p.x,dy=target.y-p.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(d>.1){p.dirX=dx/d;p.dirY=dy/d;}
   this.doAttack(target);this.attackHeld=.42;
  }
  updateWeather(s,dt);updateNeeds(s,dt);this.ai.update(s,dt);updateCombat(s,dt);
  if(p.health<=0&&!this._deathHandled){this._deathHandled=true;s.dead=true;p.health=0;this.notify('Your survivor has fallen.');this.save();}
  if(s.elapsed-this.lastSave>15){this.lastSave=s.elapsed;this.save();}
 }
 canMove(x,y){const r=.22;return ![[x-r,y-r],[x+r,y-r],[x-r,y+r],[x+r,y+r]].some(([xx,yy])=>this.world.isBlocked(xx,yy));}
 doAttack(target){
  this.resting=false;const out=attack(this.state,this.world,target);
  if(out?.message)this.notify(out.message);
  if(out?.hit)this.tone(72,.08,'sawtooth',.06);else this.tone(150,.025,'triangle',.02);
 }
 render(alpha){
  const now=performance.now(),dt=now-this.lastFrame;this.lastFrame=now;
  this.state.render={fps:Math.round(this.engine?.fps||1000/Math.max(dt,1)),zoom:this.renderer.zoom};
  this.state.resting=this.resting;
  this.renderer.render(this.state,alpha);this.ui.update(this.state);
 }
 async save(){
  if(this.persistenceBlocked)return;if(this.saveBusy){this.saveQueued=true;return;}this.saveBusy=true;
  try{this.state.worldChanges=this.world?.serialize()||this.state.worldChanges;this.state.spawnedChunks=[...this.spawned];this.state.saveStatus='Saving…';this.ui?.setSaveStatus?.('saving');await saveGame(this.state);this.ui?.setSaveStatus?.('saved');this.state.saveStatus='Saved just now';this.state.savedAt=Date.now();}
  catch(e){this.ui?.setSaveStatus?.('error');this.state.saveStatus='Save failed — export a backup';this.notify('Could not save in this browser. Export a backup from the pause menu.');console.warn(e);}
  finally{this.saveBusy=false;if(this.saveQueued){this.saveQueued=false;this.save();}}
 }
 async action(name,payload){
  const s=this.state,p=s.player;
  if(['inventory','crafting','skills','map','help'].includes(name)){this.ui.toggle(name);return;}
  if(name==='pause'){if(payload?.source==='ui'){s.paused=true;this.input?.clear();}else this.ui.toggle('pause');return;}
  if(name==='resume'){s.paused=false;this.input?.clear();return;}
  if(name==='save'){await this.save();return;}
  if(name==='export'){s.worldChanges=this.world.serialize();s.spawnedChunks=[...this.spawned];return exportSave(s);}
  if(name==='import'){
   const wasBlocked=this.persistenceBlocked;
   try{
    const next=await importSave(payload);
    // A reload fires pagehide; the old survivor must not overwrite this import.
    this.persistenceBlocked=true;this.saveQueued=false;
    await saveGame(next);location.reload();
   }catch(e){this.persistenceBlocked=wasBlocked;this.notify('Import failed: '+e.message);}return;
  }
  if(name==='restart'){
   await clearSave();this.persistenceBlocked=false;this.resting=false;this.attackHeld=0;this.ai.destroy();this.restore(createState());this.world=new World(this.state.seed,{});this.world.update(this.state.player.x,this.state.player.y);this.renderer.world=this.world;this.renderer.groundBlood?.clear();this.renderer.actorMotion?.clear();this.renderer.cameraReady=false;this.ai=new AIController(this.world);this.spawnInitial();this.initEntities();this.ui.toggle('close');this.state.paused=false;this.lastSave=0;await this.save();this.notify('A new survivor. A new chance.');return;
  }
  if(name==='zoomIn'||name==='zoomOut'){this.renderer.setZoom(Math.max(.6,Math.min(1.8,this.renderer.zoom+(name==='zoomIn'?.1:-.1))));return;}
  if(name==='sound'){s.settings.sound=!s.settings.sound;if(s.settings.sound)this.tone(440,.1);return;}
  if(s.dead)return;
  if(name==='attack'){this.doAttack();return;}
  if(name==='equip'){const id=typeof payload==='string'?payload:payload?.id;if(this.count(id)&&['bat','axe','pistol'].includes(id)){p.equipped=id;this.notify(this.getItem(id).name+' equipped.');}return;}
  if(name==='use'){
   const id=typeof payload==='string'?payload:payload?.id;
   if(!this.count(id)){this.notify('No '+this.getItem(id).name.toLowerCase()+' remaining.');return;}
   if(['bat','axe','pistol'].includes(id))return this.action('equip',id);
   if(id==='water'){if(p.thirst>=99){this.notify('You are already hydrated.');return;}p.thirst=Math.min(100,p.thirst+38);}
   else if(id==='beans'){if(p.hunger>=99){this.notify('You are already well fed.');return;}p.hunger=Math.min(100,p.hunger+32);p.energy=Math.min(100,p.energy+8);}
   else if(id==='bandage'){if(p.health>=100&&!p.bleeding){this.notify('No injuries to bandage.');return;}p.bleeding=0;p.health=Math.min(100,p.health+12);}
   else{this.notify('Use this supply in crafting or barricading.');return;}
   this.removeItem(id);this.notify(this.getItem(id).name+' used.');this.tone(340,.08);this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='craft'){
   const id=typeof payload==='string'?payload:payload?.id,r=this.getRecipes().find(r=>r.id===id);
   if(!r?.canCraft){this.notify('Gather the missing materials first.');return;}
   r.ingredients.forEach(i=>this.removeItem(i.id,i.qty));this.addItem(r.result.id,r.result.qty);p.skills.crafting+=12;s.objectives.crafted++;this.notify('Crafted '+r.name.toLowerCase()+'.');this.tone(540,.08);this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='loot'){
   const prop=this.findLoot();if(!prop){this.notify('No supplies within reach.');return;}
   const items=prop.loot.filter(i=>i.qty>0);items.forEach(i=>this.addItem(i.id,i.qty));prop.loot=[];prop.looted=true;s.objectives.looted++;
   this.notify('Collected '+items.map(i=>i.qty+' '+this.getItem(i.id).name.toLowerCase()).join(', ')+'.');this.updateNearby();this.tone(460,.065);this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='interact'){
   if(this.findLoot())return this.action('loot');
   const door=this.nearestOpening();
   if(door){if(door.barricaded){this.notify('Barricaded. Press B to remove a plank.');return;}door.open=!door.open;door.blocked=!door.open;this.notify(door.open?'Opened '+door.type+'.':'Closed '+door.type+'.');s.noise=4;this.save();return;}
   if(this.world.getNearby(p.x,p.y,2).some(o=>o.type==='bed'))return this.action('rest');
   this.notify('Move closer to a container, door, or window.');return;
  }
  if(name==='barricade'){
   const door=this.nearestOpening();if(!door){this.notify('Stand beside a door or window to barricade it.');return;}
   if(!this.count('hammer')){this.notify('You need a hammer.');return;}
   if(door.barricaded){door.barricaded--;this.addItem('plank');this.notify('Removed one plank.');}
   else{if(this.count('plank')<1||this.count('nails')<4){this.notify('Requires 1 plank and 4 nails.');return;}this.removeItem('plank');this.removeItem('nails',4);door.barricaded=(door.barricaded||0)+1;door.open=false;door.blocked=true;p.skills.crafting+=8;s.objectives.barricaded++;this.notify('Opening secured with a plank.');}
   s.noise=12;this.tone(115,.1,'square',.03);this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='build'){
   if(!this.count('hammer')||this.count('plank')<2||this.count('nails')<4){this.notify('Building requires a hammer, 2 planks, and 4 nails.');return;}
   const x=Math.floor(p.x)+(Math.abs(p.dirX)>.4?Math.sign(p.dirX):0),y=Math.floor(p.y)+(Math.abs(p.dirY)>.4?Math.sign(p.dirY):0);
   if(this.world.isBlocked(x+.5,y+.5)||s.zombies.some(z=>z.health>0&&Math.hypot(z.x-x-.5,z.y-y-.5)<.9)){this.notify('The space ahead must be clear.');return;}
   const prop=this.world.placeProp({x,y,type:'fence',label:'Wooden barrier',blocked:true,health:100,color:'#786248',built:true});
   if(!prop){this.notify('Cannot build here.');return;}
   this.removeItem('plank',2);this.removeItem('nails',4);p.skills.crafting+=15;s.objectives.barricaded++;s.noise=12;
   this.notify('Wooden barrier built. Press X nearby to dismantle it.');this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='dismantle'){
   if(!this.count('hammer')&&!this.count('axe')){this.notify('You need a hammer or axe.');return;}
   if((p.attackCooldown||0)>0)return;
   const prop=this.world.getNearby(p.x,p.y,1.9).filter(o=>['fence','wall','door','window','debris'].includes(o.type)&&this.canReach(o)).sort((a,b)=>distance(a,p)-distance(b,p))[0];
   if(!prop){this.notify('Stand closer to a wall, opening, or barrier.');return;}
   if(p.stamina<8){this.notify('Too exhausted. Rest before dismantling.');return;}
   const result=this.world.damageProp(prop.id,this.count('axe')?40:25);p.stamina-=8;p.attackCooldown=.6;s.noise=14;
   if(result?.destroyed){this.addItem('plank');p.skills.crafting+=5;this.notify('Structure dismantled. Salvaged one plank.');}
   else this.notify('Damaged structure. Keep dismantling to clear it.');
   this.tone(100,.09,'square',.03);this.save();return;
  }
  if(name==='rest'){
   this.resting=!this.resting;this.notify(this.resting?'Resting. Moving will interrupt.':'Rest interrupted.');return;
  }
 }
 tone(freq,duration,type='sine',volume=.035){
  if(!this.state.settings.sound)return;
  try{const C=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!C)return;this.audio||=new C();if(this.audio.state==='suspended')this.audio.resume();const o=this.audio.createOscillator(),g=this.audio.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.value=freq;g.gain.setValueAtTime(volume,this.audio.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,this.audio.currentTime+duration);o.connect(g);g.connect(this.audio.destination);o.start();o.stop(this.audio.currentTime+duration);}catch{}
 }
}

/** Native ES-module entry. A failed import or initialization stays visible. */
let bootErrorShown = false;

function showBootError(error) {
  console.error('[Deadfall] The game could not continue:', error);
  if (bootErrorShown) return;
  bootErrorShown = true;
  window.game?.engine?.stop?.();

  const overlay = document.createElement('section');
  overlay.id = 'deadfall-boot-error';
  overlay.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
  overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:100000;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:24px;box-sizing:border-box;background:#111711ed;color:#e9e8dc;font:16px/1.6 system-ui,sans-serif';
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  card.style.cssText = 'width:min(100%,540px);padding:30px;box-sizing:border-box;border:1px solid #697155;background:#1c211b;box-shadow:0 16px 70px #0008';
  const title = document.createElement('h1');
  title.textContent = 'DEADFALL';
  title.style.cssText = 'margin:0 0 12px;color:#d6bd83;letter-spacing:.16em;font-size:24px';
  const message = document.createElement('p');
  message.textContent = 'The game encountered a problem. Reload to try again.';
  const details = document.createElement('details');
  const summary = document.createElement('summary');
  summary.textContent = 'Technical details';
  const pre = document.createElement('pre');
  pre.textContent = error instanceof Error ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}` : String(error);
  pre.style.cssText = 'white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:12px;color:#b9bdac';
  details.append(summary, pre);
  const reload = document.createElement('button');
  reload.type = 'button';
  reload.textContent = 'Reload game';
  reload.style.cssText = 'margin-top:24px;padding:10px 20px;border:1px solid #b7a36f;background:#c4b27e;color:#181c17;cursor:pointer;font:600 14px system-ui';
  reload.addEventListener('click', () => window.location.reload());
  card.append(title, message, details, reload);
  overlay.append(card);
  document.body.append(overlay);
  reload.focus();
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  await new Promise(resolve => document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', resolve, { once: true }));
}

// Resource load errors have no event.error; only actual runtime errors halt play.
window.addEventListener('error', event => {
  if (event.error) showBootError(event.error);
});
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', event => showBootError(event.reason));

try {
  // Dynamic import lets failures in any game module reach the recovery panel.
  const { Game } = await import('./game.js');
  const game = new Game();
  await game.init();
  window.game = game;
  game.start();
} catch (error) {
  showBootError(error);
}
