===== BEGIN FILE: perf/README.md =====
# Performance tooling

`PerfMonitor` in `monitor.js` is optional and has no dependencies. Call `frame()`
once per rendered frame and `begin('render')` / `end('render')` around work to
measure it. `snapshot()` provides a bounded sample window and separate section
timings. `mount()` adds a small developer overlay; do not enable it for players
by default. This tool does not change simulation rate or visual quality.

`node build/optimized/build.mjs` verifies relative module and worker references
and creates `build/optimized/site/` containing native modules, precompressed
Gzip/Brotli sidecars and a SHA-256 manifest. It does not rewrite code. A server
must negotiate `Accept-Encoding` and set the correct `Content-Encoding` to use
the sidecars. Serving the original files remains valid. HTML and unchanged
module filenames should revalidate; do not apply immutable caching to them.

Compare browser frame samples using the same viewport, zoom, scene, browser,
and machine. Headless software rendering does not predict real GPU performance.
Node generation timings detect regressions in world generation, not rendering.

The executed browser profile is `profile-results.json`; `../tests/RESULTS.md`
records the hardware, browser matrix, sample sizes and interpretation. The
final software-rendered Chromium cache comparison measured 22–23 ms average
frames with caching and 43 ms without. This is a bounded scene measurement,
not a 60-fps guarantee or a test on a physical GPU.

===== END FILE: perf/README.md =====

/** Node-only diagnostic. No hardware-sensitive timing assertions. */
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { cpus, totalmem } from 'node:os';
import { World } from '../world/generator.js';
import { ZombieSimulation } from '../workers/simulation.js';

const percentile = (times, p) => [...times].sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.ceil(times.length * p) - 1];
const world = new World(74219), updates = [], queries = [];
let maxChunks = 0, visibleProps = 0;
for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
  const x = 7.5 + i * 8, y = 7.5 + i * 4;
  let start = performance.now(); world.update(x, y); updates.push(performance.now() - start);
  start = performance.now(); world.getVisibleTiles(x, y, 24); visibleProps = world.getProps(x, y, 24).length;
  queries.push(performance.now() - start); world.update(x, y); maxChunks = Math.max(maxChunks, world.chunks.size);
}
const grid = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 65, height: 65, cells: new Uint8Array(65 * 65) };
for (let y = 0; y < 50; y++) grid.cells[y * 65 + 32] = 1;
const sim = new ZombieSimulation(), ai = [];
let maxPaths = 0;
for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
  const start = performance.now();
  const result = sim.step({ seq: i, dt: .125, grid: i === 0 ? grid : undefined,
    player: { x: 35.5, y: 30.5 }, noise: 25, time: 720, weather: { type: 'clear' },
    zombies: Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, id) => ({ id, x: 29.5 - id % 5, y: 25.5 + Math.floor(id / 5) * .5, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, health: 100 })) });
  ai.push(performance.now() - start); maxPaths = Math.max(maxPaths, result.paths);
}
console.log(JSON.stringify({
  node: process.version, cpu: cpus()[0]?.model, logicalCPUs: cpus().length, memoryMB: Math.round(totalmem() / 1048576),
  note: 'Node timings exclude browser rendering and are diagnostic, not device-independent budgets.',
  world: { samples: updates.length, updateP50Ms: percentile(updates, .5), updateP95Ms: percentile(updates, .95), queryP95Ms: percentile(queries, .95), maxRetainedChunks: maxChunks, finalVisibleProps: visibleProps },
  ai: { actors: 100, samples: ai.length, p50Ms: percentile(ai, .5), p95Ms: percentile(ai, .95), maxPathsPerStep: maxPaths },
}, null, 2));

===== BEGIN FILE: perf/monitor.js =====
/** Optional bounded frame sampler. No DOM writes occur unless an overlay is mounted. */
export class PerfMonitor {
  constructor({ capacity = 300, budgetMs = 1000 / 60, now = () => performance.now() } = {}) {
    this.capacity = Math.max(2, Math.floor(capacity));
    this.budgetMs = budgetMs;
    this.now = now;
    this.samples = new Float64Array(this.capacity);
    this.count = 0;
    this.cursor = 0;
    this.lastFrame = null;
    this.sections = new Map();
    this.started = new Map();
    this.overlay = null;
    this.overlayAt = 0;
  }

  frame(at = this.now()) {
    if (this.lastFrame !== null) {
      const duration = at - this.lastFrame;
      // Tab suspension should not distort active rendering statistics.
      if (duration > 0 && duration < 1000) this.record(duration);
    }
    this.lastFrame = at;
    if (this.overlay && at - this.overlayAt >= 500) {
      const s = this.snapshot();
      this.overlay.textContent = `${s.fps.toFixed(0)} fps · p95 ${s.p95Ms.toFixed(1)} ms · ${s.overBudgetPercent.toFixed(0)}% over budget`;
      this.overlayAt = at;
    }
  }

  record(duration) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(duration) || duration < 0) return;
    this.samples[this.cursor] = duration;
    this.cursor = (this.cursor + 1) % this.capacity;
    this.count = Math.min(this.count + 1, this.capacity);
  }

  begin(name) { this.started.set(name, this.now()); }

  end(name) {
    if (!this.started.has(name)) return 0;
    const duration = Math.max(0, this.now() - this.started.get(name));
    this.started.delete(name);
    const previous = this.sections.get(name) || { count: 0, totalMs: 0, maxMs: 0 };
    previous.count++;
    previous.totalMs += duration;
    previous.maxMs = Math.max(previous.maxMs, duration);
    this.sections.set(name, previous);
    return duration;
  }

  snapshot() {
    const sorted = Array.from(this.samples.subarray(0, this.count)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const meanMs = this.count ? sorted.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n, 0) / this.count : 0;
    const percentile = (p) => sorted[Math.max(0, Math.ceil(sorted.length * p) - 1)] || 0;
    const sections = Object.fromEntries(Array.from(this.sections, ([name, s]) => [name, {
      count: s.count, meanMs: s.totalMs / s.count, maxMs: s.maxMs,
    }]));
    return {
      samples: this.count, meanMs, fps: meanMs ? 1000 / meanMs : 0,
      p50Ms: percentile(.5), p95Ms: percentile(.95), p99Ms: percentile(.99),
      maxMs: sorted.at(-1) || 0,
      overBudgetPercent: this.count ? sorted.filter(n => n > this.budgetMs).length / this.count * 100 : 0,
      sections,
    };
  }

  mount(parent = document.body) {
    if (this.overlay) return this.overlay;
    const el = document.createElement('output');
    el.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Developer performance statistics');
    el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:4px;right:4px;z-index:9999;padding:5px 8px;background:#10150ee8;color:#d9dfb9;font:11px monospace;pointer-events:none';
    parent.append(el);
    this.overlay = el;
    return el;
  }

  reset() {
    this.samples.fill(0);
    this.count = this.cursor = 0;
    this.lastFrame = null;
    this.sections.clear();
    this.started.clear();
  }

  destroy() { this.overlay?.remove(); this.overlay = null; this.reset(); }
}

===== END FILE: perf/monitor.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: render/README.md =====
# Canvas rendering

`new Renderer(canvas, world)` implements `resize()`, `render(state, alpha)`, `worldToScreen(x,y,z=0)`, `screenToWorld(cssX,cssY)`, `setZoom(value)`, and `destroy()`. The default zoom is 1.18 and the supported range is 0.55–2.1. Projection coordinates are always CSS pixels, independent of device pixel ratio. The renderer measures the canvas's CSS bounds and caps its backing-store density at 2×.

The camera follows the player. Projection uses 64×32 ground diamonds and 32 pixels per metre of height. Integer prop x/y values are tile origins. Rotation 0 follows the x axis; rotation 1 or 90 follows y. East and south `cutaway` walls are low walls. Tile `floorStyle`, `color`, and `rug` plus prop `variant`, `color`, `open`, `barricaded`, `looted`, `lit`, `w`, `h`, and `rotation` affect the artwork. Tall trees fade when they cover the survivor.

- `primitives.js`: deterministic visual hashes, colors, polygons, and shaded isometric geometry.
- `tiles.js`: cached material textures, including worn floorboards, tile/checker floors, concrete, grass, dirt, and cracked asphalt.
- `props.js`: cached, detailed furniture and street props, with refrigerators, sinks, stocked/looted containers, functioning doors and boards, pine/oak trees, and lights.
- `characters.js`: eight supplied survivor facings, 32 cached desaturated/bloodied infected variants derived from the same anatomy, plus original directional fallback figures, equipped weapons, and bodies.
- `renderer.js`: depth sorting, shadows, room rugs and litter, road markings, camera/zoom, light masks, window shafts, warm lamp glows, rain/fog, damage effects, and interaction outlines.

Particle physics remain owned by the combat system. The renderer reads `player.attackTimer/attackDuration/hurtTimer`, zombie `hitTimer/health/deathTimer`, `state.lastAttack`, and particle `x/y/z/life/maxLife/type/size/color`. It does not mutate simulation state. Blood decals and walk phases are transient render caches; persistent actor/world data remains owned by the game.

All texture generation happens in local canvases and all source art ships with the game. Static terrain, road paint, rugs and litter are baked into one opaque viewport-sized ground canvas with a 192px margin on each side. The cache rebuilds beyond 128px of camera movement or on zoom, resize, or world replacement. `renderer.cacheGround = false` enables the equivalent direct path for profiling. Props and actors retain independent depth sorting. Each prop sprite is alpha-cropped once when baked, with one pixel of antialias padding and adjusted projection anchors; environments without pixel reads retain the uncropped sprite. The prop cache is bounded to 420 entries; destroyed renderer caches are released. `renderer.stats` exposes visible source counts and atlas sizes for profiling. The render pass respects reduced-motion settings.

Verification: `node --test render/renderer.test.mjs` checks inverse projection with multiple zooms/cameras and DPR resizing, renders real generated neighborhoods through a validating Canvas API mock across every prop type and combat/weather branch, checks PNG asset signatures and dimensions, verifies the bounded infected variant cache, checks ground cache reuse/invalidation on camera pan, zoom, resize and world replacement, and verifies alpha-crop padding/anchor math and restricted-canvas fallbacks. This is geometry/runtime validation, not a browser visual assertion. The integration QA agent owns browser checks.

Prototype limits: lighting uses composited radial masks and ambient/contact shadows rather than physical ray tracing. Rain/fog and the day/night cycle are presentation effects driven by simulation state. The survivor's supplied art has eight directional idle views with a walking sway; infected views use a hunched transform and uneven sway, with a four-phase limb animation fallback. No copyrighted commercial game assets or external runtime libraries are included.

===== END FILE: render/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: render/characters.js =====
import { surface, hash, ellipse, polygon, line, clamp } from './primitives.js';

const FACES = [6,0,4,1,7,2,5,3]; // clockwise screen E, SE, S, SW, W, NW, N, NE
const SHIRTS = ['#616d5a','#776252','#50686b','#767263'];
const SKINS = ['#9b9e77','#959779','#a0a17e','#8e9678'];
export function facing(dx = 1, dy = 0) {
  const angle = Math.atan2((dx + dy) * .5, dx - dy);
  return ((Math.round(angle / (Math.PI / 4)) + 8) % 8);
}
export class CharacterArt {
  constructor() {
    this.images = [];
    this.cache = new Map();
    this.infected = new Map();
    if (typeof Image !== 'undefined') for(let i=0;i<8;i++) {
      const image=new Image();image.decoding='async';image.src=new URL(`../assets/survivor/face-${i}.png`,import.meta.url).href;
      this.images.push(image);
    }
  }
  draw(ctx, actor, x, y, {player = false, phase = 0, moving = false, elapsed = 0} = {}) {
    if(actor.health<=0) {this.corpse(ctx,actor,x,y,player);return;}
    const dir=facing(actor.dirX,actor.dirY), image=this.images[FACES[dir]], swing=Math.sin(phase);
    const bob=moving?Math.abs(swing)*1.2:Math.sin(elapsed*2.7+(actor.id||0))*.35;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);
    if(player&&image?.complete&&image.naturalWidth>0) {
      ctx.translate(0,-bob);
      // The supplied, original eight-view sprite remains pixel crisp at game scale.
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
      const hurt=actor.hurtTimer>0;
      if(hurt&&Math.sin(elapsed*38)>.1)ctx.globalAlpha=.62;
      ctx.drawImage(image,-18.33,-63.8,36.66,66);
      if(actor.equipped) this.weapon(ctx,actor,dir,phase);
    } else if(!player&&image?.complete&&image.naturalWidth>0) {
      const variant=((actor.id||0)%4+4)%4;
      const key=`${FACES[dir]}:${variant}`;
      let sprite=this.infected.get(key);
      if(!sprite){sprite=this.bakeInfected(image,variant);this.infected.set(key,sprite);}
      // A slight forward shear and uneven step gives the same anatomy a shambling gait.
      const lean=-Math.cos(dir*Math.PI/4)*(.055+(moving?.035:0));
      ctx.transform(1,0,lean,.94,0,-bob*.65);
      ctx.rotate(moving?swing*.018:Math.sin(elapsed*1.2+(actor.id||0))*.008);
      ctx.drawImage(sprite,-18.33,-63.8,36.66,66);
    } else {
      const frame=moving?Math.floor((phase%(Math.PI*2)+Math.PI*2)/(Math.PI/2))%4:0;
      const key=`${player?1:0}:${dir}:${frame}:${(actor.id||0)%4}`;
      let sprite=this.cache.get(key);
      if(!sprite) {sprite=this.bakeHuman(dir,frame,(actor.id||0)%4,player);this.cache.set(key,sprite);}
      ctx.drawImage(sprite,-38,-75-bob);
      if(player)this.weapon(ctx,actor,dir,phase);
    }
    if(!player&&actor.hitTimer>0){ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(221,165,130,.55)';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,-34,13,Math.PI*1.1,Math.PI*1.9);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  bakeInfected(image,variant) {
    const cv=surface(80,144),ctx=cv.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
    ctx.drawImage(image,0,0,80,144);
    const pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,80,144),data=pixels.data;
    const shirts=[[.67,.7,.64],[.6,.68,.7],[.72,.63,.52],[.56,.61,.58]];
    for(let y=0;y<144;y++)for(let x=0;x<80;x++) {
      const i=(y*80+x)*4;if(!data[i+3])continue;
      const r=data[i],g=data[i+1],b=data[i+2],l=r*.29+g*.59+b*.12;
      const tone=y>36&&y<84?shirts[variant]:[.74,.79,.68];
      data[i]=l*tone[0]+r*.14;data[i+1]=l*tone[1]+g*.12;data[i+2]=l*tone[2]+b*.12;
    }
    ctx.putImageData(pixels,0,0);
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-atop';
    // Deterministic stains retain the original silhouette and directional shading.
    for(let i=0;i<16;i++) {
      const x=24+hash(i,variant,516)*31,y=34+hash(i,variant,113)*73;
      ctx.globalAlpha=.18+hash(i,variant,813)*.28;
      polygon(ctx,[[x,y],[x+2,y-2],[x+5,y+1],[x+3,y+7],[x+1,y+4]],'#682f27');
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
    return cv;
  }
  weapon(ctx,actor,dir) {
    const dx=(actor.dirX??1)-(actor.dirY??0),dy=((actor.dirX??1)+(actor.dirY??0))*.5;
    const norm=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1, attack=clamp((actor.attackTimer||0)/(actor.attackDuration||.32),0,1);
    const angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)+(attack?Math.sin(attack*Math.PI)*1.3-.5:0);
    const handX=dir>3?-9:9,handY=-29;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(handX,handY);ctx.rotate(angle);
    if(actor.equipped==='pistol') {
      polygon(ctx,[[0,-1],[3,-4],[12,-4],[12,0],[4,0],[3,4],[0,4]],'#4c5550','#222a27',.6);
      line(ctx,[[4,-4],[11,-4]],'#a4aaa0',.6);
    } else {
      const length=actor.equipped==='axe'?24:23;
      line(ctx,[[0,0],[length,-3]],'#282c23',4.3);
      line(ctx,[[0,-.6],[length,-3.5]],'#ad8c59',2.5);
      if(actor.equipped==='axe')polygon(ctx,[[length-5,-4],[length-3,-11],[length+3,-11],[length+5,-4],[length,-1]],'#a9aaa0','#424a43',.6);
      else line(ctx,[[11,-2.2],[length,-3.4]],'#c0a773',4);
      line(ctx,[[-1,0],[3,-.5]],'#c2a47e',3.8);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  bakeHuman(dir,frame,seed,player) {
    const cv=surface(76,82),ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.translate(38,75);
    const a=dir*Math.PI/4, side=Math.cos(a), forward=Math.sin(a),step=Math.sin(frame*Math.PI/2)*3;
    const shirt=player?'#8b8974':SHIRTS[seed],skin=player?'#bd9f7c':SKINS[seed];
    const limb=(pts,col,w)=>{line(ctx,pts,'#252d26',w+1.4);line(ctx,pts,col,w);};
    ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
    // Weight shifts, staggered knees and reaching arms make infected readable at distance.
    limb([[-4,-24],[-5-step*.4,-12],[-5+step,-2]],'#424e4b',5);
    limb([[4,-24],[5+step*.35,-13],[5-step,-1]],'#4b5550',5);
    line(ctx,[[-7+step,0],[-2+step,0]],'#272e27',4);
    line(ctx,[[3-step,1],[8-step,1]],'#272e27',4);
    const reach=player?0:5;
    limb([[-6,-43],[-10+side*reach,-34],[-9+side*reach*2,-26-forward*3]],shirt,4.5);
    limb([[6,-42],[10+side*reach,-34],[9+side*reach*2,-28-forward*3]],shirt,4.5);
    limb([[-9+side*reach*2,-27-forward*3],[-10+side*reach*2,-23-forward*3]],skin,3.1);
    limb([[9+side*reach*2,-29-forward*3],[10+side*reach*2,-25-forward*3]],skin,3.1);
    polygon(ctx,[[-6,-47],[5,-47],[9,-39],[7,-26],[2,-24],[-7,-26],[-8,-39]],shirt,'#333e30',.8);
    line(ctx,[[-6,-42],[-4,-28]],'rgba(219,221,177,.18)',1.3);
    line(ctx,[[4,-41],[5,-28]],'rgba(10,24,19,.28)',2);
    line(ctx,[[-6,-26],[6,-26]],'#3a3d30',2);
    ctx.fillStyle='#a49d74';ctx.fillRect(-1,-27,2,2);
    if(!player) {
      polygon(ctx,[[-5,-42],[0,-40],[-2,-35],[2,-31],[-1,-27],[-5,-30]],'#754b3d');
      line(ctx,[[5,-38],[2,-34]],'#303e30',1.3);
      line(ctx,[[-5,-14],[-2,-12]],'#8d9276',1.5);
    }
    limb([[0,-48],[0,-45]],skin,4);
    polygon(ctx,[[-4+side,-57],[-3+side,-61],[3+side,-62],[6+side,-57],[5+side,-51],[1+side,-47],[-3+side,-50]],skin,'#424a36',.7);
    polygon(ctx,[[-5+side,-55],[-4+side,-61],[0+side,-64],[5+side,-62],[6+side,-57],[2+side,-59],[-1+side,-56]],seed%2?'#4a4d38':'#5e5940');
    if(forward>-.5) {
      const eyeX=side*2;
      line(ctx,[[eyeX-2,-54],[eyeX,-54]],'#303c2d',1.2);
      line(ctx,[[eyeX+2,-54],[eyeX+3,-54]],'#303c2d',1);
      line(ctx,[[eyeX-1,-50],[eyeX+2,-50]],player?'#7d6a51':'#725848',1.1);
      ctx.fillStyle='#bab892';ctx.fillRect(eyeX,-54,1,1);
    }
    return cv;
  }
  corpse(ctx,actor,x,y,player) {
    const seed=(actor.id||0)%4;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(hash(actor.id||0,2,56)*.8-.4);
    ellipse(ctx,0,1,22,7,'rgba(31,31,20,.38)');
    ellipse(ctx,4,1,18,7,player?'#613b31':'#563930');
    line(ctx,[[-1,0],[-15,2],[-26,0]],'#424d45',5);
    line(ctx,[[-1,2],[-14,6],[-25,6]],'#38463c',5);
    polygon(ctx,[[-8,-4],[8,-4],[13,2],[8,7],[-7,6]],player?'#8b8974':SHIRTS[seed],'#273329',1);
    line(ctx,[[4,-3],[-3,-8],[-8,-7]],SKINS[seed],3);
    ellipse(ctx,15,2,5.3,4.2,player?'#b49873':SKINS[seed]);
    ellipse(ctx,17,1,3,3,'#494d36');ctx.restore();
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: render/primitives.js =====
/** Original, dependency-free isometric drawing primitives. Distances are metres. */
export const TILE_W = 64;
export const TILE_H = 32;
export const HEIGHT = 32;
export const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
export function hash(x, y = 0, seed = 0) {
  let n = Math.imul((x * 8191) | 0, 374761393) ^ Math.imul((y * 8191) | 0, 668265263) ^ (seed | 0);
  n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177);
  return ((n ^ (n >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}
export function shade(hex, amount) {
  const h = hex.replace('#', '');
  const value = parseInt(h.length === 3 ? h.split('').map(c => c + c).join('') : h, 16);
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return hex;
  return `rgb(${clamp((value >> 16) + amount, 0, 255)},${clamp(((value >> 8) & 255) + amount, 0, 255)},${clamp((value & 255) + amount, 0, 255)})`;
}
export function surface(width, height) {
  const canvas = typeof document === 'undefined' ? new OffscreenCanvas(width, height) : document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width; canvas.height = height;
  return canvas;
}
/** Trim empty atlas pixels once, retaining the exact projected anchor and an AA guard. */
export function cropToAlpha(canvas, ox, oy, padding = 1) {
  const original = { canvas, ox, oy };
  const width=canvas.width,height=canvas.height;
  if(!Number.isFinite(width)||!Number.isFinite(height)||width<1||height<1)return original;
  try {
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    if(typeof ctx?.getImageData!=='function')return original;
    const pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,width,height)?.data;
    if(!pixels||pixels.length<width*height*4)return original;
    let left=width,top=height,right=-1,bottom=-1;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++) {
      const row=y*width*4;
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++) {
        if(!pixels[row+x*4+3])continue;
        if(x<left)left=x;if(x>right)right=x;
        if(y<top)top=y;bottom=y;
      }
    }
    if(right<left)return original;
    const guard=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(padding));
    left=Math.max(0,left-guard);top=Math.max(0,top-guard);
    right=Math.min(width,right+guard+1);bottom=Math.min(height,bottom+guard+1);
    if(left===0&&top===0&&right===width&&bottom===height)return original;
    const trimmed=surface(right-left,bottom-top),target=trimmed.getContext('2d');
    if(typeof target?.drawImage!=='function')return original;
    target.drawImage(canvas,left,top,trimmed.width,trimmed.height,0,0,trimmed.width,trimmed.height);
    return {canvas:trimmed,ox:ox-left,oy:oy-top};
  } catch {
    // Drawing remains available in restricted canvas environments and API mocks.
    return original;
  }
}
export function polygon(ctx, pts, fill, stroke = null, width = 1) {
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]);
  ctx.closePath();
  if (fill) { ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); }
  if (stroke) { ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.lineWidth = width; ctx.stroke(); }
}
export function line(ctx, pts, color, width = 1) {
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]);
  ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = width; ctx.stroke();
}
export function ellipse(ctx, x, y, rx, ry, color) {
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(x, y, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fill();
}
/** A small geometry painter; local x/y span one tile, z is height. */
export class IsoPainter {
  constructor(ctx, ox = 0, oy = 0, rotation = 0) { this.ctx = ctx; this.ox = ox; this.oy = oy; this.rotation = rotation; }
  point(x, y, z = 0) {
    if (this.rotation) [x, y] = [y, x];
    return [this.ox + (x - y) * 32, this.oy + (x + y) * 16 - z * HEIGHT];
  }
  face(points, color, stroke = null) { polygon(this.ctx, points.map(p => this.point(...p)), color, stroke, .6); }
  line(points, color, width = 1) { line(this.ctx, points.map(p => this.point(...p)), color, width); }
  box(x, y, z, w, d, h, color, options = {}) {
    const x1 = x + w, y1 = y + d, z1 = z + h;
    const outline = options.outline === false ? null : 'rgba(13,16,13,.24)';
    this.face([[x,y1,z],[x1,y1,z],[x1,y1,z1],[x,y1,z1]], shade(color, this.rotation ? -28 : -14), outline);
    this.face([[x1,y,z],[x1,y1,z],[x1,y1,z1],[x1,y,z1]], shade(color, this.rotation ? -14 : -28), outline);
    this.face([[x,y,z1],[x1,y,z1],[x1,y1,z1],[x,y1,z1]], shade(color, 11), outline);
    if (options.grain) {
      for (let i = 0; i < options.grain; i++) {
        const a = hash(i, w, 91), b = hash(i, d, 23);
        this.line([[x+a*w,y+b*d,z1+.003],[x+Math.min(1,a+.15)*w,y+b*d,z1+.003]], 'rgba(236,204,144,.1)', .55);
      }
    }
  }
  ground(x, y, w, d, color, z = .005) {
    this.face([[x,y,z],[x+w,y,z],[x+w,y+d,z],[x,y+d,z]], color);
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: render/props.js =====
import { IsoPainter, hash, surface, ellipse, polygon, line, shade, cropToAlpha } from './primitives.js';

const WALLS = ['#8c8871','#898273','#8e8670','#797c69'];
const WOOD = '#765c3c';

export class PropAtlas {
  constructor() { this.cache = new Map(); }
  get(prop) {
    const variant = Math.floor(hash(prop.x, prop.y, 56) * 4);
    const key = [prop.type,prop.w||1,prop.h||1,prop.rotation||0,prop.color||'',prop.variant||'',prop.lit===false?0:1,prop.open?1:0,prop.cutaway?1:0,prop.barricaded||0,prop.looted?1:0,variant].join(':');
    let entry = this.cache.get(key);
    if (!entry) {
      entry = this.bake(prop, variant);
      // Bounded cache also accommodates a long, streaming exploration session.
      if (this.cache.size >= 420) this.cache.delete(this.cache.keys().next().value);
      this.cache.set(key, entry);
    }
    return entry;
  }
  draw(ctx, prop, x, y) {
    const item=this.get(prop); ctx.drawImage(item.canvas,x-item.ox,y-item.oy);
  }
  bake(prop, seed) {
    const width=prop.w||1, depth=prop.h||1;
    const cv=surface(Math.ceil((width+depth)*32+128),Math.ceil((width+depth)*16+190));
    const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'), ox=depth*32+64, oy=154;
    const rotated=prop.rotation===1||prop.rotation===90||prop.rotation===270;
    const p=new IsoPainter(ctx,ox,oy,rotated?1:0);
    const w=rotated?depth:width, d=rotated?width:depth;
    const color=prop.color||WOOD;
    const box=(...args)=>p.box(...args);
    const plank=(x,y,z,ww,dd,hh,col=color)=>box(x,y,z,ww,dd,hh,col,{grain:7});
    const leg=(x,y,h=.65,col='#51432e')=>box(x,y,0,.075,.075,h,col);
    const paper=(x,y,z,ww=.28,dd=.2)=>{
      p.ground(x,y,ww,dd,'#c1b897',z);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++) p.line([[x+.04,y+.04+i*.04,z+.003],[x+ww-.04,y+.04+i*.04,z+.003]],'#7d806e',.45);
    };
    const bottle=(x,y,z,col='#5d7464')=>{
      box(x,y,z,.11,.1,.23,col);box(x+.025,y+.025,z+.23,.06,.05,.09,col);box(x+.025,y+.025,z+.3,.06,.05,.025,'#bbb69a');
      box(x-.002,y+.002,z+.06,.114,.105,.07,'#b6ae8d');
    };
    switch(prop.type) {
      case 'wall': {
        const h=prop.cutaway?.24:2.5, col=prop.color||WALLS[seed];
        box(0,.38,0,w,.24,h,col);
        plank(0,.37,0,w,.28,.14,'#464d43');
        plank(0,.37,h-.09,w,.28,.1,'#414b42');
        if(!prop.cutaway) {
          // Two-tone plaster with a chair rail and visible, irregular weathering.
          box(0,.619,.17,w,.018,.6,'#666d5b');
          plank(0,.62,.77,w,.025,.045,'#a29d7e');
          for(let i=0;i<19;i++) {
            const x=hash(seed,i,33)*w,z=.86+hash(seed,i,66)*1.42;
            p.line([[x,.645,z],[x+.035,.645,z+.02+hash(i,seed,789)*.08]],'rgba(33,37,27,.13)',.65);
          }
          if(seed===2) p.line([[.17,.65,2.4],[.22,.65,2.19],[.18,.65,1.95],[.28,.65,1.85]],'rgba(36,39,29,.3)',.8);
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'door': {
        const h=prop.cutaway?1.4:2.25;
        box(.02,.4,0,.09,.22,h,'#454b3e');box(.89,.4,0,.09,.22,h,'#454b3e');
        if(!prop.cutaway) box(.01,.4,h-.1,.98,.22,.13,'#aaa087');
        p.ground(.1,.35,.8,.36,'#82795e');
        if(prop.open) {
          plank(.08,.5,0,.10,.82,h-.1,'#5f7064');
          box(.08,1.2,.8,.14,.035,.05,'#b8ab73');
        } else {
          plank(.12,.43,.02,.76,.11,h-.08,prop.color||'#637466');
          // Recessed upper/lower wood panels and brass door handle.
          box(.22,.54,.18,.55,.014,.48,'#536457');
          if(h>1.5) box(.22,.54,.95,.55,.014,.85,'#536457');
          box(.74,.56,.88,.08,.04,.045,'#c4af70');
        }
        for(let i=0;i<(prop.barricaded||0);i++) {
          const z=.3+i*.36;
          plank(-.02,.63,z,1.05,.07,.13,'#8b704d');
          for(const x of [.1,.86]) box(x,.706,z+.065,.028,.005,.025,'#b8b9a1');
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'window': {
        const h=prop.cutaway?.32:2.5,col=prop.color||WALLS[seed];
        if(prop.cutaway) {box(0,.38,0,1,.25,h,col);plank(-.02,.35,h,1.04,.31,.07,'#50574a');break;}
        box(0,.38,0,1,.24,.88,col);box(0,.38,2.16,1,.24,.34,col);
        box(0,.38,.88,.12,.24,1.3,col);box(.88,.38,.88,.12,.24,1.3,col);
        p.face([[.13,.53,.93],[.87,.53,.93],[.87,.53,2.15],[.13,.53,2.15]],'#5e7470');
        p.face([[.15,.535,.96],[.48,.535,.96],[.48,.535,2.1],[.15,.535,2.1]],'#809089');
        p.face([[.17,.54,1.03],[.25,.54,1.03],[.65,.54,2.1],[.57,.54,2.1]],'rgba(232,222,176,.17)');
        for(const x of [.1,.49,.86]) plank(x,.57,.85,.045,.12,1.34,'#444c40');
        for(const z of [.88,1.52,2.12]) plank(.1,.57,z,.81,.12,.045,'#444c40');
        plank(.02,.36,.83,.97,.42,.08,'#97927c');
        for(let i=0;i<(prop.barricaded||0);i++) {
          const z=.99+i*.31;
          plank(.035,.72,z,.94,.065,.17,'#8e704b');
          for(const x of [.12,.83]) box(x,.79,z+.07,.03,.005,.027,'#b5b8a3');
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'crate': {
        const ww=Math.max(.65,w-.18),dd=Math.max(.6,d-.2),h=.57;
        plank(.09,.1,0,ww,dd,h,prop.color||'#887246');
        for(let i=1;i<4;i++) {
          p.line([[.1,.1+dd,.12*i],[.1+ww,.1+dd,.12*i]],'rgba(31,29,20,.6)',1);
          p.line([[.1+ww,.1,.12*i],[.1+ww,.1+dd,.12*i]],'rgba(31,29,20,.6)',1);
        }
        for(const x of [.15,ww-.04]) plank(x,.08,.01,.07,dd+.04,.6,'#a18a5b');
        if(prop.looted) {p.ground(.22,.22,ww-.26,dd-.23,'#3a3526',.605);plank(.12,.05,.63,ww,.16,.06,'#7d6946');}
        else {
          paper(.29,.18,.578,.31,.25);
          const a=p.point(.42,.1+dd+.01,.29);ctx.save();ctx.translate(...a);ctx.transform(1,.5,0,1,0,0);ctx.font='bold 5px monospace';ctx.fillStyle='#302d23';ctx.fillText('SUPPLY',-10,0);ctx.restore();
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'shelf': {
        const ww=Math.max(.85,w-.12),dd=Math.min(.58,d-.1),hh=1.8;
        if(prop.variant==='fridge') {
          box(.1,.06,.03,.8,.78,1.77,prop.color||'#b8b9a3');
          box(.12,.85,.09,.75,.028,1.14,'#a7af9a');box(.12,.85,1.27,.75,.028,.47,'#b2b8a1');
          box(.72,.889,.77,.035,.055,.31,'#515e52');box(.72,.889,1.36,.035,.055,.23,'#515e52');
          box(.22,.884,1.01,.2,.008,.12,'#c7baa0');
          p.line([[.25,.895,1.07],[.38,.895,1.07]],'#8c7557',.7);
          for(let i=0;i<4;i++)p.line([[.22,.885,.16+i*.035],[.65,.885,.16+i*.035]],'#737f6c',.6);
          if(prop.looted)p.line([[.12,.886,1.26],[.87,.886,1.26]],'#283c31',1.8);
          break;
        }
        box(.06,.08,0,ww,dd,hh,'#3d4a41');
        // Open front, thin uprights, shelves and stocked silhouettes.
        p.face([[.1,.08+dd,.1],[.02+ww,.08+dd,.1],[.02+ww,.08+dd,1.74],[.1,.08+dd,1.74]],'#28352f');
        for(const z of [.04,.59,1.14,1.69]) {
          plank(.05,.06,z,ww+.03,dd+.12,.055,'#71806a');
          if(z<1.5&&!prop.looted) for(let j=0;j<Math.ceil(ww*3);j++) {
            const x=.16+j*.27;
            if(j%3===0) bottle(x,.18,z+.06,['#607965','#8f6347','#65757e'][(j+seed)%3]);
            else if(j%3===1) {box(x,.16,z+.06,.2,.27,.25,'#9a825c');p.line([[x+.06,.445,z+.1],[x+.15,.445,z+.1]],'#4c4938',1.5);}
            else for(let k=0;k<3;k++)box(x+k*.06,.2,z+.06,.045,.2,.28+k*.03,['#776148','#526c65','#8d7959'][k]);
          }
        }
        for(const x of [.05,ww+.02])box(x,.08+dd,0,.045,.05,hh,'#84917a');
        break;
      }
      case 'table': {
        const ww=w-.16,dd=d-.16;
        for(const x of [.1,ww-.01])for(const y of [.1,dd-.01])leg(x,y,.75);
        plank(.06,.06,.73,ww+.08,dd+.08,.095,prop.color||'#7a5b3d');
        paper(.19,.18,.83,.4,.29);
        p.line([[.23,.2,.834],[.34,.25,.834],[.4,.21,.834],[.54,.36,.834]],'#667b69',.9);
        bottle(Math.max(.64,ww-.2),Math.max(.42,dd-.22),.83,'#636c57');
        box(.27,dd-.21,.83,.19,.14,.04,'#827154');
        break;
      }
      case 'chair': {
        for(const x of [.25,.64])for(const y of [.25,.64])leg(x,y,.46);
        plank(.22,.22,.45,.52,.53,.075,'#735d40');
        for(const x of [.23,.67])box(x,.23,.48,.055,.06,.64,'#665238');
        plank(.22,.23,.88,.51,.06,.22,'#816b49');
        break;
      }
      case 'bed': {
        const ww=Math.max(.85,w-.1),dd=Math.max(.85,d-.1),col=prop.color||'#677563';
        for(const x of [.08,ww-.04])for(const y of [.08,dd-.04])leg(x,y,.35,'#49534a');
        box(.05,.05,.21,ww,dd,.2,'#574d3d');box(.08,.08,.4,ww-.06,dd-.06,.2,'#c2bea0');
        box(.09,.37,.6,ww-.08,Math.max(.3,dd-.38),.055,col);
        for(let i=0;i<5;i++)p.line([[.12,.4+i*(dd-.45)/5,.66],[ww,.4+i*(dd-.45)/5,.66]],'rgba(24,36,29,.19)',1);
        box(.17,.12,.62,ww-.24,.28,.105,'#d2c9a9');
        box(.05,.04,.26,ww,.08,.55,'#4c5a4e');
        break;
      }
      case 'sofa': {
        const ww=Math.max(.9,w-.1),dd=d-.1,col=prop.color||'#66664e';
        for(const x of [.14,ww-.14])for(const y of [.14,dd-.1])leg(x,y,.2);
        box(.05,.06,.17,ww,dd,.36,col);box(.05,.06,.5,ww,.2,.54,col);
        for(const x of [.05,ww-.08])box(x,.23,.51,.14,dd-.16,.29,col);
        const cushions=Math.max(1,Math.round(ww));
        for(let i=0;i<cushions;i++)box(.21+i*(ww-.28)/cushions,.29,.54,(ww-.32)/cushions-.035,dd-.29,.105,shade(col,10));
        p.line([[.23,.273,.77],[ww-.12,.273,.77]],'rgba(209,199,150,.2)',.7);
        break;
      }
      case 'counter': {
        const ww=w-.04,dd=d-.04;
        box(.02,.02,.06,ww,dd,.88,prop.color||'#6c7562');
        for(let i=0;i<Math.max(1,Math.round(ww));i++) {
          const cw=ww/Math.max(1,Math.round(ww));
          box(.05+i*cw,dd+.021,.14,cw-.07,.018,.57,'#626c5a');
          box(.12+i*cw,dd+.047,.62,.19,.026,.025,'#c0b69a');
        }
        box(0,0,.94,ww+.04,dd+.04,.075,'#aaa590');
        if(prop.variant==='sink') {p.ground(.15,.16,.48,.38,'#3c514c',1.018);p.ground(.19,.19,.4,.3,'#8b9b90',1.022);box(.36,.07,1.02,.045,.045,.25,'#c5c4af');box(.36,.07,1.23,.045,.24,.035,'#a9b4a5');}
        else {paper(.2,.2,1.02);bottle(.66,.24,1.02);}
        break;
      }
      case 'car': {
        // The long footprint is always the chassis axis, regardless of world rotation.
        const c=new IsoPainter(ctx,ox,oy,width>depth?1:0);
        const ww=Math.max(.9,Math.min(width,depth)-.1),dd=Math.max(1.8,Math.max(width,depth)-.1),col=prop.color||['#785b47','#526d65','#a69a75','#655d54'][seed];
        for(const x of [.03,ww-.17])for(const y of [.25,dd-.55])c.box(x,y,.06,.2,.4,.38,'#222a27');
        c.box(.13,.04,.29,ww-.2,dd,.4,col);c.box(.13,.12,.69,ww-.2,dd-.16,.105,col);
        c.box(.23,dd*.34,.78,ww-.4,dd*.43,.46,col);
        // Both visible glazing planes; inset pillars retain the battered body color.
        c.face([[ww-.166,dd*.37,.83],[ww-.166,dd*.72,.83],[ww-.166,dd*.72,1.17],[ww-.166,dd*.37,1.17]],'#334a48');
        c.face([[.28,dd*.775,.85],[ww-.22,dd*.775,.85],[ww-.25,dd*.775,1.17],[.31,dd*.775,1.17]],'#3e5350');
        c.line([[ww-.163,dd*.56,.83],[ww-.163,dd*.56,1.2]],col,2);
        c.line([[.35,dd*.778,.91],[ww-.37,dd*.778,1.1]],'rgba(170,193,168,.19)',1);
        c.box(.16,.02,.36,ww-.25,.055,.1,'#647269');c.box(.16,dd+.01,.35,ww-.25,.05,.1,'#647269');
        for(const x of [.21,ww-.38]) {c.box(x,.015,.52,.15,.04,.08,'#d4c792');c.box(x,dd+.025,.52,.13,.025,.075,'#8c4e37');}
        c.line([[ww-.12,.27,.66],[ww-.12,dd-.13,.66]],'rgba(230,217,173,.24)',.8);
        c.line([[.33,.16,.806],[ww-.23,dd*.24,.806]],'rgba(80,46,28,.45)',1.5);
        c.box(ww-.145,dd*.6,.69,.035,.13,.028,'#b5b49a');
        break;
      }
      case 'lamp': {
        const floorLamp=/street|pole/i.test(prop.label||''),h=floorLamp?2.6:.74;
        box(.41,.4,0,.2,.2,.075,'#323e34');box(.485,.475,.07,.045,.045,h,'#4b5140');
        box(.36,.35,h,.29,.28,.04,'#373d30');box(.41,.4,h+.04,.18,.18,.29,prop.lit===false?'#697366':'#d8b977');
        box(.35,.34,h+.33,.31,.31,.06,'#414a37');
        for(const x of [.39,.57])box(x,.58,h+.05,.025,.02,.29,'#5b6144');
        if(prop.lit!==false){const pt=p.point(.51,.51,h+.2);ellipse(ctx,pt[0],pt[1],2,4,'#fff1b3');}
        break;
      }
      case 'fence': {
        const h=.97;
        for(let i=0;i<5;i++) {
          const x=i*.21;
          plank(x,.43,0,.125,.10,h+(i%2?.02:0),prop.color||'#77785c');
          p.face([[x,.43,h],[x+.125,.43,h],[x+.065,.43,h+.11]],'#8f8d6c');
        }
        for(const z of [.25,.7])plank(0,.53,z,1,.055,.075,'#5f694e');
        break;
      }
      case 'tree': {
        const center=p.point(.5,.5), trunkHeight=1.75+seed*.18;
        box(.42,.43,0,.16,.16,trunkHeight,'#4e503a');
        p.line([[.5,.5,.45],[.26,.32,1.5]],'#424a34',3);
        p.line([[.5,.5,.85],[.77,.67,2]],'#5b5e3e',3);
        const cx=center[0],cy=center[1]-trunkHeight*32-12;
        if(prop.variant==='pine') {
          for(let layer=0;layer<5;layer++) {
            const z=layer*14,r=34-layer*5;
            const pts=[[cx,cy-z-18],[cx-r*.34,cy-z-4],[cx-r*.26,cy-z-3],[cx-r*.75,cy-z+11],[cx-r*.54,cy-z+9],[cx-r,cy-z+23],[cx-r*.44,cy-z+20],[cx-r*.1,cy-z+26],[cx+r*.6,cy-z+22],[cx+r,cy-z+18],[cx+r*.67,cy-z+6],[cx+r*.38,cy-z-3]];
            polygon(ctx,pts,['#2b4034','#304a3b','#36503c','#3d5940','#496147'][layer]);
            for(let n=0;n<14;n++) {
              const xx=cx+(hash(n,seed,12+layer)-.5)*r*1.5,yy=cy-z+hash(n,seed,51+layer)*18;
              line(ctx,[[xx-3,yy+1],[xx+2,yy]],'rgba(131,149,98,.16)',.8);
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        // Ragged, layered canopy with individual clusters catches the north light.
        for(let layer=0;layer<3;layer++) {
          const points=[],radius=39-layer*5;
          for(let i=0;i<22;i++) {
            const a=i/22*Math.PI*2,r=radius*(.72+hash(i,seed,11+layer)*.3);
            points.push([cx+Math.cos(a)*r+(layer-1)*4,cy+Math.sin(a)*r*.63-layer*9]);
          }
          polygon(ctx,points,['#293d2d','#344b33','#42563a'][layer]);
        }
        for(let n=0;n<135;n++) {
          const angle=hash(n,seed,882)*Math.PI*2, r=Math.sqrt(hash(n,seed,982))*34;
          const xx=cx+Math.cos(angle)*r,yy=cy-10+Math.sin(angle)*r*.69;
          ctx.fillStyle=['#506143','#60704c','#3e5538','#263c2c'][n%4];
          ctx.fillRect(xx,yy,1+hash(n,seed,22)*3,1.2+hash(n,seed,9)*2);
        }
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++)p.line([[.45,.5,.2],[.25+i*.25,.6,.01]],'#596044',1.5);
        break;
      }
      case 'debris': {
        for(let i=0;i<5;i++) {
          const x=.1+hash(i,seed,19)*.63,y=.1+hash(i,seed,131)*.65;
          if(i%2)paper(x,y,.025,.15,.17);else box(x,y,0,.10,.12,.07,['#72735d','#785f3e','#4b5e4e'][i%3]);
        }
        break;
      }
      default: box(.15,.15,0,w-.3,d-.3,.4,'#757359');
    }
    return cropToAlpha(cv,ox,oy,1);
  }
}

===== END FILE: render/props.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: render/renderer.js =====
import { TILE_W, TILE_H, HEIGHT, IsoPainter, clamp, hash, surface, polygon, ellipse, line } from './primitives.js';
import { TileAtlas } from './tiles.js';
import { PropAtlas } from './props.js';
import { CharacterArt } from './characters.js';

/** Canvas2D world renderer. Public projection methods always use CSS pixels. */
export class Renderer {
  constructor(canvas, world) {
    this.canvas=canvas;this.world=world;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});
    if(!this.ctx)throw new Error('Deadfall requires a Canvas 2D compatible browser.');
    this.zoom=1.18;this.camera={x:7.5,y:7.5};this.cameraReady=false;
    this.width=1;this.height=1;this.dpr=1;this.origin={x:0,y:0};
    this.tiles=new TileAtlas();this.props=new PropAtlas();this.characters=new CharacterArt();
    this.actorMotion=new Map();this.groundBlood=new Map();this.lastElapsed=0;this.lastNow=0;
    this.cacheGround=true;this.groundLayer=null;
    this.lightCanvas=surface(1,1);this.lightCtx=this.lightCanvas.getContext('2d');
    this.reduceMotion=typeof matchMedia==='function'&&matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    this._destroyed=false;
    this.resize();
  }
  resize() {
    const rect=this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect?.();
    this.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(rect?.width||globalThis.innerWidth||this.canvas.width||1280));
    this.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(rect?.height||globalThis.innerHeight||this.canvas.height||720));
    this.dpr=Math.min(2,globalThis.devicePixelRatio||1);
    this.canvas.width=Math.round(this.width*this.dpr);this.canvas.height=Math.round(this.height*this.dpr);
    // The half-resolution lighting mask is smooth and cheap even on retina displays.
    this.lightCanvas.width=Math.ceil(this.width*.5);this.lightCanvas.height=Math.ceil(this.height*.5);
    this._setOrigin();
    this._vignette=null;
    this.groundLayer=null;
  }
  setZoom(value) {if(Number.isFinite(value))this.zoom=clamp(value,.55,2.1);this._setOrigin();return this.zoom;}
  _setOrigin() {this.origin.x=this.width*.5;this.origin.y=this.height*.54;}
  worldToScreen(x,y,z=0) {
    return {x:this.origin.x+(x-y-this.camera.x+this.camera.y)*TILE_W*.5*this.zoom,
      y:this.origin.y+((x+y-this.camera.x-this.camera.y)*TILE_H*.5-z*HEIGHT)*this.zoom};
  }
  screenToWorld(x,y) {
    const dx=(x-this.origin.x)/(TILE_W*.5*this.zoom),dy=(y-this.origin.y)/(TILE_H*.5*this.zoom);
    return {x:this.camera.x+(dx+dy)*.5,y:this.camera.y+(dy-dx)*.5};
  }
  _point(x,y,z=0) {return [(x-y)*32,(x+y)*16-z*HEIGHT];}
  _visible(x,y,padding=160) {
    const s=this.worldToScreen(x,y);return s.x>-padding&&s.x<this.width+padding&&s.y>-60&&s.y<this.height+padding;
  }
  render(state,alpha=1) {
    if(this._destroyed||!state?.player)return;
    const ctx=this.ctx,p=state.player,elapsed=Number.isFinite(state.elapsed)?state.elapsed:0;
    const now=typeof performance==='undefined'?Date.now():performance.now();
    const dt=this.lastNow?clamp((now-this.lastNow)/1000,0,.1):1/60;this.lastNow=now;
    if(!this.cameraReady||Math.hypot(p.x-this.camera.x,p.y-this.camera.y)>25) {this.camera.x=p.x;this.camera.y=p.y;this.cameraReady=true;}
    const follow=this.reduceMotion?1:1-Math.exp(-dt*9);
    this.camera.x+=(p.x-this.camera.x)*follow;this.camera.y+=(p.y-this.camera.y)*follow;
    this._setOrigin();
    const radius=clamp(Math.ceil((this.width/64+this.height/32)/this.zoom*.5)+4,20,44);
    const tiles=this.world.getVisibleTiles(p.x,p.y,radius);
    const props=this.world.getProps(p.x,p.y,radius).filter(o=>this._visible(o.x,o.y));
    const night=this._night(state.time??540);
    ctx.setTransform(this.dpr,0,0,this.dpr,0,0);ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
    ctx.fillStyle='#2d382e';ctx.fillRect(0,0,this.width,this.height);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(this.origin.x,this.origin.y);ctx.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
    ctx.translate(-(this.camera.x-this.camera.y)*32,-(this.camera.x+this.camera.y)*16);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
    this._drawGround(ctx,tiles,radius);
    this._propShadows(ctx,props,night);
    this._windowShafts(ctx,props,night);
    this._blood(ctx,state);
    if(state.nearby)this._interaction(ctx,state.nearby,elapsed);
    // Each object's ground contact determines its depth; actors interleave with furniture.
    const objects=props.map(prop=>({kind:'prop',object:prop,depth:prop.x+prop.y+((prop.w||1)+(prop.h||1))*.5}));
    for(const zombie of state.zombies||[])if(this._visible(zombie.x,zombie.y))objects.push({kind:'actor',object:zombie,depth:zombie.x+zombie.y+.03});
    objects.push({kind:'player',object:p,depth:p.x+p.y+.04});
    objects.sort((a,b)=>a.depth-b.depth||(a.kind==='prop'?-1:1));
    const liveIds=new Set();
    for(const entry of objects) {
      const o=entry.object,q=this._point(o.x,o.y);
      if(entry.kind==='prop') {
        const obstructs=o.type==='tree'&&o.x+o.y>p.x+p.y&&Math.abs((o.x-o.y)-(p.x-p.y))<1.25&&o.x+o.y-p.x-p.y<6;
        if(obstructs)ctx.globalAlpha=.38;
        this.props.draw(ctx,o,...q);ctx.globalAlpha=1;
        if(o.type==='wall'&&o.label&&!o.cutaway)this._wallSign(ctx,o);
      } else {
        const isPlayer=entry.kind==='player';
        const key=`${isPlayer?'p':'z'}${o.id}`;liveIds.add(key);
        let motion=this.actorMotion.get(key);
        if(!motion) {motion={x:o.x,y:o.y,phase:0,moving:false};this.actorMotion.set(key,motion);}
        const movement=Math.hypot(o.x-motion.x,o.y-motion.y);
        motion.phase+=Math.min(movement,.3)*7;motion.moving=movement>.001||(isPlayer&&o.moving);
        motion.x=o.x;motion.y=o.y;
        ellipse(ctx,q[0]+3,q[1]+1,isPlayer?12:11,5,'rgba(13,22,17,.33)');
        if(isPlayer&&o.health>0)this._playerMarker(ctx,o,q,state);
        this.characters.draw(ctx,o,...q,{player:isPlayer,phase:this.reduceMotion?0:motion.phase,moving:motion.moving&&!state.paused,elapsed:this.reduceMotion?0:elapsed});
        if(!isPlayer&&o.health>0&&o.health<75&&(o.hitTimer>0||Math.hypot(o.x-p.x,o.y-p.y)<3.5)) {
          ctx.fillStyle='rgba(20,27,21,.8)';ctx.fillRect(q[0]-10,q[1]-70,20,2);
          ctx.fillStyle='#ad805a';ctx.fillRect(q[0]-10,q[1]-70,20*clamp(o.health/80,0,1),2);
        }
      }
    }
    for(const key of this.actorMotion.keys())if(!liveIds.has(key))this.actorMotion.delete(key);
    this._attack(ctx,state);
    this._particles(ctx,state.particles||[]);
    ctx.restore();
    this._lighting(state,props,night,elapsed);
    this._weather(state,elapsed,night);
    this._vignettePass(state);
    this.lastElapsed=elapsed;
    this.stats={tiles:tiles.length,props:props.length,actors:liveIds.size,cachedTiles:this.tiles.cache.size,cachedProps:this.props.cache.size};
  }
  _night(minutes) {
    const hour=((minutes%1440)+1440)%1440/60;
    if(hour>=8&&hour<=17)return 0;
    if(hour<5||hour>21)return 1;
    return hour<8?clamp((8-hour)/3,0,1):clamp((hour-17)/4,0,1);
  }
  _drawGround(ctx,tiles,radius) {
    if(!this.cacheGround) {
      for(const tile of tiles) {
        if(!this._visible(tile.x+.5,tile.y+.5,70))continue;
        this.tiles.draw(ctx,tile,...this._point(tile.x,tile.y));
        if(tile.type==='road')this._roadMarkings(ctx,tile);
      }
      this._floorDetails(ctx,tiles);return;
    }
    const margin=192;
    let ground=this.groundLayer;
    const offset=ground?{x:((ground.x-ground.y)-(this.camera.x-this.camera.y))*32*this.zoom,y:((ground.x+ground.y)-(this.camera.x+this.camera.y))*16*this.zoom}:{x:0,y:0};
    if(!ground||ground.zoom!==this.zoom||ground.world!==this.world||Math.abs(offset.x)>margin-64||Math.abs(offset.y)>margin-64) {
      const canvas=ground?.canvas||surface(1,1);
      canvas.width=this.width+margin*2;canvas.height=this.height+margin*2;
      const gc=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});
      gc.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);gc.fillStyle='#2d382e';gc.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      gc.setTransform(this.zoom,0,0,this.zoom,margin+this.origin.x-(this.camera.x-this.camera.y)*32*this.zoom,margin+this.origin.y-(this.camera.x+this.camera.y)*16*this.zoom);
      const expanded=this.world.getVisibleTiles(this.camera.x,this.camera.y,radius+Math.ceil(margin/(16*this.zoom)));
      const keep=[];
      for(const tile of expanded) {
        const q=this.worldToScreen(tile.x+.5,tile.y+.5);
        if(q.x< -margin-70||q.x>this.width+margin+70||q.y< -margin-40||q.y>this.height+margin+40)continue;
        keep.push(tile);this.tiles.draw(gc,tile,...this._point(tile.x,tile.y));
        if(tile.type==='road')this._roadMarkings(gc,tile);
      }
      this._floorDetails(gc,keep,margin+50);
      ground=this.groundLayer={canvas,x:this.camera.x,y:this.camera.y,zoom:this.zoom,world:this.world};
      offset.x=0;offset.y=0;
    }
    ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(this.dpr,0,0,this.dpr,0,0);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    ctx.drawImage(ground.canvas,Math.round(offset.x)-margin,Math.round(offset.y)-margin);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  _roadMarkings(ctx,tile) {
    const mx=((tile.x%32)+32)%32,my=((tile.y%32)+32)%32;
    const p=new IsoPainter(ctx,...this._point(tile.x,tile.y));
    if(mx===21&&!(my>=19&&my<=24)&&((tile.y%4+4)%4)<2)p.ground(.8,.02,.055,.96,'#a39862',.003);
    if(my===21&&!(mx>=19&&mx<=24)&&((tile.x%4+4)%4)<2)p.ground(.02,.8,.96,.055,'#a39862',.003);
    if(mx===19)p.ground(.14,0,.025,1,'rgba(181,183,157,.24)',.005);
    if(my===19)p.ground(0,.14,1,.025,'rgba(181,183,157,.24)',.005);
  }
  _floorDetails(ctx,tiles,padding=50) {
    for(const tile of tiles) {
      const screen=this.worldToScreen(tile.x,tile.y);
      if(tile.type!=='floor'||screen.x< -padding||screen.x>this.width+padding||screen.y< -padding||screen.y>this.height+padding)continue;
      const q=this._point(tile.x,tile.y),p=new IsoPainter(ctx,...q),n=hash(tile.x,tile.y,648);
      if(tile.rug) {
        p.ground(0,0,1,1,tile.rug,.01);
        for(let i=0;i<6;i++)p.line([[0,i/6,.012],[1,i/6,.012]],'rgba(204,176,130,.10)',.65);
        p.face([[.5,.18,.013],[.82,.5,.013],[.5,.82,.013],[.18,.5,.013]],'rgba(193,161,111,.13)');
        for(const [dx,dy,points] of [[-1,0,[[.08,0,.014],[.08,1,.014]]],[1,0,[[.92,0,.014],[.92,1,.014]]],[0,-1,[[0,.08,.014],[1,.08,.014]]],[0,1,[[0,.92,.014],[1,.92,.014]]]]) {
          if(this.world.getTile(tile.x+dx,tile.y+dy).rug!==tile.rug)p.line(points,'rgba(209,184,135,.35)',1.4);
        }
      }
      if(n>.968) {
        p.ground(.25,.31,.28,.31,'#a4a18a',.006);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++)p.line([[.29,.36+i*.05,.008],[.49,.36+i*.05,.008]],'#6d7262',.6);
      } else if(n<.018) {
        for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const a=p.point(.2+hash(i,tile.x,772)*.5,.2+hash(i,tile.y,185)*.5);ellipse(ctx,a[0],a[1],2+i*.6,1+i*.3,'rgba(23,29,21,.22)');}
      }
    }
  }
  _propShadows(ctx,props,night) {
    for(const o of props) {
      const p=new IsoPainter(ctx,...this._point(o.x,o.y));
      const w=o.w||1,d=o.h||1;
      if(o.type==='wall'||o.type==='window') {
        const alongY=o.rotation===1||o.rotation===90;
        if(!o.cutaway) {
          if(alongY)p.ground(.45,.03,.9,d,'rgba(15,25,20,.14)');
          else p.ground(.03,.46,w,.9,'rgba(15,25,20,.14)');
        }
      } else if(o.type==='tree') {
        const q=p.point(.7,.65);ellipse(ctx,q[0]+18,q[1]+8,37,16,'rgba(16,28,19,.23)');
      } else if(o.type!=='debris'&&o.type!=='door') {
        const q=p.point(w*.53,d*.6);ellipse(ctx,q[0]+6,q[1]+2,(w+d)*14,(w+d)*6,'rgba(14,22,17,.23)');
      }
    }
  }
  _windowShafts(ctx,props,night) {
    for(const o of props) {
      if(o.type!=='window'||o.cutaway)continue;
      const p=new IsoPainter(ctx,...this._point(o.x,o.y),o.rotation===1||o.rotation===90?1:0);
      const intensity=.055+night*.015;
      const start=.72,end=3.9;
      p.face([[.1,start,.013],[.88,start,.013],[1.35,end,.013],[.22,end,.013]],`rgba(219,169,85,${intensity})`);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++)p.ground(.15+i*.25,.8,.10,2.5,`rgba(245,197,112,${intensity*.7})`,.015);
    }
  }
  _wallSign(ctx,prop) {
    const label=String(prop.label).replace(/\s+wall$/i,'').toUpperCase();
    if(label.length<3)return;
    const q=this._point(prop.x+.18,prop.y+.64,1.65);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(...q);
    const rotated=prop.rotation===1||prop.rotation===90;
    ctx.transform(rotated?-1:1,.5,0,1,0,0);
    if(rotated)ctx.scale(-1,1);
    ctx.font='600 6px monospace';const width=Math.min(92,ctx.measureText(label).width+8);
    ctx.fillStyle='#3c493c';ctx.fillRect(-3,-7,width,11);ctx.strokeStyle='#9c9a78';ctx.lineWidth=.45;ctx.strokeRect(-3,-7,width,11);
    ctx.fillStyle='#d5c69b';ctx.fillText(label,1,1,width-6);ctx.restore();
  }
  _interaction(ctx,prop,elapsed) {
    const p=new IsoPainter(ctx,...this._point(prop.x,prop.y));
    const w=prop.w||1,d=prop.h||1,alpha=this.reduceMotion?.65:.45+Math.sin(elapsed*3)*.12;
    p.face([[.03,.03,.025],[w-.03,.03,.025],[w-.03,d-.03,.025],[.03,d-.03,.025]],`rgba(215,179,95,${alpha*.13})`);
    const length=.19,color=`rgba(220,192,123,${alpha})`;
    for(const [x,y,sx,sy] of [[0,0,1,1],[w,0,-1,1],[w,d,-1,-1],[0,d,1,-1]])p.line([[x+sx*length,y,.025],[x,y,.025],[x,y+sy*length,.025]],color,1.2);
  }
  _playerMarker(ctx,p,q,state) {
    ctx.strokeStyle=state.sneaking?'rgba(154,180,153,.5)':'rgba(219,199,141,.4)';ctx.lineWidth=.8;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(q[0],q[1],13,6,0,.1,Math.PI*.9);ctx.stroke();
    const dx=(p.dirX??1)-(p.dirY??0),dy=((p.dirX??1)+(p.dirY??0))*.5,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
    const ux=dx/len,uy=dy/len;
    polygon(ctx,[[q[0]+ux*18,q[1]+uy*9],[q[0]+ux*13-uy*2,q[1]+uy*6+ux*2],[q[0]+ux*13+uy*2,q[1]+uy*6-ux*2]],'rgba(228,207,143,.7)');
  }
  _blood(ctx,state) {
    for(const z of state.zombies||[]) {
      if(z.health>0)continue;
      if(!this.groundBlood.has(z.id))this.groundBlood.set(z.id,{x:z.x,y:z.y,at:state.elapsed||0});
    }
    if(this.groundBlood.size>240)this.groundBlood.delete(this.groundBlood.keys().next().value);
    for(const [id,b] of this.groundBlood) {
      if(!this._visible(b.x,b.y))continue;
      const q=this._point(b.x,b.y);
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++) {const x=q[0]+(hash(id,i,32)-.5)*30,y=q[1]+(hash(id,i,81)-.5)*12;ellipse(ctx,x,y,3+hash(i,id,56)*5,1.5+hash(i,id,98)*2,'rgba(83,36,27,.65)');}
    }
  }
  _attack(ctx,state) {
    const a=state.lastAttack;if(!a||a.ttl<=0)return;
    const q=this._point(a.x,a.y,.9),ratio=clamp(a.ttl/(a.duration||.3),0,1);
    const dx=(a.dirX??1)-(a.dirY??0),dy=((a.dirX??1)+(a.dirY??0))*.5,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(...q);ctx.rotate(angle);
    if(a.weapon==='pistol') {
      const range=Math.min(280,(a.range||8)*26);
      line(ctx,[[12,0],[range,0]],`rgba(233,210,140,${ratio*.5})`,1);
    } else {
      ctx.scale(1,.62);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,(a.range||1.5)*24,-.95,.95);
      ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(219,220,190,${ratio*.32})`;ctx.lineWidth=2.4;ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  _particles(ctx,particles) {
    for(const part of particles) {
      if(!Number.isFinite(part.x)||!Number.isFinite(part.y)||part.life<=0)continue;
      const q=this._point(part.x,part.y,part.z||0),life=clamp(part.life/(part.maxLife||1),0,1);
      ctx.globalAlpha=life;ctx.fillStyle=part.color||(part.type==='blood'?'#853f30':'#d9c491');
      const size=clamp(part.size||2,.7,7);
      if(part.type==='muzzle') {ellipse(ctx,q[0],q[1],size*2,size,'#ffdea0');}
      else if(part.type==='dust')ellipse(ctx,q[0],q[1],size*2,size,'rgba(163,163,131,.4)');
      else ctx.fillRect(q[0]-size*.5,q[1]-size*.5,size,size);
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  _lighting(state,props,night,elapsed) {
    const ctx=this.ctx,lctx=this.lightCtx,w=this.lightCanvas.width,h=this.lightCanvas.height;
    const cloud=state.weather?.type==='overcast'?.045:state.weather?.type==='rain'?.075:0;
    lctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);lctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    lctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';lctx.fillStyle=`rgba(12,24,30,${.055+cloud+night*.65})`;lctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
    const lamps=props.filter(o=>o.type==='lamp'&&o.lit!==false).slice(0,28);
    const sources=lamps.map(o=>({x:o.x+.5,y:o.y+.5,z:.85,r:132}));
    // A survivor's close visibility keeps navigation readable through the darkest hours.
    sources.push({x:state.player.x,y:state.player.y,z:.4,r:110,player:true});
    lctx.globalCompositeOperation='destination-out';
    for(const source of sources) {
      const q=this.worldToScreen(source.x,source.y,source.z),r=source.r*this.zoom*.5;
      const gradient=lctx.createRadialGradient(q.x*.5,q.y*.5,0,q.x*.5,q.y*.5,r);
      gradient.addColorStop(0,`rgba(0,0,0,${source.player?.4:.92})`);gradient.addColorStop(.4,`rgba(0,0,0,${source.player?.2:.65})`);gradient.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      lctx.fillStyle=gradient;lctx.fillRect(q.x*.5-r,q.y*.5-r,r*2,r*2);
    }
    lctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
    ctx.drawImage(this.lightCanvas,0,0,this.width,this.height);
    ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';
    for(const source of sources) {
      if(source.player)continue;
      const q=this.worldToScreen(source.x,source.y,source.z),r=source.r*this.zoom;
      const flicker=this.reduceMotion?1:1+.04*Math.sin(elapsed*7.8+source.x)+.025*Math.sin(elapsed*19);
      const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(q.x,q.y,1,q.x,q.y,r);
      g.addColorStop(0,`rgba(247,174,72,${(.19+night*.1)*flicker})`);g.addColorStop(.35,'rgba(224,139,49,.055)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(198,119,44,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(q.x-r,q.y-r,r*2,r*2);
      const core=ctx.createRadialGradient(q.x,q.y,0,q.x,q.y,12*this.zoom);
      core.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,220,128,.3)');core.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,201,101,0)');ctx.fillStyle=core;ctx.fillRect(q.x-15*this.zoom,q.y-15*this.zoom,30*this.zoom,30*this.zoom);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  _weather(state,elapsed,night) {
    const ctx=this.ctx,weather=state.weather||{},type=weather.type||'overcast';
    if(type==='rain'||type==='storm') {
      const intensity=clamp(weather.intensity??.5,.1,1),count=Math.floor(60+intensity*135),t=this.reduceMotion?0:elapsed;
      ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(168,188,182,${.13+intensity*.08})`;ctx.lineWidth=.65;
      ctx.beginPath();
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++) {
        const x=(hash(i,61,372)*this.width-t*65*(.7+hash(i,43,611))%(this.width+80)+this.width+80)%(this.width+80)-40;
        const y=(hash(i,51,922)*this.height+t*(340+hash(i,13,881)*180))%(this.height+80)-40;
        const ground=this.screenToWorld(x,y+14);
        if(this.world.getTile(ground.x,ground.y).type==='floor')continue;
        ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x-4,y+12+intensity*8);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      // Ground splashes are sparse and do not cross the indoor floor.
      for(let i=0;i<18;i++) {
        const x=hash(i,16,18)*this.width,y=hash(i,31,77)*this.height;
        const world=this.screenToWorld(x,y);if(this.world.getTile(world.x,world.y).type==='floor')continue;
        const life=(elapsed*1.7+hash(i,0,39))%1;
        ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(178,195,180,${(1-life)*.18})`;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,life*6+1,life*2+.5,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    if(type==='fog'||type==='mist') {
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,this.width,this.height);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(156,177,165,.14)');g.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(153,173,162,.05)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(171,179,154,.17)');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,this.width,this.height);
    }
    // Windborne dust catches the lanterns, with deterministic positions and no state writes.
    if(!this.reduceMotion)for(let i=0;i<18;i++) {
      const x=(hash(i,9,11)*this.width+elapsed*(2+i%3))%this.width,y=(hash(i,71,19)*this.height+Math.sin(elapsed*.23+i)*8);
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(218,216,174,${.06+hash(i,0,12)*.08})`;ctx.fillRect(x,y,1,1);
    }
  }
  _vignettePass(state) {
    const ctx=this.ctx;
    if(!this._vignette) {
      this._vignette=ctx.createRadialGradient(this.width*.5,this.height*.51,this.height*.14,this.width*.5,this.height*.51,Math.max(this.width,this.height)*.66);
      this._vignette.addColorStop(0,'rgba(4,12,10,0)');this._vignette.addColorStop(.55,'rgba(4,12,10,.025)');this._vignette.addColorStop(1,'rgba(4,12,10,.4)');
    }
    ctx.fillStyle=this._vignette;ctx.fillRect(0,0,this.width,this.height);
    if((state.player.hurtTimer||0)>0||state.player.health<25) {
      const hurt=clamp((state.player.hurtTimer||0)*.5+(state.player.health<25?.16:0),0,.4);
      const gradient=ctx.createRadialGradient(this.width*.5,this.height*.5,this.height*.26,this.width*.5,this.height*.5,this.width*.62);
      gradient.addColorStop(0,'rgba(107,23,12,0)');gradient.addColorStop(1,`rgba(122,32,18,${hurt})`);ctx.fillStyle=gradient;ctx.fillRect(0,0,this.width,this.height);
    }
  }
  destroy() {this._destroyed=true;this.groundLayer=null;this.actorMotion.clear();this.groundBlood.clear();this.tiles.cache.clear();this.props.cache.clear();this.characters.cache.clear();this.characters.infected.clear();}
}

===== END FILE: render/renderer.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: render/renderer.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { World } from '../world/generator.js';

let calls=0;
const numericMethods=new Set(['moveTo','lineTo','ellipse','arc','rect','fillRect','clearRect','strokeRect','translate','rotate','scale','transform','setTransform']);
function context() {
  return new Proxy({}, {
    get(target,key) {
      if(key==='measureText')return text=>({width:String(text).length*4});
      if(key==='getImageData')return (x,y,width,height)=>({data:new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4).fill(150),width,height});
      if(key==='createRadialGradient'||key==='createLinearGradient')return (...values)=>{
        assert.ok(values.every(Number.isFinite),`${key} must have finite coordinates`);return {addColorStop(){}};
      };
      if(key==='drawImage')return (image,...values)=>{assert.ok(image);assert.ok(values.every(Number.isFinite));calls++;};
      if(key in target)return target[key];
      return (...values)=>{if(numericMethods.has(key))assert.ok(values.every(value=>typeof value==='boolean'||Number.isFinite(value)),`${String(key)} has invalid coordinates ${values}`);calls++;};
    },
    set(target,key,value) {target[key]=value;return true;}
  });
}
class Canvas {
  constructor(width=1280,height=800){this.width=width;this.height=height;this.cssWidth=width;this.cssHeight=height;this.context=context();}
  getContext(){return this.context;}
  getBoundingClientRect(){return {width:this.cssWidth,height:this.cssHeight};}
}
globalThis.OffscreenCanvas=Canvas;
const {Renderer}=await import('./renderer.js');
const {cropToAlpha}=await import('./primitives.js');
const world=new World(74219);world.update(7.5,7.5);
const state={time:540,elapsed:0,weather:{type:'overcast',intensity:.25},player:{id:1,x:7.5,y:7.5,dirX:1,dirY:0,health:100,equipped:'bat'},zombies:[],particles:[]};

test('CSS projection round trips after camera movement, resize, DPR and zoom',()=>{
  const canvas=new Canvas(),renderer=new Renderer(canvas,world);
  for(const zoom of [.55,1,1.18,2.1])for(const camera of [[7.5,7.5],[-19.2,31.8],[420,-912]]) {
    renderer.camera={x:camera[0],y:camera[1]};renderer.setZoom(zoom);
    for(const [x,y] of [[0,0],[7.5,9.3],[-72.3,8.1],[420,-912]]) {
      const screen=renderer.worldToScreen(x,y),roundtrip=renderer.screenToWorld(screen.x,screen.y);
      assert.ok(Math.abs(roundtrip.x-x)<1e-9);assert.ok(Math.abs(roundtrip.y-y)<1e-9);
    }
  }
  canvas.cssWidth=860;canvas.cssHeight=620;globalThis.devicePixelRatio=2;renderer.resize();
  assert.equal(canvas.width,1720);assert.equal(renderer.width,860);
  const center=renderer.worldToScreen(renderer.camera.x,renderer.camera.y);assert.equal(center.x,430);assert.equal(center.y,334.8);
  renderer.setZoom(8);assert.equal(renderer.zoom,2.1);renderer.setZoom(.01);assert.equal(renderer.zoom,.55);
  delete globalThis.devicePixelRatio;
});

test('real generated worlds render all prop types, combat, night, rain and fog with finite geometry',()=>{
  const renderer=new Renderer(new Canvas(),world);
  const props=['wall','door','window','tree','crate','shelf','bed','table','chair','sofa','counter','car','lamp','debris','fence'];
  for(const type of props)for(const rotation of [0,1])for(const cutaway of [false,true]) {
    const o={id:type,x:4,y:4,type,rotation,cutaway,w:2,h:1,barricaded:3,open:rotation===1,looted:cutaway};
    renderer.props.get(o);
  }
  for(const [x,y] of [[7.5,7.5],[39,8],[-25,8],[7,-24],[42,42]]) {
    world.update(x,y);state.player.x=x;state.player.y=y;
    state.zombies=[{id:30,x:x+2,y:y+2,health:45,dirX:-1,dirY:0,hitTimer:.3},{id:32,x:x-2,y:y+2,health:0,deathTimer:5}];
    state.nearby=world.getNearby(x,y,3).find(o=>o.type==='crate');
    for(const type of ['overcast','rain','fog']) {
      state.weather={type,intensity:.9};state.time=type==='rain'?1380:540;state.elapsed+=.02;
      state.lastAttack={x,y,dirX:1,dirY:0,weapon:type==='fog'?'pistol':'axe',ttl:.2,duration:.3,range:1.5};
      state.particles=['blood','spark','muzzle','dust'].map((type,index)=>({type,x:x+.1*index,y,z:.5,life:.4,maxLife:1,size:2}));
      renderer.render(state,.5);
    }
  }
  assert.ok(calls>10000);assert.ok(renderer.props.cache.size<=420);assert.ok(renderer.stats.tiles>0);
  assert.ok(renderer.groundBlood.size>0);renderer.destroy();assert.equal(renderer.props.cache.size,0);
});

test('eight supplied survivor assets have valid PNG signatures',()=>{
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++) {
    const bytes=fs.readFileSync(new URL(`../assets/survivor/face-${i}.png`,import.meta.url));
    assert.equal(bytes.subarray(0,8).toString('hex'),'89504e470d0a1a0a');assert.equal(bytes.readUInt32BE(16),80);assert.equal(bytes.readUInt32BE(20),144);
  }
});

test('infected reuse loaded directional sprites through a bounded variant cache',()=>{
  const renderer=new Renderer(new Canvas(),world),art=renderer.characters;
  art.images=Array.from({length:8},()=>({complete:true,naturalWidth:80,naturalHeight:144}));
  for(let id=0;id<4;id++)for(let i=0;i<8;i++) {
    const angle=i*Math.PI/4,dx=Math.cos(angle)+Math.sin(angle)*2,dy=Math.sin(angle)*2-Math.cos(angle);
    art.draw(renderer.ctx,{id,x:0,y:0,health:80,dirX:dx,dirY:dy},100,100,{moving:true,phase:1.3,elapsed:4});
  }
  assert.equal(art.infected.size,32);
  art.draw(renderer.ctx,{id:0,health:80,dirX:1,dirY:0},100,100,{moving:true,phase:2,elapsed:4});
  assert.equal(art.infected.size,32);renderer.destroy();assert.equal(art.infected.size,0);
});

test('ground cache reuses static paint while panning and rebuilds on viewport, zoom and world changes',()=>{
  const canvas=new Canvas(),localWorld=new World(74219),renderer=new Renderer(canvas,localWorld);
  renderer.reduceMotion=true;
  const local={...state,player:{...state.player,x:7.5,y:7.5},zombies:[],particles:[],lastAttack:null};
  let draws=0;
  const draw=renderer.tiles.draw.bind(renderer.tiles);
  renderer.tiles.draw=(...args)=>{draws++;return draw(...args);};
  localWorld.update(7.5,7.5);renderer.render(local);assert.ok(draws>500);
  const first=renderer.groundLayer;
  draws=0;renderer.render(local);assert.equal(draws,0);assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer,first);
  local.player.x+=1;renderer.render(local);assert.equal(draws,0);assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer,first);
  local.player.x+=15;localWorld.update(local.player.x,local.player.y);renderer.render(local);assert.ok(draws>500);assert.notEqual(renderer.groundLayer,first);
  draws=0;renderer.setZoom(1.5);renderer.render(local);assert.ok(draws>500);assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer.zoom,1.5);
  canvas.cssWidth=900;renderer.resize();assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer,null);renderer.render(local);assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer.canvas.width,1284);
  const replacement=new World(123);renderer.world=replacement;renderer.render(local);assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer.world,replacement);
  renderer.cacheGround=false;draws=0;renderer.render(local);assert.ok(draws>100);renderer.destroy();assert.equal(renderer.groundLayer,null);
});

test('alpha crop preserves world anchors, retains antialias padding, and tolerates limited canvas APIs',()=>{
  const width=20,height=30,data=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
  for(const [x,y,alpha] of [[7,11,1],[12,18,255],[9,13,200]])data[(y*width+x)*4+3]=alpha;
  const canvas={width,height,getContext:()=>({getImageData:()=>({data})})};
  const crop=cropToAlpha(canvas,10,25);
  assert.equal(crop.canvas.width,8);assert.equal(crop.canvas.height,10);
  assert.equal(crop.ox,4);assert.equal(crop.oy,15);
  // Old pixel (9,13) becomes (3,3): both land on precisely the same screen point.
  assert.equal(500-crop.ox+3,500-10+9);assert.equal(600-crop.oy+3,600-25+13);
  const edgeData=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);edgeData[3]=1;
  const edge=cropToAlpha({width,height,getContext:()=>({getImageData:()=>({data:edgeData})})},10,25);
  assert.equal(edge.canvas.width,2);assert.equal(edge.canvas.height,2);assert.equal(edge.ox,10);assert.equal(edge.oy,25);
  for(const getContext of [()=>({}),()=>({getImageData:()=>({})}),()=>({getImageData(){throw new Error('No pixel reads');}}),()=>({getImageData:()=>({data:new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4)})})]) {
    const source={width,height,getContext};const fallback=cropToAlpha(source,10,25);
    assert.equal(fallback.canvas,source);assert.equal(fallback.ox,10);assert.equal(fallback.oy,25);
  }
});

===== BEGIN FILE: render/tiles.js =====
import { IsoPainter, hash, surface, polygon, shade } from './primitives.js';

const GRASS = ['#454d35','#424a33','#485038','#3f4832','#48503a','#424b36'];
const ASPHALT = ['#3f4542','#424744','#3e4441','#414642'];
const TIMBER = ['#66513d','#6c5540','#614c39','#715740','#63503b','#6b513b'];
const STONE = ['#77796c','#73766b','#7d7d70','#74776b'];

/** Tile sprites are generated once per material/variation. No frame-time texture noise. */
export class TileAtlas {
  constructor() { this.cache = new Map(); }
  get(tile) {
    const variant = Math.floor(hash(tile.x, tile.y, 127) * 12);
    const style = tile.floorStyle || (typeof tile.room === 'string' && /clinic|market|garage|diner/i.test(tile.room) ? 'tile' : 'wood');
    const key = `${tile.type}:${style}:${tile.color||''}:${variant}`;
    if (!this.cache.has(key)) this.cache.set(key, this.bake(tile.type, style, variant, tile.color));
    return this.cache.get(key);
  }
  bake(type, style, seed, tint) {
    const cv = surface(68, 36), ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
    const p = new IsoPainter(ctx, 34, 2);
    let color = type === 'road' ? ASPHALT[seed % 4] : type === 'floor' ? TIMBER[seed % 6] : type === 'sidewalk' ? STONE[seed % 4] : type === 'dirt' ? '#625b43' : GRASS[seed % 6];
    if (type === 'floor' && /tile|check|linoleum|concrete/i.test(style)) color = tint||'#69716a';
    // A subpixel bleed removes dark diamond seams when the atlas is zoomed.
    p.ground(-.018,-.018,1.036,1.036,color);
    ctx.save(); polygon(ctx, [[34,2],[66,18],[34,34],[2,18]], null); ctx.clip();
    if (type === 'floor') {
      if (/concrete/i.test(style)) {
        p.ground(.01,.01,.98,.98,shade(tint||'#777d72',seed-10));
        if(seed%3===1)p.line([[.01,.16,0],[.27,.44,0],[.35,.46,0],[.64,.9,0]],'rgba(32,43,34,.34)',.7);
        p.line([[0,0,0],[0,1,0],[1,1,0]],'rgba(37,47,38,.15)',.6);
      } else if (/tile|check|linoleum/i.test(style)) {
        for(let y=0;y<2;y++) for(let x=0;x<2;x++) {
          const dark=/check/i.test(style)?-45:-15;
          p.ground(x*.5+.012,y*.5+.012,.48,.48,shade(tint||'#858d7a',(x+y)%2?dark:4));
          p.line([[x*.5,y*.5,0],[x*.5+.5,y*.5,0]],'rgba(222,221,179,.12)',.6);
        }
      } else {
        for (let y=0;y<5;y++) {
          const tone = TIMBER[Math.floor(hash(seed,y,72)*6)];
          p.ground(0,y*.2+.01,1,.183,tone);
          p.line([[0,y*.2+.195,0],[1,y*.2+.195,0]],'rgba(18,14,10,.45)',.7);
          const seam = ((y+seed)%3)*.31+.1;
          p.line([[seam,y*.2,0],[seam,y*.2+.2,0]],'rgba(18,14,10,.38)',.7);
          for(let n=0;n<4;n++) {
            const a=hash(seed+y,n,8), b=hash(n,y+seed,17);
            p.line([[a,y*.2+b*.18,.001],[Math.min(1,a+.25),y*.2+b*.18,.001]],'rgba(218,172,109,.1)',.45);
          }
        }
      }
    } else if(type === 'sidewalk') {
      p.line([[0,0,0],[1,0,0],[1,1,0]],'rgba(203,204,176,.16)',1);
      p.line([[0,0,0],[0,1,0],[1,1,0]],'rgba(26,34,28,.3)',1);
      if(seed%4===1) p.line([[.1,.3,0],[.4,.45,0],[.47,.72,0],[.9,.78,0]],'#555e51',.6);
    }
    // Fine mineral grain, individual grass blades, dust and occasional worn marks.
    for(let n=0;n<(type==='grass'?60:34);n++) {
      const a=hash(n,seed,801), b=hash(n,seed,107), q=p.point(a,b);
      ctx.fillStyle= n%3 ? 'rgba(10,17,13,.13)' : 'rgba(223,220,169,.12)';
      ctx.fillRect(q[0],q[1],type==='floor'?1.4:1,.65);
      if(type==='grass'&&n%4===0) {
        p.line([[a,b,.01],[a-.02,b-.01,.04+hash(n,seed,981)*.045]],n%8?'#647052':'#333e2a',.8);
      }
    }
    if(type==='road'&&seed%4===0) {
      p.line([[.1,.1,0],[.34,.42,0],[.2,.61,0],[.5,.84,0],[.55,1,0]],'rgba(14,22,20,.5)',.8);
      p.line([[.34,.42,0],[.66,.37,0]],'rgba(14,22,20,.5)',.6);
    }
    if(type==='grass'&&seed===3) {
      for(let n=0;n<5;n++) {const q=p.point(.25+hash(n,seed,5)*.3,.2+hash(n,seed,7)*.4);ctx.fillStyle='#93916a';ctx.fillRect(q[0],q[1],1.2,1.2);}
    }
    ctx.restore();
    return cv;
  }
  draw(ctx, tile, x, y) { ctx.drawImage(this.get(tile), x-34, y-2); }
}
