===== BEGIN FILE: save/README.md =====
# Save system

Import the public functions from `save/indexedDB.js`:

- `await loadGame()` returns a validated state, or `null` for an empty/cleared slot.
- `await saveGame(state)` captures an independent snapshot immediately and saves it.
- `await clearSave()` records a new empty slot on all available backends.
- `await exportSave(state)` downloads a versioned JSON save.
- `await importSave(file)` reads and validates a File/Blob, returning state. It does
  not write storage or mutate the running game.
- `serialize(state)` and `deserialize(envelopeOrJSON)` are also exported from their
  own modules. Version-1 plain state is accepted for early prototype migration.
- `getSaveStatus()` returns `{backend,lastSavedAt,error,warning}`. Browser consumers
  can also listen for the `deadfall:save-status` event and read `event.detail`.

Call `world.serialize()` and put its result into the snapshot's `worldChanges`
before saving. Persisted data includes player/inventory/skills, zombies, seed,
worldChanges, day/time/weather, objectives, stats, settings, and JSON extension
fields, including visited `spawnedChunks`. Messages, particles, nearby interaction
references, render data, location/save-status labels and pause state are excluded.
Loading resumes unpaused with empty particles/messages.

All persistence functions return promises. Catch `SaveError` and display its
`message`; do not report success before the promise resolves. Corrupt, unsupported,
oversized or unavailable saves reject. A valid surviving copy is recovered when
possible. A corrupt only copy is preserved and reported instead of silently
starting over. Imports accept only finite, bounded, safe plain JSON data.

IndexedDB database `deadfall`, object store `saves`, key `current` is primary.
The localStorage mirror/fallback key is `deadfall.save.v1`. The newest valid
timestamp wins at load. Requests time out after 2.5 seconds per database stage.
Writes within this module are ordered; database writes resolve only when their
transaction commits. Clear markers prevent an older copy returning after a
temporary backend outage. The localStorage mirror is written before asynchronous
database work, improving save reliability during `pagehide`. Multiple open tabs should not play the same survivor
concurrently: the latest completed snapshot is the survivor that will resume.

Run `node --test save/save.test.mjs` from the project root. No runtime packages
are required. The tests use Node's standard library and a focused asynchronous
IndexedDB test double; a real browser round trip is an integration check.

===== END FILE: save/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: save/deserializer.js =====
import { SaveError } from './errors.js';
import { SAVE_FORMAT, SAVE_VERSION, normalizeState, checkSize } from './schema.js';

export function parsePayload(payload) {
  if (typeof payload === 'string') {
    checkSize(payload);
    try { return JSON.parse(payload); }
    catch (cause) { throw new SaveError('This file is not valid save JSON.', 'INVALID_JSON', cause); }
  }
  return payload;
}

export function validateEnvelope(envelope) {
  if (!envelope || typeof envelope !== 'object' || Array.isArray(envelope) || envelope.format !== SAVE_FORMAT) {
    throw new SaveError('This file is not a Deadfall save.', 'INVALID_SAVE');
  }
  if (envelope.version !== SAVE_VERSION) throw new SaveError(`Save version ${envelope.version} is not supported by this version of Deadfall.`, 'UNSUPPORTED_VERSION');
  if (typeof envelope.savedAt !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(envelope.savedAt))) {
    throw new SaveError('Invalid save: the save date is missing or damaged.', 'INVALID_SAVE');
  }
}

/** Accept current envelopes or plain version-1 state for early prototype saves. */
export function deserialize(payload) {
  const parsed = parsePayload(payload);
  let data = parsed;
  if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && ('format' in parsed || 'data' in parsed)) {
    validateEnvelope(parsed);
    if (parsed.deleted === true) throw new SaveError('This save slot has been cleared.', 'EMPTY_SAVE');
    data = parsed.data;
  }
  const state = normalizeState(data);
  checkSize(JSON.stringify(state));
  state.paused = false;
  state.particles = [];
  state.messages = [];
  return state;
}

===== END FILE: save/deserializer.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: save/errors.js =====
/** Errors are safe to show directly in the game's save notification. */
export class SaveError extends Error {
  constructor(message, code = 'SAVE_FAILED', cause) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'SaveError';
    this.code = code;
    if (cause !== undefined) this.cause = cause;
  }
}

===== END FILE: save/errors.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: save/indexedDB.js =====
import { serialize, nextSavedAt, observeSavedAt } from './serializer.js';
import { deserialize, parsePayload, validateEnvelope } from './deserializer.js';
import { SaveError } from './errors.js';
import { SAVE_FORMAT, SAVE_VERSION, MAX_SAVE_BYTES } from './schema.js';

export { serialize, deserialize, SaveError };
export const DATABASE_NAME = 'deadfall';
export const STORE_NAME = 'saves';
export const SAVE_KEY = 'current';
export const LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY = 'deadfall.save.v1';
const TIMEOUT_MS = 2500;
let queue = Promise.resolve();
let status = { backend: null, lastSavedAt: null, error: null, warning: null };

/** Optional HUD hook. Errors are strings; event detail and snapshots are detached. */
export function getSaveStatus() { return { ...status }; }

function report(update) {
  status = { ...status, ...update };
  if (typeof globalThis.dispatchEvent === 'function' && typeof CustomEvent === 'function') {
    globalThis.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deadfall:save-status', { detail: getSaveStatus() }));
  }
}

function queued(operation) {
  const next = queue.then(operation);
  queue = next.catch(() => {});
  return next;
}

function asStorageError(causes, action) {
  const quota = causes.some(error => error?.name === 'QuotaExceededError' || error?.cause?.name === 'QuotaExceededError');
  return new SaveError(quota
    ? `Could not ${action}: browser storage is full. Export a save before closing the game.`
    : `Could not ${action}: browser storage is unavailable. Enable site storage or export a save before closing the game.`,
  quota ? 'STORAGE_FULL' : 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE', causes);
}

function openDatabase() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false;
    let request;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new SaveError('The save database did not open in time.', 'STORAGE_TIMEOUT')), TIMEOUT_MS);
    function finish(error, database) {
      if (settled) { database?.close(); return; }
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (error) reject(error); else resolve(database);
    }
    try {
      if (!globalThis.indexedDB) throw new SaveError('IndexedDB is unavailable.', 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE');
      request = globalThis.indexedDB.open(DATABASE_NAME, 1);
      request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
        if (settled) { request.transaction?.abort(); return; }
        if (!request.result.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) request.result.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME);
      };
      request.onsuccess = () => {
        const db = request.result;
        db.onversionchange = () => db.close();
        finish(null, db);
      };
      request.onerror = () => finish(request.error || new SaveError('Could not open the save database.', 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE'));
      // A blocked open can succeed after another tab releases its connection.
      // The timeout above keeps gameplay responsive if that never happens.
    } catch (error) { finish(error); }
  });
}

async function databaseOperation(mode, record) {
  const db = await openDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let transaction;
    let result;
    let settled = false;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      try { transaction?.abort(); } catch {}
      finish(new SaveError('The save database did not respond in time.', 'STORAGE_TIMEOUT'));
    }, TIMEOUT_MS);
    function finish(error) {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      db.close();
      if (error) reject(error); else resolve(result);
    }
    try {
      transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, mode);
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      const request = mode === 'readonly' ? store.get(SAVE_KEY) : store.put(record, SAVE_KEY);
      request.onsuccess = () => { result = request.result; };
      // A successful request is not durable until its transaction completes.
      transaction.oncomplete = () => finish();
      transaction.onabort = () => finish(transaction.error || request.error || new SaveError('The save transaction was cancelled.', 'SAVE_FAILED'));
      transaction.onerror = () => { /* onabort carries the final transaction result. */ };
    } catch (error) { finish(error); }
  });
}

function localRead() {
  if (!globalThis.localStorage) throw new SaveError('Local storage is unavailable.', 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE');
  return globalThis.localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY);
}

function localWrite(record) {
  if (!globalThis.localStorage) throw new SaveError('Local storage is unavailable.', 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE');
  globalThis.localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(record));
}

function candidate(payload, backend) {
  if (payload === undefined || payload === null) return null;
  const envelope = parsePayload(payload);
  if (envelope?.format === SAVE_FORMAT) {
    validateEnvelope(envelope);
    const state = envelope.deleted === true ? null : deserialize(envelope);
    return { state, savedAt: envelope.savedAt, backend, timestamp: Date.parse(envelope.savedAt) };
  }
  // Plain state is supported for prototype migration and predates envelopes.
  return { state: deserialize(envelope), savedAt: null, backend, timestamp: 0 };
}

/** Load the newest valid record, including clear markers that supersede old saves. */
export async function loadGame() {
  return queued(async () => {
    const reads = await Promise.allSettled([
      databaseOperation('readonly'),
      Promise.resolve().then(localRead),
    ]);
    const candidates = [];
    const errors = [];
    let available = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < reads.length; i++) {
      const read = reads[i];
      if (read.status === 'rejected') { errors.push(read.reason); continue; }
      available++;
      try {
        const result = candidate(read.value, i === 0 ? 'indexedDB' : 'localStorage');
        if (result) candidates.push(result);
      } catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
    }
    candidates.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);
    if (candidates.length) {
      const chosen = candidates[0];
      if (chosen.savedAt) observeSavedAt(chosen.savedAt);
      report({ backend: chosen.backend, lastSavedAt: chosen.state ? chosen.savedAt : null, error: null, warning: errors.length ? 'Recovered using the available browser save storage.' : null });
      return chosen.state;
    }
    const invalid = errors.find(error => ['INVALID_SAVE', 'INVALID_JSON', 'UNSUPPORTED_VERSION', 'SAVE_TOO_LARGE'].includes(error?.code));
    if (invalid || !available) {
      const error = invalid || asStorageError(errors, 'load the game');
      report({ error: error.message });
      throw error;
    }
    report({ backend: reads[0].status === 'fulfilled' ? 'indexedDB' : 'localStorage', lastSavedAt: null, error: null, warning: errors.length ? 'Using the available browser save storage.' : null });
    return null;
  });
}

async function persist(record) {
  // Timestamp when this queued write starts, so a clear followed immediately by
  // a save remains ordered even though saveGame captures its snapshot eagerly.
  record = { ...record, savedAt: nextSavedAt() };
  const errors = [];
  // Write the synchronous mirror before awaiting IndexedDB. On pagehide the
  // browser may discard pending database work as soon as the page is unloaded.
  let fallback = false;
  try { localWrite(record); fallback = true; }
  catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
  let primary = false;
  try { await databaseOperation('readwrite', record); primary = true; }
  catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
  if (!primary && !fallback) {
    const error = asStorageError(errors, record.deleted ? 'clear the save' : 'save the game');
    report({ error: error.message });
    throw error;
  }
  report({ backend: primary ? 'indexedDB' : 'localStorage', lastSavedAt: record.deleted ? null : record.savedAt, error: null,
    warning: primary ? null : 'IndexedDB is unavailable; saved using local storage.' });
}

/** Snapshot immediately; serialize writes so an older autosave cannot win a race. */
export async function saveGame(state) {
  let record;
  try { record = serialize(state); }
  catch (error) { report({ error: error.message }); throw error; }
  return queued(() => persist(record));
}

/** A timestamped clear marker prevents an inaccessible backend resurrecting a save. */
export async function clearSave() {
  return queued(() => persist({ format: SAVE_FORMAT, version: SAVE_VERSION, savedAt: nextSavedAt(), deleted: true }));
}

export async function exportSave(state) {
  const envelope = serialize(state);
  if (typeof document === 'undefined' || typeof globalThis.URL?.createObjectURL !== 'function') {
    throw new SaveError('Save downloads are available in a browser.', 'DOWNLOAD_UNAVAILABLE');
  }
  const json = JSON.stringify(envelope);
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' }));
  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = `deadfall-day-${envelope.data.day}-${envelope.savedAt.slice(0, 19).replaceAll(':', '-')}.json`;
  anchor.style.display = 'none';
  try {
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
  } finally {
    anchor.remove();
    // Give browsers time to start consuming the download before revoking its URL.
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 30000);
  }
}

/** Validate only: the caller restores the world and explicitly saves after success. */
export async function importSave(file) {
  if (!file || typeof file.text !== 'function') throw new SaveError('Choose a Deadfall JSON save file.', 'INVALID_FILE');
  if (typeof file.size === 'number' && file.size > MAX_SAVE_BYTES) throw new SaveError('This save exceeds the 20 MB size limit.', 'SAVE_TOO_LARGE');
  let payload;
  try { payload = await file.text(); }
  catch (cause) { throw new SaveError('Could not read the selected save file.', 'FILE_READ_FAILED', cause); }
  return deserialize(payload);
}

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { serialize } from './serializer.js';
import { deserialize } from './deserializer.js';
import { MAX_SAVE_BYTES } from './schema.js';

function state() {
  return {
    version: 1, seed: 74219, time: 540, day: 3, elapsed: 63, paused: true, dead: false,
    weather: { type: 'rain', intensity: 0.7, temperature: 12, nextChange: 400 },
    player: { id: 1, x: 7.5, y: 7.5, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, health: 88, hunger: 86,
      thirst: 82, energy: 100, stamina: 62, bleeding: 0, infection: 0,
      inventory: [{ id: 'bat', qty: 1 }, { id: 'water', qty: 2 }], equipped: 'bat',
      skills: { strength: 2, fitness: 0.25, crafting: 3 }, kills: 4 },
    zombies: [{ id: 101, x: 14, y: 8, health: 60, mode: 'chase', dirX: -1,
      dirY: 0, attackCooldown: 0.5, memory: { x: 7.5, y: 7.5 } }],
    particles: [{ x: 1 }], messages: [{ text: 'test', at: 12345 }],
    render: { fps: 60 }, nearby: { type: 'door' }, location: 'Safehouse', saveStatus: 'Saving…',
    worldChanges: { 'door:12:7': { open: false, barricaded: 2 },
      'crate:8:7': { looted: true, loot: [] } },
    objectives: { looted: 2, crafted: 1, barricaded: 2 }, stats: { distance: 54.5 },
    settings: { sound: true }, noise: 4, spawnedChunks: ['0,0', '1,0'],
  };
}

function fakeLocalStorage() {
  const data = new Map();
  return { data, getItem: key => data.get(key) ?? null,
    setItem: (key, value) => data.set(key, String(value)),
    removeItem: key => data.delete(key), clear: () => data.clear() };
}

// This double schedules request and transaction completion separately. Writes
// only become visible on commit, allowing abort and request-success races.
function fakeIndexedDB() {
  const records = new Map();
  const controls = { abortWrites: false, closeCount: 0, commitDelay: 0 };
  const api = {
    records, controls,
    open() {
      const request = {};
      setTimeout(() => {
        const db = {
          objectStoreNames: { contains: () => true },
          close() { controls.closeCount++; },
          transaction(_store, mode) {
            let aborted = false;
            let pending;
            const transaction = {
              error: null,
              abort() {
                if (aborted) return;
                aborted = true;
                transaction.error = new DOMException('Aborted', 'AbortError');
                queueMicrotask(() => transaction.onabort?.());
              },
              objectStore() {
                function operation(key, value) {
                  const operationRequest = {};
                  pending = { key, value };
                  setTimeout(() => {
                    operationRequest.result = mode === 'readonly' ? structuredClone(records.get(key)) : key;
                    operationRequest.onsuccess?.();
                    setTimeout(() => {
                      if (aborted) return;
                      if (mode === 'readwrite' && controls.abortWrites) { transaction.abort(); return; }
                      if (mode === 'readwrite') records.set(pending.key, structuredClone(pending.value));
                      transaction.oncomplete?.();
                    }, controls.commitDelay);
                  }, 0);
                  return operationRequest;
                }
                return { get: key => operation(key), put: (value, key) => operation(key, value) };
              },
            };
            return transaction;
          },
        };
        request.result = db;
        request.onsuccess?.();
      }, 0);
      return request;
    },
  };
  return api;
}

let moduleId = 0;
async function storage(t, indexedDB, localStorage) {
  const priorIDB = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'indexedDB');
  const priorLocal = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'localStorage');
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'indexedDB', { configurable: true, value: indexedDB });
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', { configurable: true, value: localStorage });
  t.after(() => {
    if (priorIDB) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'indexedDB', priorIDB); else delete globalThis.indexedDB;
    if (priorLocal) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', priorLocal); else delete globalThis.localStorage;
  });
  return import(`./indexedDB.js?test=${++moduleId}`);
}

test('round trip preserves all persistent world, actor and extension fields', () => {
  const original = state();
  const before = structuredClone(original);
  const envelope = serialize(original);
  for (const key of ['particles', 'messages', 'render', 'nearby', 'paused', 'saveStatus', 'location']) assert.equal(key in envelope.data, false);
  const restored = deserialize(JSON.stringify(envelope));
  assert.deepEqual(original, before);
  for (const key of ['worldChanges', 'player', 'zombies', 'weather', 'objectives', 'stats', 'spawnedChunks']) assert.deepEqual(restored[key], original[key]);
  assert.equal(restored.paused, false);
  assert.deepEqual(restored.messages, []);
  assert.deepEqual(restored.particles, []);
  original.player.inventory[0].qty = 99;
  original.worldChanges['door:12:7'].barricaded = 0;
  assert.equal(restored.player.inventory[0].qty, 1);
  assert.equal(envelope.data.worldChanges['door:12:7'].barricaded, 2);
});

test('transient objects may be circular and are never traversed', () => {
  const original = state();
  original.nearby.state = original;
  original.render.canvas = original;
  assert.doesNotThrow(() => serialize(original));
});

test('plain prototype state receives missing optional defaults', () => {
  const restored = deserialize({ seed: 42, time: 60, player: { x: 1, y: 2, health: 100, inventory: [] } });
  assert.equal(restored.version, 1);
  assert.equal(restored.day, 1);
  assert.equal(restored.player.stamina, 100);
  assert.deepEqual(restored.zombies, []);
  assert.equal(restored.paused, false);
});

test('invalid values, unsafe keys, cycles and unsupported versions are rejected', () => {
  for (const mutate of [
    s => { s.player.x = NaN; }, s => { s.player.health = 200; },
    s => { s.player.inventory[0].qty = -1; }, s => { s.time = 'noon'; },
    s => { s.zombies.push({ ...s.zombies[0] }); }, s => { s.worldChanges.self = s.worldChanges; },
    s => { s.worldChanges = JSON.parse('{"__proto__":{"polluted":true}}'); },
    s => { s.worldChanges = new Map(); }, s => { s.weather.intensity = Infinity; },
    s => { s.spawnedChunks = {}; }, s => { s.spawnedChunks = ['invalid-chunk']; },
  ]) {
    const input = state(); mutate(input);
    assert.throws(() => serialize(input), { code: 'INVALID_SAVE' });
  }
  assert.throws(() => deserialize('{bad json'), { code: 'INVALID_JSON' });
  const future = serialize(state()); future.version = 99;
  assert.throws(() => deserialize(future), { code: 'UNSUPPORTED_VERSION' });
  assert.equal({}.polluted, undefined);
});

test('zero health resumes as dead even when a stale death flag is false', () => {
  const input = state(); input.player.health = 0;
  assert.equal(deserialize(serialize(input)).dead, true);
});

test('IndexedDB round trip commits, mirrors and closes connections', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  assert.equal(await api.loadGame(), null);
  await api.saveGame(state());
  assert.ok(db.records.has(api.SAVE_KEY));
  assert.ok(local.data.has(api.LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY));
  assert.equal(api.getSaveStatus().backend, 'indexedDB');
  assert.deepEqual((await api.loadGame()).worldChanges, state().worldChanges);
  assert.equal(db.controls.closeCount, 3);
});

test('unavailable IndexedDB uses localStorage and reports the fallback', async t => {
  const local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, undefined, local);
  await api.saveGame(state());
  assert.equal(api.getSaveStatus().backend, 'localStorage');
  assert.match(api.getSaveStatus().warning, /local storage/);
  assert.deepEqual((await api.loadGame()).spawnedChunks, ['0,0', '1,0']);
  await api.clearSave();
  assert.equal(await api.loadGame(), null);
});

test('both storage backends unavailable reject with an actionable error', async t => {
  const api = await storage(t, undefined, undefined);
  await assert.rejects(api.saveGame(state()), { code: 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' });
  await assert.rejects(api.loadGame(), { code: 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' });
  await assert.rejects(api.clearSave(), { code: 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' });
  assert.match(api.getSaveStatus().error, /export a save/i);
});

test('quota failures are reported and a rejected operation does not poison the queue', async t => {
  const local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const goodWrite = local.setItem;
  local.setItem = () => { throw new DOMException('Full', 'QuotaExceededError'); };
  const api = await storage(t, undefined, local);
  await assert.rejects(api.saveGame(state()), { code: 'STORAGE_FULL' });
  local.setItem = goodWrite;
  await api.saveGame(state());
  assert.equal((await api.loadGame()).day, 3);
});

test('snapshot is captured before waiting; overlapping writes preserve invocation order', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage();
  db.controls.commitDelay = 8;
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  const first = state();
  const one = api.saveGame(first);
  first.player.x = 999;
  const second = state(); second.day = 4;
  const two = api.saveGame(second);
  second.day = 99;
  await Promise.all([one, two]);
  const restored = await api.loadGame();
  assert.equal(restored.day, 4);
  assert.equal(restored.player.x, 7.5);
});

  const api = await storage(t, db, undefined);
  await assert.rejects(api.saveGame(state()), { code: 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE' });

});

test('an aborted database transaction still recovers the synchronous mirror', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage(); db.controls.abortWrites = true;
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  await api.saveGame(state());
  assert.equal(api.getSaveStatus().backend, 'localStorage');
  assert.equal((await api.loadGame()).day, 3);

test('load chooses the newest valid copy and preserves a corrupt only copy', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  const older = serialize(state());
  const newerState = state(); newerState.day = 7;
  const newer = serialize(newerState);
  db.records.set(api.SAVE_KEY, older);
  local.setItem(api.LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(newer));
  assert.equal((await api.loadGame()).day, 7);
  local.setItem(api.LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, '{broken');
  assert.equal((await api.loadGame()).day, 3);
  db.records.clear();
  await assert.rejects(api.loadGame(), { code: 'INVALID_JSON' });
  assert.equal(local.getItem(api.LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY), '{broken');
});

test('clear marker supersedes a stale database after a temporary write outage', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  await api.saveGame(state());
  db.controls.abortWrites = true;
  await api.clearSave();
  assert.ok(db.records.get(api.SAVE_KEY).data);
  assert.equal(await api.loadGame(), null);
});

test('a queued clear followed by a save produces a later timestamp even if one backend fails', async t => {
  const db = fakeIndexedDB(), local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, db, local);
  const first = api.clearSave();
  const second = api.saveGame(state());
  await Promise.all([first, second]);
  const saved = db.records.get(api.SAVE_KEY);
  assert.ok(saved.data);
  assert.equal((await api.loadGame()).day, 3);
  assert.equal(saved.savedAt, JSON.parse(local.getItem(api.LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY)).savedAt);
});

test('import validates before storage and handles oversized or unreadable files', async t => {
  const local = fakeLocalStorage();
  const api = await storage(t, undefined, local);
  const file = new Blob([JSON.stringify(serialize(state()))], { type: 'application/json' });
  assert.equal((await api.importSave(file)).day, 3);
  assert.equal(local.data.size, 0);
  await assert.rejects(api.importSave(new Blob(['nope'])), { code: 'INVALID_JSON' });
  await assert.rejects(api.importSave({ size: MAX_SAVE_BYTES + 1, text() {} }), { code: 'SAVE_TOO_LARGE' });
  await assert.rejects(api.importSave({ async text() { throw Error('Unreadable'); } }), { code: 'FILE_READ_FAILED' });
  await assert.rejects(api.importSave(null), { code: 'INVALID_FILE' });
});

===== BEGIN FILE: save/schema.js =====
import { SaveError } from './errors.js';

export const SAVE_FORMAT = 'deadfall-save';
export const SAVE_VERSION = 1;
export const MAX_SAVE_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024;
const TRANSIENT_KEYS = new Set(['particles', 'messages', 'render', 'nearby', 'paused', 'saveStatus', 'location']);
const UNSAFE_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'prototype', 'constructor']);

function invalid(path, detail) {
  throw new SaveError(`Invalid save: ${path} ${detail}.`, 'INVALID_SAVE');
}

function object(value, path) {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
    invalid(path, 'must be an object');
  }
  return value;
}

function number(value, path, min = -Infinity, max = Infinity, integer = false) {
  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < min || value > max || (integer && !Number.isInteger(value))) {
    invalid(path, 'contains an invalid number');
  }
}

function text(value, path, max = 256) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.length || value.length > max) invalid(path, 'contains invalid text');
}

/** Copy only JSON data; reject cycles, oversized trees and unsafe property names. */
function copyJSON(value, path, context, depth = 0) {
  if (++context.nodes > 500000 || depth > 64) invalid(path, 'is too complex');
  if (value === null || typeof value === 'boolean') return value;
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    number(value, path);
    return value;
  }
  if (typeof value === 'string') {
    if (value.length > 1000000) invalid(path, 'is too long');
    return value;
  }
  if (typeof value !== 'object') invalid(path, 'must contain only JSON data');
  if (context.parents.has(value)) invalid(path, 'contains a circular reference');
  const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
  if (!Array.isArray(value) && prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) invalid(path, 'must contain plain objects');
  context.parents.add(value);
  let result;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    if (value.length > 100000) invalid(path, 'has too many entries');
    result = Array.from(value, (entry, index) => copyJSON(entry, `${path}[${index}]`, context, depth + 1));
  } else {
    result = {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
      if (UNSAFE_KEYS.has(key) || key.length > 512) invalid(path, 'contains an unsafe property');
      // Optional properties are omitted just as they are by JSON.stringify.
      if (value[key] !== undefined) result[key] = copyJSON(value[key], `${path}.${key}`, context, depth + 1);
    }
  }
  context.parents.delete(value);
  return result;
}

function inventory(items, path) {
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length > 10000) invalid(path, 'must be an item list');
  items.forEach((item, index) => {
    const name = `${path}[${index}]`;
    object(item, name);
    text(item.id, `${name}.id`);
    number(item.qty, `${name}.qty`, 0, 1000000, true);
  });
}

function position(actor, path) {
  object(actor, path);
  number(actor.x, `${path}.x`, -1000000, 1000000);
  number(actor.y, `${path}.y`, -1000000, 1000000);
}

function defaultNumber(target, key, fallback, path, min, max, integer = false) {
  if (target[key] === undefined) target[key] = fallback;
  number(target[key], `${path}.${key}`, min, max, integer);
}

/** Returns an independent validated persistent snapshot, preserving extension data. */
export function normalizeState(input) {
  object(input, 'state');
  const persistent = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(input)) {
    if (UNSAFE_KEYS.has(key)) invalid('state', 'contains an unsafe property');
    if (!TRANSIENT_KEYS.has(key) && input[key] !== undefined) persistent[key] = input[key];
  }
  const state = copyJSON(persistent, 'state', { nodes: 0, parents: new Set() });
  if (state.version === undefined) state.version = SAVE_VERSION;
  if (state.version !== SAVE_VERSION) throw new SaveError(`Save version ${state.version} is not supported by this version of Deadfall.`, 'UNSUPPORTED_VERSION');
  number(state.seed, 'state.seed', -2147483648, 4294967295, true);
  number(state.time, 'state.time', 0, 1440);
  defaultNumber(state, 'day', 1, 'state', 1, 1000000, true);
  defaultNumber(state, 'elapsed', 0, 'state', 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
  defaultNumber(state, 'noise', 0, 'state', 0, 1000000);
  position(state.player, 'state.player');
  const player = state.player;
  if (player.id === undefined) player.id = 1;
  if ((typeof player.id !== 'number' && typeof player.id !== 'string') || player.id === '') invalid('state.player.id', 'must be an actor ID');
  number(player.health, 'state.player.health', 0, 100);
  inventory(player.inventory, 'state.player.inventory');
  for (const key of ['hunger', 'thirst', 'energy', 'stamina']) defaultNumber(player, key, 100, 'state.player', 0, 100);
  for (const key of ['bleeding', 'infection']) defaultNumber(player, key, 0, 'state.player', 0, 100);
  defaultNumber(player, 'dirX', 1, 'state.player', -1, 1);
  defaultNumber(player, 'dirY', 0, 'state.player', -1, 1);
  defaultNumber(player, 'kills', 0, 'state.player', 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, true);
  if (player.equipped === undefined) player.equipped = null;
  if (player.equipped !== null) text(player.equipped, 'state.player.equipped');
  if (player.skills === undefined) player.skills = {};
  object(player.skills, 'state.player.skills');
  for (const key of ['strength', 'fitness', 'crafting']) defaultNumber(player.skills, key, 0, 'state.player.skills', 0, 1000000000);
  if (state.dead === undefined) state.dead = player.health <= 0;
  if (typeof state.dead !== 'boolean') invalid('state.dead', 'must be a boolean');
  if (player.health <= 0) state.dead = true;
  if (state.zombies === undefined) state.zombies = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(state.zombies) || state.zombies.length > 10000) invalid('state.zombies', 'must be a zombie list');
  const ids = new Set();
  state.zombies.forEach((zombie, index) => {
    const path = `state.zombies[${index}]`;
    position(zombie, path);
    if ((typeof zombie.id !== 'string' && typeof zombie.id !== 'number') || zombie.id === '' || ids.has(zombie.id)) invalid(`${path}.id`, 'must be unique');
    ids.add(zombie.id);
    number(zombie.health, `${path}.health`, -1000000, 1000000);
    if (zombie.mode === undefined) zombie.mode = 'idle';
    if (!['idle', 'wander', 'chase', 'attack', 'dead'].includes(zombie.mode)) invalid(`${path}.mode`, 'is unknown');
    defaultNumber(zombie, 'dirX', 1, path, -1, 1);
    defaultNumber(zombie, 'dirY', 0, path, -1, 1);
    defaultNumber(zombie, 'attackCooldown', 0, path, 0, 1000000);
  });
  if (state.worldChanges === undefined) state.worldChanges = {};
  object(state.worldChanges, 'state.worldChanges');
  if (state.spawnedChunks !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(state.spawnedChunks)) invalid('state.spawnedChunks', 'must be a chunk list');
    state.spawnedChunks.forEach((key, index) => {
      text(key, `state.spawnedChunks[${index}]`);
      if (!/^-?\d+,-?\d+$/.test(key)) invalid(`state.spawnedChunks[${index}]`, 'must identify a chunk');
    });
  }
  if (state.weather === undefined) state.weather = { type: 'overcast', intensity: 0.25, temperature: 16 };
  object(state.weather, 'state.weather');
  if (state.weather.type === undefined) state.weather.type = 'overcast';
  text(state.weather.type, 'state.weather.type', 64);
  defaultNumber(state.weather, 'intensity', 0.25, 'state.weather', 0, 1);
  defaultNumber(state.weather, 'temperature', 16, 'state.weather', -200, 200);
  for (const [key, defaults] of Object.entries({ objectives: { looted: 0, crafted: 0, barricaded: 0 }, stats: { distance: 0 } })) {
    if (state[key] === undefined) state[key] = {};
    object(state[key], `state.${key}`);
    for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(defaults)) defaultNumber(state[key], field, value, `state.${key}`, 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
  }
  if (state.settings === undefined) state.settings = { sound: true };
  object(state.settings, 'state.settings');
  if (state.settings.sound === undefined) state.settings.sound = true;
  if (typeof state.settings.sound !== 'boolean') invalid('state.settings.sound', 'must be a boolean');
  return state;
}

export function checkSize(json) {
  // UTF-8 size, not UTF-16 code units; Blob is available in all target browsers.
  const bytes = typeof TextEncoder === 'function' ? new TextEncoder().encode(json).byteLength : json.length * 3;
  if (bytes > MAX_SAVE_BYTES) throw new SaveError('This save exceeds the 20 MB size limit.', 'SAVE_TOO_LARGE');
}

===== END FILE: save/schema.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: save/serializer.js =====
import { SAVE_FORMAT, SAVE_VERSION, normalizeState, checkSize } from './schema.js';

let lastTimestamp = 0;

export function nextSavedAt() {
  lastTimestamp = Math.max(Date.now(), lastTimestamp + 1);
  return new Date(lastTimestamp).toISOString();
}

export function observeSavedAt(savedAt) {
  const time = Date.parse(savedAt);
  if (Number.isFinite(time)) lastTimestamp = Math.max(lastTimestamp, time);
}

/** Create a versioned JSON envelope without retaining references to live state. */
export function serialize(state) {
  const envelope = {
    format: SAVE_FORMAT,
    version: SAVE_VERSION,
    savedAt: nextSavedAt(),
    data: normalizeState(state),
  };
  checkSize(JSON.stringify(envelope));
  return envelope;
}

===== END FILE: save/serializer.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: systems/combat.js =====
export const WEAPONS = Object.freeze({
  unarmed: { name: 'Shove', range: 1.15, damage: 12, stamina: 6, cooldown: 0.55, arc: 0.25, knockback: 3.2, targets: 1, noise: 3 },
  bat: { name: 'Baseball bat', range: 1.75, damage: 36, stamina: 9, cooldown: 0.58, arc: 0.2, knockback: 2.7, targets: 2, noise: 6 },
  axe: { name: 'Fire axe', range: 1.6, damage: 57, stamina: 15, cooldown: 0.82, arc: 0.35, knockback: 1.8, targets: 2, noise: 7 },
  pistol: { name: 'Pistol', range: 13, damage: 62, stamina: 3, cooldown: 0.42, arc: 0.965, knockback: 1.1, targets: 1, noise: 32, ranged: true },
});

function random(value) {
  let x = Math.imul((value | 0) ^ 0x9e3779b9, 0x85ebca6b);
  x = Math.imul(x ^ x >>> 13, 0xc2b2ae35);
  return ((x ^ x >>> 16) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

function addParticle(state, particle) {
  state.particles ||= [];
  if (state.particles.length >= 220) state.particles.shift();
  state.particles.push(particle);
}

function blood(state, x, y, dx, dy, count, seed) {
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const angle = random(seed + i * 37) * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = 0.5 + random(seed + i * 41) * 1.8;
    const life = 0.35 + random(seed + i * 43) * 0.65;
    addParticle(state, { type: 'blood', x, y, z: 0.55, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed + dx * 0.5,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed + dy * 0.5, vz: 0.5 + random(seed + i) * 1.2,
      life, maxLife: life, size: 1.5 + random(seed + i * 13) * 2.5,
      color: i % 3 === 0 ? '#802b23' : '#57221d' });
  }
}

function clearShot(world, ax, ay, bx, by) {
  if (!world?.isBlocked) return true;
  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay) * 8));
  let lx = ax, ly = ay;
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = ax + (bx - ax) * i / steps, y = ay + (by - ay) * i / steps;
    if (world.isBlocked(x, y)) return false;
    if (Math.floor(lx) !== Math.floor(x) && Math.floor(ly) !== Math.floor(y) &&
      (world.isBlocked(x, ly) || world.isBlocked(lx, y))) return false;
    lx = x; ly = y;
  }
  return true;
}

/** Resolve a deliberate swing or shot, with optional world-coordinate aiming. */
export function attack(state, world, target) {
  const p = state.player, miss = { hit: false, killed: 0 };
  if (state.paused || state.dead || p.health <= 0 || (p.attackCooldown || 0) > 0) return miss;
  const owned = (p.inventory || []).some(item => item.id === p.equipped && item.qty > 0);
  const weaponId = owned && WEAPONS[p.equipped] ? p.equipped : 'unarmed';
  const weapon = WEAPONS[weaponId];
  if ((p.stamina ?? 100) < weapon.stamina * 0.45) return { ...miss, message: 'Too exhausted. Back away and catch your breath.' };
  let ammo;
  if (weapon.ranged) {
    ammo = p.inventory.find(item => item.id === 'ammo' && item.qty > 0);
    if (!ammo) return { ...miss, message: 'No ammunition. Equip a melee weapon.' };
  }
  if (target && Number.isFinite(target.x) && Number.isFinite(target.y)) {
    const dx = target.x - p.x, dy = target.y - p.y, length = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (length > 0.01) { p.dirX = dx / length; p.dirY = dy / length; }
  }
  let dirX = p.dirX || 0, dirY = p.dirY || 0;
  const facingLength = Math.hypot(dirX, dirY);
  if (facingLength < 0.01) dirX = 1;
  else { dirX /= facingLength; dirY /= facingLength; }
  const fatigue = 0.63 + Math.min(100, p.stamina ?? 100) * 0.0037;
  p.stamina = Math.max(0, (p.stamina ?? 100) - weapon.stamina);
  p.attackCooldown = weapon.cooldown * ((p.energy ?? 100) < 15 ? 1.25 : 1);
  p.attackDuration = weapon.ranged ? 0.15 : 0.28;
  p.attackTimer = p.attackDuration;
  p.attackWeapon = weaponId;
  p.attackCount = (p.attackCount || 0) + 1;
  p.resting = false;
  state.noise = Math.max(state.noise || 0, weapon.noise);
  state.lastAttack = { x: p.x, y: p.y, dirX, dirY, weapon: weaponId, range: weapon.range,
    ttl: p.attackDuration, duration: p.attackDuration };
  if (ammo) {
    ammo.qty--;
    p.inventory = p.inventory.filter(item => item.qty > 0);
    addParticle(state, { type: 'muzzle', x: p.x + dirX * 0.45, y: p.y + dirY * 0.45,
      z: 0.72, life: 0.09, maxLife: 0.09, size: 8, color: '#ffe4a3', vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
  }
  const candidates = [];
  for (const z of state.zombies || []) {
    if (z.health <= 0) continue;
    const dx = z.x - p.x, dy = z.y - p.y, distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (distance > weapon.range) continue;
    const facing = distance < 0.05 ? 1 : (dx * dirX + dy * dirY) / distance;
    // The pistol has a narrow reticle; melee attacks sweep a broad forward arc.
    const aim = weapon.ranged ? facing >= weapon.arc && Math.abs(dx * dirY - dy * dirX) < 0.4 : facing >= weapon.arc;
    if (!aim || !clearShot(world, p.x, p.y, z.x, z.y)) continue;
    candidates.push({ z, distance, facing });
  }
  candidates.sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance);
  let killed = 0, hits = 0;
  for (const { z, distance } of candidates.slice(0, weapon.targets)) {
    const seed = (state.seed || 74219) + p.attackCount * 941 + (Number(z.id) || 0);
    const skill = Math.min(12, Math.max(0, p.skills?.strength || 0) / 50);
    const sweep = hits > 0 ? 0.6 : 1;
    const falloff = weapon.ranged ? Math.max(0.68, 1 - distance * 0.018) : 1;
    const damage = Math.round((weapon.damage + skill) * fatigue * sweep * falloff * (0.93 + random(seed) * 0.14));
    z.health = Math.max(0, z.health - damage);
    z.hitTimer = 0.3;
    z.knockbackX = dirX * weapon.knockback;
    z.knockbackY = dirY * weapon.knockback;
    z.canAttack = false;
    z.attackCooldown = Math.max(z.attackCooldown || 0, 0.45);
    blood(state, z.x, z.y, dirX, dirY, z.health <= 0 ? 12 : 7, seed);
    if (z.health <= 0) {
      killed++;
      z.mode = 'idle'; z.vx = z.vy = 0; z.deathTimer = 0;
      z.deathDirection = Math.atan2(dirY, dirX);
      p.kills = (p.kills || 0) + 1;
    }
    hits++;
  }
  if (hits) {
    p.skills ||= { strength: 0, fitness: 0, crafting: 0 };
    p.skills.strength = (p.skills.strength || 0) + hits * 0.8;
  }
  return { hit: hits > 0, killed, ...(killed ? { message: killed > 1 ? `${killed} infected put down.` : 'Infected put down.' } : {}) };
}

/** Advance attack feedback, enemy strikes, noise, and short-lived particles. */
export function updateCombat(state, dt) {
  if (state.paused || state.dead || !Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0) return;
  const p = state.player;
  for (const key of ['attackCooldown', 'attackTimer', 'hurtCooldown', 'hurtTimer']) p[key] = Math.max(0, (p[key] || 0) - dt);
  state.noise = Math.max(0, (state.noise || 0) - dt * 4.5);
  if (state.lastAttack) {
    state.lastAttack.ttl -= dt;
    if (state.lastAttack.ttl <= 0) delete state.lastAttack;
  }
  for (const z of state.zombies || []) {
    z.hitTimer = Math.max(0, (z.hitTimer || 0) - dt);
    z.attackTimer = Math.max(0, (z.attackTimer || 0) - dt);
    z.attackCooldown = Math.max(0, (z.attackCooldown || 0) - dt);
    if (z.health <= 0) { z.deathTimer = (z.deathTimer || 0) + dt; continue; }
    if (z.mode !== 'attack' || z.canAttack === false || z.attackCooldown > 0 || z.hitTimer > 0.12 ||
      Math.hypot(z.x - p.x, z.y - p.y) > 1.05) continue;
    z.attackCooldown = 1.5 + random(Number(z.id) || 13) * 0.45;
    z.attackTimer = 0.32;
    if (p.hurtCooldown > 0 || p.health <= 0) continue;
    p.damageEvents = (p.damageEvents || 0) + 1;
    const seed = (state.seed || 74219) + p.damageEvents * 491 + (Number(z.id) || 13);
    const roll = random(seed);
    const damage = 7 + Math.floor(random(seed + 71) * 6);
    p.health = Math.max(0, p.health - damage);
    p.hurtCooldown = 0.48;
    p.hurtTimer = 0.42;
    if (roll < 0.38) p.bleeding = Math.min(100, (p.bleeding || 0) + 7 + Math.floor(roll * 20));
    if (roll < 0.035) p.infection = Math.min(100, (p.infection || 0) + 6);
    blood(state, p.x, p.y, p.x - z.x, p.y - z.y, 6, seed);
    p.resting = false;
  }
  if (p.health <= 0) { p.health = 0; state.dead = true; }
  const particles = state.particles || [];
  let write = 0;
  for (const particle of particles) {
    particle.life = (particle.life ?? 0.5) - dt;
    if (particle.life <= 0) continue;
    particle.x += (particle.vx || 0) * dt;
    particle.y += (particle.vy || 0) * dt;
    if (Number.isFinite(particle.z)) {
      particle.z = Math.max(0, particle.z + (particle.vz || 0) * dt);
      if (particle.type === 'blood' || particle.type === 'dust') particle.vz = (particle.vz || 0) - dt * 4;
      if (particle.z === 0) { particle.vx = 0; particle.vy = 0; particle.vz = 0; }
    }
    particles[write++] = particle;
  }
  particles.length = write;
}

===== END FILE: systems/combat.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: systems/needs.js =====
const clamp = value => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0));
const positions = new WeakMap();

/** Rates are per real second; food/water matter over several in-game days. */
export function updateNeeds(state, dt) {
  if (state.paused || state.dead || !Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0) return;
  const p = state.player;
  const last = positions.get(p);
  const measuredMovement = last && Math.hypot(p.x - last.x, p.y - last.y) > 0.001;
  positions.set(p, { x: p.x, y: p.y });
  const moving = p.moving ?? measuredMovement ?? false;
  const running = !!(p.running ?? state.running) && moving;
  const resting = !!p.resting && !moving;
  const exertion = running ? 1.9 : moving ? 1.2 : 1;
  const heat = Math.max(0, (state.weather?.temperature ?? 16) - 24) * 0.025;
  p.hunger = clamp((p.hunger ?? 100) - dt * 0.043 * exertion);
  p.thirst = clamp((p.thirst ?? 100) - dt * 0.065 * (exertion + heat));
  // The game owns rest energy/health bonuses so they are not applied twice.
  if (!resting) p.energy = clamp((p.energy ?? 100) - dt * (running ? 0.13 : moving ? 0.055 : 0.026));
  else p.energy = clamp(p.energy ?? 100);
  const fitness = Math.min(10, Math.max(0, p.skills?.fitness || 0) / 100);
  if (running) p.stamina = clamp((p.stamina ?? 100) - dt * Math.max(5.8, 9 - fitness * 0.25));
  else {
    const fed = p.hunger > 15 && p.thirst > 15 ? 1 : 0.45;
    const rested = p.energy > 15 ? 1 : 0.55;
    const recovering = (p.attackCooldown || 0) > 0 ? 0.15 : 1;
    const recovery = (resting ? 23 : moving ? 9 : 16) * fed * rested * recovering;
    p.stamina = clamp((p.stamina ?? 100) + dt * recovery);
  }
  p.bleeding = clamp(p.bleeding || 0);
  p.infection = clamp(p.infection || 0);
  let harm = p.bleeding * 0.009;
  if (p.hunger <= 0) harm += 0.085;
  if (p.thirst <= 0) harm += 0.22;
  if (p.infection > 0) {
    p.infection = clamp(p.infection + dt * 0.004);
    if (p.infection >= 45) harm += (p.infection - 40) * 0.0015;
  }
  p.health = clamp((p.health ?? 100) - harm * dt);
  p.exhausted = p.stamina < 10 || p.energy < 12;
  if (p.health <= 0) state.dead = true;
}

===== END FILE: systems/needs.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: systems/weather.js =====
const TYPES = ['clear', 'overcast', 'rain', 'fog', 'storm'];
function random(seed) {
  let n = Math.imul((seed | 0) ^ 0x9e3779b9, 0x85ebca6b);
  n = Math.imul(n ^ n >>> 13, 0xc2b2ae35);
  return ((n ^ n >>> 16) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

/** Five in-game minutes per real second; weather fronts last six game hours. */
export function updateWeather(state, dt) {
  if (state.paused || state.dead || !Number.isFinite(dt) || dt <= 0) return;
  const previousTime = Number.isFinite(state.time) ? state.time : 540;
  const previousPeriod = (Math.max(1, state.day || 1) - 1) * 4 + Math.floor(previousTime / 360);
  const total = Math.max(0, previousTime + dt * 5);
  state.day = Math.max(1, state.day || 1) + Math.floor(total / 1440);
  state.time = total % 1440;
  const weather = state.weather ||= { type: 'overcast', intensity: 0.25, temperature: 16 };
  const period = (state.day - 1) * 4 + Math.floor(state.time / 360);
  if (!Number.isFinite(weather.period)) weather.period = previousPeriod;
  if (period !== weather.period) {
    weather.period = period;
    const roll = random((state.seed || 74219) + period * 131);
    weather.type = roll < 0.29 ? 'clear' : roll < 0.64 ? 'overcast' : roll < 0.85 ? 'rain' : roll < 0.95 ? 'fog' : 'storm';
  }
  if (!TYPES.includes(weather.type)) weather.type = 'overcast';
  const variation = random((state.seed || 74219) + period * 83 + 912);
  const targets = { clear: 0.05, overcast: 0.25 + variation * 0.22, rain: 0.42 + variation * 0.42,
    fog: 0.38 + variation * 0.4, storm: 0.8 + variation * 0.2 };
  const mix = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / 7);
  const intensity = Number.isFinite(weather.intensity) ? weather.intensity : targets[weather.type];
  weather.intensity = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, intensity + (targets[weather.type] - intensity) * mix));
  const daytime = Math.sin((state.time / 1440 - 0.28) * Math.PI * 2);
  const temperature = 15 + daytime * 6 - (weather.type === 'rain' || weather.type === 'storm' ? 3 : 0);
  weather.temperature = (Number.isFinite(weather.temperature) ? weather.temperature : temperature) +
    (temperature - (Number.isFinite(weather.temperature) ? weather.temperature : temperature)) * mix;
  weather.daylight = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, (Math.sin((state.time / 1440 - 0.25) * Math.PI * 2) + 0.25) * 1.05));
  weather.wind = 0.2 + variation * (weather.type === 'storm' ? 0.8 : 0.4);
}

===== END FILE: systems/weather.js =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/README.md =====
# Deadfall verification

Run from the project root with Node 20+:

```sh
node --test tests/*.test.mjs ai/*.test.mjs save/*.test.mjs world/*.test.mjs render/*.test.mjs
node perf/benchmark.mjs
node build/optimized/build.mjs
```

The first command covers the engine/input lifecycle, ECS joins, worker
pathfinding/protocol, needs/weather/combat, world generation/streaming, game
actions, and save serialization/fallbacks. The save specialist's suite adds an
asynchronous IndexedDB test double. Browser smoke tests separately exercise
real browser storage and native module workers.

`fixtures.mjs` provides plain contract states. `worker-bridge.mjs` adapts browser
worker globals to an actual Node worker; pathfinding and simulation modules are
the production code. `game.test.mjs` runs the real Game update/actions with DOM
and saving hooks replaced; it does not assert that Canvas or real browser IDB
works. No tests silently skip missing production modules.

## Browser suite

Run on the dedicated browser machine, not the small main workspace. Install
`cloakbrowser` and `playwright-core` in its browser workspace and configure the
Solid relay on `127.0.0.1:1080`. Copy `browser-smoke.mjs` beside those packages.
Install Firefox/WebKit with Playwright when running those variants.

```sh
DEADFALL_URL=https://your-endpoint QA_BROWSER=chromium node browser-smoke.mjs
DEADFALL_URL=https://your-endpoint QA_BROWSER=firefox node browser-smoke.mjs
DEADFALL_URL=https://your-endpoint QA_BROWSER=webkit node browser-smoke.mjs
```

Artifacts default to `/workspace/browser/artifacts/<browser>/`: JSON report,
screenshots and an exported save. Each run uses a new isolated browser context.
`QA_OUTPUT` overrides the artifact directory. Chromium uses CloakBrowser via
the relay. Firefox/WebKit use Playwright's actual engines for compatibility
checks; WebKit on Linux is not a Safari/macOS hardware test.

The browser suite combines keyboard/UI checks with explicit state setup for
bounded scenarios such as fatal bleeding and consuming water. Frame timing is
reported for diagnosis and has no universal pass/fail threshold. Screenshots
must also be inspected for visual defects. See `RESULTS.md` for executed runs.

`browser-render-check.mjs` runs the bounded three-engine render/movement/zoom
check and captures daylight/night-rain views. `browser-profile.mjs` records
Chromium method timings, a CPU profile, the GPU feature report and an on/off/on
ground-cache comparison. Run these beside the same browser packages. The
temporary QA relay was removed after the recorded run; provision/configure a
new relay before repeating the scripts. Compact measured reports are retained
in `browser-results.json` and `../perf/profile-results.json` independently of
the larger screenshot/CPU-profile artifacts.

===== END FILE: tests/README.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/RESULTS.md =====
# Deadfall verification results

Executed 12 October 2026. **80/80 Node tests passed**, with no failures,
cancellations or skips. **14/14 gameplay checks passed in each of Chromium,
Firefox and WebKit**, with no page errors. The final renderer-only alpha crop
then passed a separate render, movement, zoom and night/rain check in all three
engines. No blocking functional issue remains from these executed scenarios.

## Reproducible evidence

- Node command: `node --test tests/*.test.mjs ai/*.test.mjs save/*.test.mjs world/*.test.mjs render/*.test.mjs`.
- Raw Node result: [node-results.tap](node-results.tap), 80 passes in 11.27 seconds.
- Browser scenarios: [browser-smoke.mjs](browser-smoke.mjs); compact reports: [browser-results.json](browser-results.json).
- Final rendering checks: [browser-render-check.mjs](browser-render-check.mjs).
- Performance harness: [browser-profile.mjs](browser-profile.mjs); measured output: [profile-results.json](../perf/profile-results.json).
- Package report: [build-results.json](../build/optimized/build-results.json).

Node 20 ran the engine/input lifecycle, ECS, actual worker protocol, AI,
needs/weather/combat, world streaming, game actions, persistence and renderer
suites. Game action tests use the production Game class with DOM/storage hooks;
Canvas tests use a validating context double. Browser checks cover real Canvas,
module workers, file downloads, file input and browser storage separately.

The browser machine was Debian 13 with 2 vCPUs and approximately 3.83 GiB RAM.
Runs were sequential, headless, and used isolated contexts through a Solid
relay. Chromium was CloakBrowser 146.0.7680.177; Firefox was 157.0; WebKit was
27.2. Chromium's device report identifies ANGLE SwiftShader, software Canvas,
and disabled hardware compositing/rasterization. WebKit on Linux is not a test
of Safari on Apple hardware.

## Browser behavior checked

Each engine executed the same 14-check harness: direct boot with a module
worker; E to search starter supplies and update the journal; keyboard movement
with collision; pause/resume; inventory and water consumption; inventory and
position after actual reload; projection inversion at several zooms; JSON
download; file-input import and reload; Space and aimed pointer combat;
keyboard barricading followed by reload; a 1280px layout/frame sample; death
and a fresh survivor; and absence of page errors.

Combat uses controlled actor placement, then real keyboard/pointer events.
Fatal bleeding, low thirst and import comparisons also use explicit scenario
setup. These bounded checks establish input-to-action integration; they are
not a long survival playthrough or a balance assessment. Node tests additionally
cover basic wooden barrier placement/dismantling and streamed world changes.

An earlier import test exposed an overwrite: a pagehide autosave could save the
old live state after importing a new snapshot. The coordinator added a guard
before awaiting the import save. The Node regression and actual file-input
roundtrip now pass in all three engines. Earlier pause-modal and restart
transient-state issues were also corrected and verified.

The 14-check gameplay runs include the ground cache, import guard and restart
cleanup. Only the renderer's prop-atlas alpha crop changed afterward. The final
three-engine smoke verified painting, movement, collision, keyboard zoom,
projection roundtrip, daylight and controlled night/rain without page errors.
Daylight and night screenshots were visually reviewed for populated scenes,
prop anchors, lighting and readable HUD. Full gameplay was not repeated solely
for that renderer-only crop.

## Measured performance

The gameplay harness sampled 90 frames at 1280px after combat and barricading,
before the last alpha crop. These are observations from software rendering:

| Browser | Mean frame time | p95 frame time | Approximate rate |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Chromium 146 | 47.35 ms | 66.60 ms | 21.1 fps |
| Firefox 157 | 55.42 ms | 67.32 ms | 18.0 fps |
| WebKit 27.2 | 119.54 ms | 156.00 ms | 8.4 fps |

The final alpha-cropped build received a separate same-view Chromium profile
at 1280×720, 60 frames per phase, with the static ground cache toggled:

| Final build phase | Mean frame time | p95 frame time |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Ground cache enabled | 22.05 ms | 33.40 ms |
| Ground cache disabled | 43.10 ms | 50.10 ms |
| Ground cache re-enabled | 23.42 ms | 33.40 ms |
| Canvas rendering disabled, diagnostic only | 16.70 ms | 16.80 ms |

The ground cache reduced measured frame time by approximately 46–49% against
the uncached path in this short comparison. The enabled samples correspond to
roughly 43–45 fps in this scene. This comparison isolates the cache toggle;
it does not isolate the alpha crop's contribution against earlier builds.

Instrumented method averages across the profile phases were 3.56 ms for render,
0.166 ms for simulation update and 0.119 ms for UI update. Lighting averaged
1.05 ms. These calls overlap and their averages must not be added together.
JavaScript timings exclude deferred raster/compositor work. The larger frame
intervals, software device report and no-Canvas diagnostic point to significant
rendering cost outside the measured JavaScript methods. Removing HUD backdrop
filter did not produce a reliable improvement in this short sample.

**There is no sustained 60-fps or GPU performance claim.** WebKit was especially
slow in the software environment. Representative GPU laptops, actual Safari,
mobile controls, long sessions, memory growth and large hordes remain untested.
The short samples are diagnostic evidence, not universal performance budgets.

## Deliverable and scope

The optimized packaging script validated the local module/worker/resource
graph and produced 39 runtime files: 347,614 raw bytes, 175,191 Gzip bytes and
164,274 Brotli bytes. Compression sidecars require server content negotiation.
The build preserves native ES modules and has no external runtime packages.

Screenshot evidence is retained in `tests/artifacts/render-<engine>/` as
`daylight.png`, `movement-zoom.png` and `night-rain.png`. Full gameplay artifacts
are in `tests/artifacts/final-<engine>/`; the raw CPU profile is in
`tests/artifacts/final-profile/`. These larger artifacts may be omitted from a
source archive; this report, the compact JSON reports and test scripts should
remain included.

This is a playable survival foundation. Basic barrier building and dismantling
are implemented. Advanced construction, a WebGL renderer, multiplayer and the
full systems of a commercial survival game remain future work described in
[ROADMAP.md](../ROADMAP.md).

After testing, the temporary QA relay `okevuU` was torn down on the browser
machine and deleted. No browser processes remained and port 1080 was closed.
The hosting machine and HTTP endpoint were preserved; `deadfall-web` remained
active and its local HTTP check returned 200. See [cleanup-results.json](cleanup-results.json).

===== END FILE: tests/RESULTS.md =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/ai.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { findPath, lineOfSight, blocked } from '../workers/pathfinding.js';
import { ZombieSimulation } from '../workers/simulation.js';
import { makeZombie } from './fixtures.mjs';

function grid(width = 12, height = 12) { return { x: 0, y: 0, width, height, cells: new Uint8Array(width * height) }; }
function snapshot(collision, zombies, extra = {}) {
  return { type: 'step', seq: 1, dt: .125, grid: collision, player: { x: 8.5, y: 5.5 }, zombies, time: 720, weather: { type: 'clear' }, noise: 0, ...extra };
}

test('A* navigates the only wall opening with legal steps and a reachable goal', () => {
  const g = grid();
  for (let y = 0; y < 12; y++) if (y !== 9) g.cells[y * 12 + 5] = 1;
  const path = findPath(g, 2.5, 5.5, 8.5, 5.5);
  assert.ok(path.length > 6, 'detour should be longer than a straight route');
  assert.deepEqual(path.at(-1), { x: 8.5, y: 5.5 });
  let previous = { x: 2.5, y: 5.5 };
  for (const point of path) {
    assert.equal(blocked(g, point.x, point.y), false);
    assert.ok(Math.hypot(point.x - previous.x, point.y - previous.y) <= Math.SQRT2 + 1e-8);
    assert.equal(lineOfSight(g, previous.x, previous.y, point.x, point.y), true);
    previous = point;
  }
  assert.ok(path.some(p => p.x === 5.5 && p.y === 9.5));
});

test('blocked corners prevent pathfinding and sight from cutting diagonally', () => {
  const g = grid(2, 2); g.cells[1] = 1; g.cells[2] = 1;
  assert.deepEqual(findPath(g, .5, .5, 1.5, 1.5), []);
  assert.equal(lineOfSight(g, .5, .5, 1.5, 1.5), false);
  assert.deepEqual(findPath(g, -.5, .5, 1.5, 1.5), []);
});

test('visible survivors trigger pursuit; a solid wall prevents sight and attacks', () => {
  const g = grid();
  const zombie = makeZombie({ x: 3.5, y: 5.5, dirX: 1, dirY: 0 });
  const open = new ZombieSimulation().step(snapshot(g, [zombie])).actors[0];
  assert.equal(open.mode, 'chase'); assert.ok(open.vx > 0); assert.equal(open.canAttack, false);
  for (let y = 0; y < 12; y++) g.cells[y * 12 + 5] = 1;
  const hidden = new ZombieSimulation().step(snapshot(g, [zombie])).actors[0];
  assert.notEqual(hidden.mode, 'chase'); assert.equal(hidden.canAttack, false);
});

test('noise creates an investigation target behind cover and repaths stay bounded', () => {
  const g = grid();
  for (let y = 1; y < 10; y++) g.cells[y * 12 + 5] = 1;
  const zombies = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => makeZombie({ id: i + 10, x: 2.5 + (i % 3) * .3, y: 3.5 + Math.floor(i / 3) * .4 }));
  const result = new ZombieSimulation().step(snapshot(g, zombies, { noise: 20 }));
  assert.ok(result.paths > 0 && result.paths <= 7);
  assert.ok(result.actors.some(z => Math.hypot(z.vx, z.vy) > 0));
  assert.ok(result.actors.every(z => !z.canAttack));
});

test('a real worker returns sequenced actor decisions and ignores unrelated messages', { timeout: 5000 }, async () => {
  const worker = new Worker(new URL('./worker-bridge.mjs', import.meta.url));
  try {
    const [ready] = await once(worker, 'message'); assert.equal(ready.type, 'ready');
    const reply = once(worker, 'message');
    worker.postMessage({ type: 'ignored' });
    worker.postMessage(snapshot(grid(), [makeZombie({ x: 7.8, y: 5.5 })], { seq: 42 }));
    const [result] = await reply;
    assert.equal(result.type, 'result'); assert.equal(result.seq, 42);
    assert.equal(result.actors[0].mode, 'attack');
    assert.equal(result.actors[0].canAttack, true);
  } finally { await worker.terminate(); }
});

===== END FILE: tests/ai.test.mjs =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/browser-profile.mjs =====
import { launch } from 'cloakbrowser';
import { writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
const directory = process.env.QA_OUTPUT || '/workspace/browser/artifacts/final-profile';
await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
const browser = await launch({ headless: true, proxy: 'socks5://127.0.0.1:1080', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--host-resolver-rules=MAP * ~NOTFOUND, EXCLUDE 127.0.0.1'] });
try {
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 720 } });
  await page.goto(process.env.DEADFALL_URL || 'https://deadfall-cwdknte.solid.site', { waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine);
  const client = await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
  const browserClient = await browser.newBrowserCDPSession();
  const systemInfo = await browserClient.send('SystemInfo.getInfo');
  await client.send('Profiler.enable'); await client.send('Profiler.start');
  const timings = await page.evaluate(async () => {
    const phases = [], sections = {};
    const wrap = (object, key, label) => {
      const original = object[key];
      object[key] = function (...args) { const t = performance.now(); const result = original.apply(this, args); (sections[label] ||= []).push(performance.now() - t); return result; };
    };
    wrap(game.renderer, 'render', 'render'); wrap(game, 'update', 'update'); wrap(game.ui, 'update', 'ui');
    wrap(game.world, 'getVisibleTiles', 'getVisibleTiles'); wrap(game.world, 'getProps', 'getProps');
    for (const key of ['_floorDetails', '_propShadows', '_windowShafts', '_blood', '_lighting', '_weather', '_vignettePass', '_attack', '_particles'])
      if (typeof game.renderer[key] === 'function') wrap(game.renderer, key, key);
    const frameSample = async label => {
      const frames = []; let last = performance.now();
      for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(now => { frames.push(now - last); last = now; resolve(); }));
      frames.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      phases.push({ label, meanMs: frames.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames.length, p95Ms: frames[56] });
    };
    await frameSample('baseline');
    game.renderer.cacheGround = false;
    await frameSample('ground-cache-disabled');
    game.renderer.cacheGround = true;
    await frameSample('ground-cache-reenabled');
    const style = document.createElement('style'); style.textContent = '*{backdrop-filter:none!important;-webkit-backdrop-filter:none!important}'; document.head.append(style);
    await frameSample('without-backdrop-filter');
    const originalRender = game.renderer.render; game.renderer.render = () => {};
    await frameSample('without-canvas-render'); game.renderer.render = originalRender;
    return { phases, sections: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(sections).map(([name, values]) => [name, { calls: values.length, meanMs: values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length, maxMs: Math.max(...values) }])) };
  });
  const { profile } = await client.send('Profiler.stop');
  await writeFile(`${directory}/cpu-profile.json`, JSON.stringify(profile));
  const samples = new Map();
  for (let i = 0; i < profile.samples.length; i++) samples.set(profile.samples[i], (samples.get(profile.samples[i]) || 0) + profile.timeDeltas[i]);
  const top = profile.nodes.map(node => ({ function: node.callFrame.functionName || '(anonymous)', url: node.callFrame.url, line: node.callFrame.lineNumber + 1, selfMs: (samples.get(node.id) || 0) / 1000 })).sort((a, b) => b.selfMs - a.selfMs).slice(0, 20);
  const report = { browser: browser.version(), gpu: { devices: systemInfo.gpu.devices, featureStatus: systemInfo.gpu.featureStatus }, timings, top };
  await writeFile(`${directory}/profile-summary.json`, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    game.engine.stop(); game.state.time = 1380;
    game.state.weather = { type: 'rain', intensity: .75, temperature: 12, daylight: .12, wind: .4 };
    game.renderer.render(game.state, 0); game.ui.update(game.state, true);
  });
  await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/night-rain.png` });
  console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
} finally { await browser.close(); }

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/browser-render-check.mjs =====
/** Small visual regression pass after renderer-only changes. */
import { launch } from 'cloakbrowser';
import { firefox, webkit } from 'playwright-core';
import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

const reports = [];
for (const name of ['chromium', 'firefox', 'webkit']) {
  const directory = `/workspace/browser/artifacts/render-${name}`;
  await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
  const browser = name === 'chromium'
    ? await launch({ headless: true, proxy: 'socks5://127.0.0.1:1080', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--host-resolver-rules=MAP * ~NOTFOUND, EXCLUDE 127.0.0.1'] })
    : await ({ firefox, webkit }[name]).launch({ headless: true, proxy: { server: 'socks5://127.0.0.1:1080' } });
  const report = { browser: name, version: browser.version(), passed: false, errors: [] };
  try {
    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
    page.on('pageerror', error => report.errors.push(error.message));
    await page.goto(process.env.DEADFALL_URL || 'https://deadfall-cwdknte.solid.site', { waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine?.frame > 2 && window.game?.state?.ai);
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/daylight.png` });
    const initial = await page.evaluate(() => ({ x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y, zoom: game.renderer.zoom, cache: game.renderer.cacheGround }));
    assert.equal(initial.cache, true);
    await page.keyboard.down('d'); await page.waitForTimeout(750); await page.keyboard.up('d');
    await page.waitForFunction(p => Math.hypot(game.state.player.x - p.x, game.state.player.y - p.y) > .05, initial);
    await page.keyboard.press('=');
    await page.waitForFunction(zoom => game.renderer.zoom > zoom, initial.zoom);
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/movement-zoom.png` });
    report.geometry = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const p = game.state.player, screen = game.renderer.worldToScreen(p.x, p.y), world = game.renderer.screenToWorld(screen.x, screen.y);
      return { error: Math.hypot(p.x - world.x, p.y - world.y), passable: game.canMove(p.x, p.y), zoom: game.renderer.zoom, x: p.x, y: p.y };
    });
    assert.ok(report.geometry.error < 1e-6); assert.equal(report.geometry.passable, true);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      game.engine.stop(); game.state.time = 1380;
      game.state.weather = { type: 'rain', intensity: .75, temperature: 12, daylight: .12, wind: .4 };
      game.renderer.render(game.state, 0); game.ui.update(game.state, true);
    });
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/night-rain.png` });
    assert.deepEqual(report.errors, []);
    report.passed = true;
  } catch (error) { report.errors.push(error.stack); }
  finally { await browser.close(); await writeFile(`${directory}/report.json`, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)); }
  reports.push(report);
}
console.log(JSON.stringify(reports, null, 2));
if (reports.some(r => !r.passed)) process.exitCode = 1;
