/** Copy to the dedicated browser machine's /workspace/browser and run there.
 * Requires dev-only cloakbrowser + playwright-core; never part of app runtime.
 * DEADFALL_URL=https://… node browser-smoke.mjs
 */
import { launch } from 'cloakbrowser';
import { firefox, webkit } from 'playwright-core';
import { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

const url = process.env.DEADFALL_URL || 'https://deadfall-cwdknte.solid.site';
const browserName = process.env.QA_BROWSER || 'chromium';
const directory = process.env.QA_OUTPUT || `/workspace/browser/artifacts/${browserName}`;
await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
const browser = browserName === 'chromium'
  ? await launch({ headless: true, proxy: 'socks5://127.0.0.1:1080', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--host-resolver-rules=MAP * ~NOTFOUND, EXCLUDE 127.0.0.1'] })
  : await ({ firefox, webkit }[browserName]).launch({ headless: true, proxy: { server: 'socks5://127.0.0.1:1080' } });
const report = { url, at: new Date().toISOString(), browserName, browser: browser.version(), checks: [], errors: [], workers: [] };
const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 }, deviceScaleFactor: 1 });
const page = await context.newPage();
page.on('pageerror', error => report.errors.push(error.message));
page.on('worker', worker => report.workers.push(worker.url()));
const check = async (name, run) => {
  try { const detail = await run(); report.checks.push({ name, passed: true, detail }); }
  catch (error) { report.checks.push({ name, passed: false, error: error.message }); }
};
try {
  await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle', timeout: 45000 });
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.state && window.game?.renderer, null, { timeout: 20000 });
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine?.frame > 2 && window.game?.state?.ai, null, { timeout: 20000 });
  await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/01-first-play.png` });

  await check('direct boot and browser worker', async () => {
    assert.equal(await page.locator('#game-canvas').count(), 1);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => game.state.ai?.worker), true);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.game.state.dead), false);
    return { workers: report.workers };
  });

  await check('E searches starter supplies and updates the journal', async () => {
    const before = await page.evaluate(() => ({ looted: game.state.objectives.looted, items: game.state.player.inventory.reduce((n, i) => n + i.qty, 0) }));
    await page.keyboard.press('e');
    await page.waitForFunction(n => game.state.objectives.looted > n, before.looted);
    const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({ looted: game.state.objectives.looted, items: game.state.player.inventory.reduce((n, i) => n + i.qty, 0) }));
    assert.equal(after.looted, before.looted + 1); assert.ok(after.items > before.items);
    assert.ok(await page.locator('#hud-objectives li.complete').count() >= 1);
    return { before, after };
  });

  await check('keyboard movement and collision', async () => {
    await page.evaluate(() => { window.game.state.paused = false; });
    const before = await page.evaluate(() => ({ x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y }));
    await page.keyboard.down('d'); await page.waitForTimeout(650); await page.keyboard.up('d');
    const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({ x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y, passable: game.canMove(game.state.player.x, game.state.player.y) }));
    assert.ok(Math.hypot(after.x - before.x, after.y - before.y) > .05, 'keyboard should move survivor');
    assert.equal(after.passable, true);
    return { before, after };
  });

  await check('pause freezes simulation and resumes', async () => {
    await page.keyboard.press('p');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => game.state.paused && game.ui.panel === 'pause');
    const before = await page.evaluate(() => ({ paused: game.state.paused, time: game.state.time, elapsed: game.state.elapsed }));
    assert.equal(before.paused, true);
    await page.waitForTimeout(350);
    const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({ time: game.state.time, elapsed: game.state.elapsed }));
    assert.equal(after.time, before.time); assert.equal(after.elapsed, before.elapsed);
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/02-pause.png` });
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => !game.state.paused && game.ui.panel === null);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => game.state.paused), false);
  });

  await check('inventory dialog and consumable action', async () => {
    await page.keyboard.press('i');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => game.state.paused && game.ui.panel === 'inventory');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => game.state.paused), true);
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/03-inventory.png` });
    const result = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      game.state.player.thirst = 40;
      const before = game.count('water');
      await game.action('use', 'water');
      return { before, after: game.count('water'), thirst: game.state.player.thirst };
    });
    assert.equal(result.after, result.before - 1); assert.equal(result.thirst, 78);
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => !game.state.paused && game.ui.panel === null);
    return result;
  });

  await check('persistent inventory and position survive reload', async () => {
    const saved = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      game.state.paused = true;
      // Wait for any action-triggered save to finish before capturing this one.
      while (game.saveBusy) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 30));
      await game.save();
      return { x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y, water: game.count('water') };
    });
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine?.frame > 2 && window.game?.state?.ai);
    const loaded = await page.evaluate(() => ({ x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y, water: game.count('water') }));
    assert.deepEqual(loaded, saved);
    return loaded;
  });

  await check('zoom projection remains invertible', async () => {
    const errors = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const r = game.renderer, point = game.state.player, results = [];
      for (const zoom of [.7, 1, 1.5]) {
        r.setZoom(zoom); r.render(game.state, 0);
        const screen = r.worldToScreen(point.x, point.y), world = r.screenToWorld(screen.x, screen.y);
        results.push(Math.hypot(world.x - point.x, world.y - point.y));
      }
      r.setZoom(1); return results;
    });
    assert.ok(errors.every(n => n < 1e-6)); return errors;
  });

  await check('save export downloads a valid versioned snapshot', async () => {
    const event = page.waitForEvent('download');
    await page.evaluate(() => game.action('export'));
    const download = await event;
    await download.saveAs(`${directory}/exported-save.json`);
    const envelope = JSON.parse(await readFile(`${directory}/exported-save.json`, 'utf8'));
    assert.equal(envelope.format, 'deadfall-save'); assert.equal(envelope.version, 1);
    assert.equal(envelope.data.objectives.looted, 1);
    return { name: download.suggestedFilename(), health: envelope.data.player.health };
  });

  await check('file import restores exported inventory, position and health', async () => {
    const envelope = JSON.parse(await readFile(`${directory}/exported-save.json`, 'utf8'));
    await page.evaluate(() => { game.state.player.health = 17; game.state.player.inventory = []; });
    await Promise.all([
      page.waitForNavigation({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' }),
      page.locator('#save-import-input').setInputFiles(`${directory}/exported-save.json`),
    ]);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine?.frame > 2 && window.game?.state?.ai);
    const restored = await page.evaluate(() => ({ x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y, hp: game.state.player.health, inventory: game.state.player.inventory }));
    assert.equal(restored.x, envelope.data.player.x); assert.equal(restored.y, envelope.data.player.y);
    assert.equal(restored.hp, envelope.data.player.health); assert.deepEqual(restored.inventory, envelope.data.player.inventory);
    return restored;
  });

  await check('Space and aimed pointer attacks damage a nearby infected', async () => {
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      const p = game.state.player; p.x = 7.5; p.y = 7.5; p.dirX = 1; p.dirY = 0;
      p.stamina = 100; p.attackCooldown = 0; p.equipped = 'bat'; game.state.paused = false;
      game.state.zombies.push({ id: 999999, x: 8.5, y: 7.5, health: 100, mode: 'idle', dirX: -1, dirY: 0, attackCooldown: 10 });
    });
    await page.keyboard.press('Space');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => game.state.zombies.find(z => z.id === 999999)?.health < 100);
    const keyboardHealth = await page.evaluate(() => game.state.zombies.find(z => z.id === 999999).health);
    const point = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const z = game.state.zombies.find(z => z.id === 999999); z.health = 100; z.x = 8.5; z.y = 7.5;
      z.knockbackX = z.knockbackY = 0; game.state.player.attackCooldown = 0; game.state.player.stamina = 100;
      game.state.player.dirX = -1; game.state.player.dirY = 0;
      return game.renderer.worldToScreen(z.x, z.y);
    });
    await page.mouse.move(point.x, point.y); await page.mouse.down();
    try { await page.waitForFunction(() => game.state.zombies.find(z => z.id === 999999)?.health < 100); }
    finally { await page.mouse.up(); }
    const pointerHealth = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const z = game.state.zombies.find(z => z.id === 999999), health = z.health;
      z.health = 0; z.canAttack = false; return health;
    });
    assert.ok(keyboardHealth < 100 && pointerHealth < 100);
    return { keyboardHealth, pointerHealth };
  });

  await check('keyboard barricade changes collision and persists through browser reload', async () => {
    const id = await page.evaluate(() => {
      game.state.player.x = 11.3; game.state.player.y = 7.5; game.state.paused = false;
      return game.nearestOpening().id;
    });
    await page.keyboard.press('b');
    await page.waitForFunction(id => game.world.getProps(12, 7, 2).find(p => p.id === id)?.barricaded > 0, id);
    await page.evaluate(async () => {
      game.state.paused = true;
      while (game.saveBusy) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 30));
      await game.save();
    });
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.game?.engine?.frame > 2 && window.game?.state?.ai);
    const restored = await page.evaluate(id => ({
      barricaded: game.world.getProps(12, 7, 2).find(p => p.id === id)?.barricaded,
      blocked: game.world.isBlocked(12.5, 7.5), objectives: game.state.objectives.barricaded,
    }), id);
    assert.equal(restored.barricaded, 1); assert.equal(restored.blocked, true); assert.equal(restored.objectives, 1);
    return restored;
  });

  await check('1280px viewport and frame sample', async () => {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1280, height: 720 });
    await page.waitForTimeout(250);
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/04-1280x720.png` });
    const sample = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      game.state.paused = false;
      const frames = []; let previous = performance.now();
      for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(now => { frames.push(now - previous); previous = now; resolve(); }));
      frames.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      return { meanMs: frames.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames.length, p95Ms: frames[Math.ceil(frames.length * .95) - 1], samples: frames.length, width: innerWidth, documentWidth: document.documentElement.scrollWidth };
    });
    assert.equal(sample.documentWidth, sample.width, 'page should not overflow horizontally'); return sample;
  });

  await check('death overlay can restart with a fresh survivor', async () => {
    await page.evaluate(() => { game.state.player.health = .001; game.state.player.bleeding = 100; game.state.paused = false; });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => game.state.dead && game.ui.panel === 'death');
    await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/05-death.png` });
    await page.evaluate(() => game.action('restart'));
    await page.waitForTimeout(150);
    const fresh = await page.evaluate(() => ({ dead: game.state.dead, hp: game.state.player.health, panel: game.ui.panel, looted: game.state.objectives.looted, x: game.state.player.x, y: game.state.player.y }));
    assert.equal(fresh.dead, false); assert.equal(fresh.hp, 100); assert.equal(fresh.panel, null);
    assert.equal(fresh.looted, 0); assert.equal(fresh.x, 7.5); assert.equal(fresh.y, 7.5);
    return fresh;
  });

  await check('runtime errors absent', () => assert.deepEqual(report.errors, []));
} catch (error) {
  report.errors.push(error.stack);
  await page.screenshot({ path: `${directory}/failure.png` }).catch(() => {});
} finally {
  await writeFile(`${directory}/browser-report.json`, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
  await browser.close();
}
console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
if (report.errors.length || report.checks.some(check => !check.passed)) process.exitCode = 1;

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/ecs.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { ECS } from '../ecs/world.js';

test('ECS joins requested components, preserves falsy values, and removes entities', () => {
  const ecs = new ECS();
  const player = ecs.create({ position: { x: 1, y: 2 }, health: 0 });
  const prop = ecs.create({ position: { x: 3, y: 4 } });
  assert.deepEqual([...ecs.query('health', 'position')], [[player, 0, { x: 1, y: 2 }]]);
  ecs.add(prop, 'health', 10); assert.equal([...ecs.query('health', 'position')].length, 2);
  ecs.remove(player); assert.equal(ecs.get(player, 'position'), undefined);
  ecs.remove(prop, 'health'); assert.deepEqual([...ecs.query('health')], []);
  assert.equal(ecs.has(prop), true);
});

===== END FILE: tests/ecs.test.mjs =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/engine-lifecycle.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Engine } from '../engine/loop.js';
import { Input } from '../engine/input.js';
import { ECS } from '../ecs/world.js';

function animation(t) {
  const previous = { requestAnimationFrame: globalThis.requestAnimationFrame, cancelAnimationFrame: globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame, document: globalThis.document };
  const requests = new Map(); let id = 0;
  const doc = new EventTarget(); doc.hidden = false;
  globalThis.document = doc;
  globalThis.requestAnimationFrame = callback => { requests.set(++id, callback); return id; };
  globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame = id => requests.delete(id);
  t.after(() => Object.assign(globalThis, previous));
  return { requests, doc, tick(time) { const entry = requests.entries().next().value; assert.ok(entry); requests.delete(entry[0]); entry[1](time); } };
}

test('two seconds of movement is identical at 30, 60 and 144Hz', t => {
  const frames = animation(t);
  for (const hz of [30, 60, 144]) {
    let x = 0, count = 0;
    const engine = new Engine({ update: dt => { x += dt * 2; count++; }, render: () => {} });
    engine.start();
    for (let i = 0; i <= hz * 2; i++) frames.tick(i * 1000 / hz);
    assert.equal(count, 120);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(x - 4) < 1e-9);
    engine.destroy();
    assert.equal(frames.requests.size, 0);
  }
});

test('hidden tabs skip simulation and resume without offline drain', t => {
  const frames = animation(t); let updates = 0;
  const engine = new Engine({ update: () => updates++, render: () => {} });
  engine.start(); frames.tick(0); frames.tick(1000 / 60);
  assert.equal(updates, 1);
  frames.doc.hidden = true; frames.doc.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'));
  frames.tick(15000);
  assert.equal(updates, 1);
  frames.doc.hidden = false; frames.doc.dispatchEvent(new Event('visibilitychange'));
  frames.tick(30000); assert.equal(updates, 1);
  frames.tick(30000 + 1000 / 60); assert.equal(updates, 2);
  engine.destroy();
});

test('stop from a callback and error callbacks leave no scheduled work', t => {
  const frames = animation(t); let renders = 0;
  const engine = new Engine({ update: () => engine.stop(), render: () => renders++ });
  engine.start(); frames.tick(0); frames.tick(1000);
  assert.equal(renders, 1); assert.equal(frames.requests.size, 0);
  engine.destroy();
  let failure;
  const failing = new Engine({ update: () => { throw new Error('expected'); }, render: () => {}, onError: error => { failure = error; } });
  failing.start(); frames.tick(0); frames.tick(17);
  assert.equal(failure.message, 'expected'); assert.equal(failing.running, false);
  assert.equal(frames.requests.size, 0); failing.destroy();
});

function fakeDOM() {
  const win = new EventTarget(), doc = new EventTarget(), canvas = new EventTarget();
  doc.defaultView = win; canvas.ownerDocument = doc;
  canvas.style = { touchAction: 'pan-x' };
  const attributes = new Map([['tabindex', '3']]);
  canvas.getAttribute = key => attributes.get(key) ?? null;
  canvas.setAttribute = (key, value) => attributes.set(key, value);
  canvas.removeAttribute = key => attributes.delete(key);
  canvas.getBoundingClientRect = () => ({ left: 10, top: 20, width: 800, height: 600 });
  canvas.focus = () => {};
  const emit = (target, type, props = {}) => {
    const event = new Event(type, { cancelable: true });
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(props)) Object.defineProperty(event, key, { value });
    target.dispatchEvent(event); return event;
  };
  return { win, doc, canvas, emit };
}

test('Z sneak remains available while browser modifier shortcuts are ignored', () => {
  const { win, canvas, emit } = fakeDOM(); const input = new Input(canvas);
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'z', code: 'KeyZ' });
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'w', code: 'KeyW' });
  assert.equal(input.keys.has('z'), true);
  assert.deepEqual(input.movement(), { x: 0, y: -1 });
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'r', code: 'KeyR', ctrlKey: true });
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'i', code: 'KeyI', altKey: true });
  assert.equal(input.consume('r'), false);
  assert.equal(input.consume('i'), false);
  emit(win, 'keyup', { key: 'w', code: 'KeyW' });
  emit(win, 'keyup', { key: 'z', code: 'KeyZ' });
  assert.equal(input.keys.size, 0); input.destroy();
});

test('holding a shortcut cannot repeat its edge and editing focus releases held controls', () => {
  const { win, doc, canvas, emit } = fakeDOM(); const input = new Input(canvas);
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'i', code: 'KeyI' });
  assert.equal(input.consume('i'), true);
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'i', code: 'KeyI', repeat: true });
  assert.equal(input.consume('i'), false);
  emit(doc, 'focusin', { target: { isContentEditable: true } });
  assert.equal(input.keys.size, 0);
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'w', target: { isContentEditable: true } });
  assert.deepEqual(input.movement(), { x: 0, y: 0 }); input.destroy();
});

test('cancelled pointers stop attacks; wheel and context menu handling stay on canvas', () => {
  const { win, canvas, emit } = fakeDOM(); const input = new Input(canvas);
  emit(canvas, 'pointerdown', { pointerId: 2, button: 0, buttons: 1, clientX: 110, clientY: 220 });
  assert.equal(input.consume('mouse0'), true); assert.equal(input.pointer.down, true);
  emit(win, 'pointercancel', { pointerId: 2 }); assert.equal(input.pointer.down, false);
  assert.equal(emit(canvas, 'wheel', { deltaY: 50 }).defaultPrevented, true);
  assert.equal(input.consumeWheel(), 1); assert.equal(input.consumeWheel(), 0);
  assert.equal(emit(canvas, 'contextmenu').defaultPrevented, true);
  input.destroy();
  assert.equal(canvas.style.touchAction, 'pan-x'); assert.equal(canvas.getAttribute('tabindex'), '3');
  emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'w' }); assert.equal(input.keys.size, 0);
  assert.equal(emit(canvas, 'contextmenu').defaultPrevented, false);
});

test('ECS keeps actor references, intersects sparse components, and removes all traces', () => {
  const world = new ECS(); const player = { x: 7.5, y: 7.5, health: 100 };
  const survivor = world.create({ survivor: player, position: player });
  const zombie = world.add('z1', { zombie: { health: 80 }, position: { x: 8, y: 8 } });
  world.add('only-health', 'health', undefined);
  assert.equal(world.has('only-health', 'health'), true);
  assert.deepEqual([...world.query('zombie', 'position')].map(row => row[0]), [zombie]);
  player.x += 1; assert.equal(world.get(survivor, 'position').x, 8.5);
  world.get(zombie, 'zombie').health = 0;
  for (const [id, actor] of world.query('zombie')) if (actor.health <= 0) world.remove(id);
  assert.equal(world.has(zombie), false); assert.equal(world.get(zombie, 'position'), undefined);
  assert.equal([...world.query('zombie')].length, 0);
  assert.equal(world.size, 2);
  world.clear(); assert.equal(world.size, 0); assert.equal(world.components.size, 0);
  assert.notEqual(world.create(), survivor);
});

===== END FILE: tests/engine-lifecycle.test.mjs =====


===== BEGIN FILE: tests/engine.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Engine } from '../engine/loop.js';
import { Input } from '../engine/input.js';

test('fixed simulation step caps catch-up after a long frame and renders a valid alpha', () => {
  const requests = new Map(); let requestId = 0;
  const oldRAF = globalThis.requestAnimationFrame, oldCancel = globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame;
  globalThis.requestAnimationFrame = cb => { requests.set(++requestId, cb); return requestId; };
  globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame = id => requests.delete(id);
  const updates = [], renders = [];
  const engine = new Engine({ update: dt => updates.push(dt), render: alpha => renders.push(alpha) });
  const tick = time => { const [id, cb] = requests.entries().next().value; requests.delete(id); cb(time); };
  try {
    engine.start(); engine.start();
    assert.equal(requests.size, 1, 'repeated start must not create two loops');
    tick(0); tick(1000 / 60); tick(10000);
    assert.equal(updates.length, 6, 'one normal step plus at most five catch-up steps');
    assert.ok(updates.every(dt => dt === 1 / 60));
    assert.ok(renders.every(alpha => alpha >= 0 && alpha <= 1));
    engine.stop(); assert.equal(requests.size, 0);
  } finally { engine.destroy(); globalThis.requestAnimationFrame = oldRAF; globalThis.cancelAnimationFrame = oldCancel; }
});

function fakeDOM() {
  const win = new EventTarget(), doc = new EventTarget(), canvas = new EventTarget();
  doc.defaultView = win; canvas.ownerDocument = doc;
  canvas.style = { touchAction: 'auto' };
  const attributes = new Map();
  canvas.getAttribute = key => attributes.get(key) ?? null;
  canvas.setAttribute = (key, value) => attributes.set(key, value);
  canvas.removeAttribute = key => attributes.delete(key);
  canvas.getBoundingClientRect = () => ({ left: 10, top: 20, width: 800, height: 600 });
  canvas.focus = () => {};
  const emit = (target, type, props = {}) => {
    const event = new Event(type, { cancelable: true });
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(props)) Object.defineProperty(event, key, { value });
    target.dispatchEvent(event); return event;
  };
  return { win, doc, canvas, emit };
}

test('input normalizes diagonal speed, preserves tap edges and clears keys after blur', () => {
  const { win, canvas, emit } = fakeDOM(); const input = new Input(canvas);
  try {
    emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'W', code: 'KeyW' });
    emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'd', code: 'KeyD' });
    assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.hypot(...Object.values(input.movement())) - 1) < 1e-9);
    emit(win, 'keyup', { key: 'W', code: 'KeyW' });
    assert.equal(input.consume('w'), true);
    assert.equal(input.consume('w'), false);
    emit(win, 'blur'); assert.deepEqual(input.movement(), { x: 0, y: 0 });
  } finally { input.destroy(); }
});

test('typing is ignored and pointer uses CSS coordinates rather than backing pixels', () => {
  const { win, canvas, emit } = fakeDOM(); const input = new Input(canvas);
  try {
    emit(win, 'keydown', { key: 'i', target: { closest: () => ({}) } });
    assert.equal(input.consume('i'), false);
    emit(canvas, 'pointerdown', { pointerId: 1, button: 0, buttons: 1, clientX: 110, clientY: 220 });
    assert.equal(input.pointer.x, 100); assert.equal(input.pointer.y, 200);
    assert.equal(input.pointer.down, true);
    emit(win, 'pointerup', { pointerId: 1, buttons: 0, clientX: 110, clientY: 220 });
    assert.equal(input.pointer.down, false);
  } finally { input.destroy(); }
});

export function makeState(overrides = {}) {
  const state = {
    version: 1, seed: 74219, time: 540, day: 1, elapsed: 0, paused: false, dead: false,
    weather: { type: 'overcast', intensity: .25, temperature: 16 },
    player: { id: 1, x: 7.5, y: 7.5, dirX: 1, dirY: 0, health: 100, hunger: 86, thirst: 82, energy: 100, stamina: 100, bleeding: 0, infection: 0,
      inventory: [{ id: 'bat', qty: 1 }, { id: 'water', qty: 2 }, { id: 'beans', qty: 2 }, { id: 'bandage', qty: 2 }, { id: 'plank', qty: 4 }, { id: 'nails', qty: 8 }, { id: 'hammer', qty: 1 }], equipped: 'bat', skills: { strength: 0, fitness: 0, crafting: 0 }, kills: 0 },
    zombies: [], particles: [], messages: [], worldChanges: {},
    objectives: { looted: 0, crafted: 0, barricaded: 0 }, stats: { distance: 0 }, settings: { sound: true }, noise: 0,
  };
  return Object.assign(state, overrides);
}

export function makeZombie(overrides = {}) {
  return { id: 2, x: 8.2, y: 7.5, health: 100, mode: 'idle', dirX: -1, dirY: 0, attackCooldown: 0, ...overrides };
}

export const emptyWorld = { isBlocked: () => false, getNearby: () => [], getTile: (x, y) => ({ x: Math.floor(x), y: Math.floor(y), type: 'grass', blocked: false }) };

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/game.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Game } from '../game.js';
import { World } from '../world/generator.js';
import { AIController } from '../ai/controller.js';
import { serialize, loadGame } from '../save/indexedDB.js';

function gameFixture() {
  const game = new Game();
  game.state.settings.sound = false;
  game.world = new World(game.state.seed); game.world.update(7.5, 7.5);
  game.initEntities();
  game.ai = new AIController(game.world);
  game.input = { keys: new Set(), pointer: { down: false }, consume: () => false, consumeWheel: () => 0, movement: () => ({ x: 0, y: 0 }), clear() {} };
  game.ui = { update() {}, notify() {}, toggle() {} };
  game.save = async () => { game.saved = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(game.state)); };
  for (let x = -1; x <= 1; x++) for (let y = -1; y <= 1; y++) game.spawned.add(`${x},${y}`);
  return game;
}

test('game interaction loots starter container once and crafting consumes the required materials', async () => {
  const game = gameFixture(); const prop = game.findLoot(); assert.ok(prop);
  const contents = structuredClone(prop.loot), before = Object.fromEntries(contents.map(i => [i.id, game.count(i.id)]));
  await game.action('interact');
  for (const item of contents) assert.equal(game.count(item.id), before[item.id] + item.qty);
  assert.equal(prop.looted, true); assert.equal(game.state.objectives.looted, 1);
  await game.action('loot'); assert.equal(game.state.objectives.looted, 1);
  game.addItem('cloth', 2);
  const cloth = game.count('cloth'), bandages = game.count('bandage');
  await game.action('craft', 'bandage');
  assert.equal(game.count('cloth'), cloth - 2); assert.equal(game.count('bandage'), bandages + 1);
  assert.equal(game.state.objectives.crafted, 1);
  game.ai.destroy();
});

test('real game movement stops at a closed exit and passes after opening it', async () => {
  const game = gameFixture(); game.state.player.x = 11.5;
  game.input.movement = () => ({ x: Math.SQRT1_2, y: Math.SQRT1_2 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) game.update(1 / 60);
  assert.ok(game.state.player.x < 11.8, `closed-door position ${game.state.player.x}`);
  assert.equal(game.canMove(game.state.player.x, game.state.player.y), true);
  await game.action('interact');
  assert.equal(game.world.isBlocked(12.5, 7.5), false);
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) game.update(1 / 60);
  assert.ok(game.state.player.x > 12.8, `opened-door position ${game.state.player.x}`);
  assert.ok(game.state.stats.distance > 1);
  game.ai.destroy();
});

test('barricade consumes materials and stays collidable after snapshot restore', async () => {
  const game = gameFixture(); game.state.player.x = 11.4;
  const planks = game.count('plank'), nails = game.count('nails');
  await game.action('barricade');
  const opening = game.nearestOpening(); assert.equal(opening.barricaded, 1);
  assert.equal(game.count('plank'), planks - 1); assert.equal(game.count('nails'), nails - 4);
  assert.equal(game.state.objectives.barricaded, 1);
  const fresh = new World(game.state.seed, game.world.serialize());
  assert.equal(fresh.isBlocked(12.5, 7.5), true);
  await game.action('barricade'); assert.equal(opening.barricaded, 0); assert.equal(game.count('plank'), planks);
  game.ai.destroy();
});

test('paused game cannot move or deplete needs; fatal needs are saved once', () => {
  const game = gameFixture(); game.state.paused = true;
  game.input.movement = () => ({ x: 1, y: 0 });
  const before = structuredClone(game.state);
  game.update(1 / 60); assert.deepEqual(game.state, before);
  game.state.paused = false; game.input.movement = () => ({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  game.state.player.health = .001; game.state.player.bleeding = 100;
  let saves = 0; game.save = async () => { saves++; };
  game.update(1 / 60); assert.equal(game.state.dead, true); assert.equal(saves, 1);
  game.update(1 / 60); assert.equal(saves, 1);
  game.ai.destroy();
});

test('building and dismantling update resources, collision and the persistent world', async () => {
  const game = gameFixture(), planks = game.count('plank'), nails = game.count('nails');
  await game.action('build');
  const barrier = game.world.getNearby(7.5, 7.5, 2).find(p => p.built);
  assert.ok(barrier); assert.equal(game.world.isBlocked(8.5, 7.5), true);
  assert.equal(game.count('plank'), planks - 2); assert.equal(game.count('nails'), nails - 4);
  let restored = new World(game.state.seed, game.world.serialize());
  assert.equal(restored.isBlocked(8.5, 7.5), true);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { game.state.player.attackCooldown = 0; await game.action('dismantle'); }
  assert.equal(game.world.isBlocked(8.5, 7.5), false);
  assert.equal(game.count('plank'), planks - 1);
  restored = new World(game.state.seed, game.world.serialize());
  assert.equal(restored.isBlocked(8.5, 7.5), false);
  game.ai.destroy();
});

test('import cannot be overwritten by the old survivor during pagehide autosave', async () => {
  const game = gameFixture(), storage = new Map();
  const oldStorage = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'localStorage');
  const oldLocation = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'location');
  let pagehideSave;
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', { configurable: true, value: {
    getItem: key => storage.get(key) ?? null, setItem: (key, value) => storage.set(key, value),
  } });
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'location', { configurable: true, value: { reload: () => { pagehideSave = game.save(); } } });
  try {
    const imported = JSON.stringify(serialize(game.state));
    game.state.player.health = 17; game.state.player.inventory = [];
    game.save = Game.prototype.save.bind(game);
    await game.action('import', { size: imported.length, text: async () => imported });
    await pagehideSave;
    assert.equal((await loadGame()).player.health, 100);
    assert.ok((await loadGame()).player.inventory.length > 0);
  } finally {
    if (oldStorage) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', oldStorage); else delete globalThis.localStorage;
    if (oldLocation) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'location', oldLocation); else delete globalThis.location;
    game.ai.destroy();
  }
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PerfMonitor } from '../perf/monitor.js';

test('frame samples remain bounded and report percentiles', () => {
  const m = new PerfMonitor({ capacity: 4, budgetMs: 20 });
  for (const duration of [999, 10, 20, 30, 40]) m.record(duration);
  const s = m.snapshot();
  assert.equal(s.samples, 4);
  assert.equal(s.meanMs, 25);
  assert.equal(s.fps, 40);
  assert.equal(s.p95Ms, 40);
  assert.equal(s.overBudgetPercent, 50);
});

test('tab suspension is ignored and named timings can be reset', () => {
  let now = 0;
  const m = new PerfMonitor({ now: () => now });
  m.frame(0); m.frame(16); m.frame(5016); m.frame(5032);
  assert.equal(m.snapshot().samples, 2);
  m.begin('render'); now = 5; m.end('render');
  assert.equal(m.snapshot().sections.render.meanMs, 5);
  m.reset();
  assert.equal(m.snapshot().samples, 0);
  assert.deepEqual(m.snapshot().sections, {});
});

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/save.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { serialize } from '../save/serializer.js';
import { deserialize } from '../save/deserializer.js';
import { saveGame, loadGame, clearSave, importSave, getSaveStatus, LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY } from '../save/indexedDB.js';
import { makeState, makeZombie } from './fixtures.mjs';

test('save round-trip retains persistent world, actors, skills and objectives without aliases', () => {
  const state = makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie()], worldChanges: { 'door:12,7': { open: true, barricaded: 0 }, 'crate:6,7': { loot: [], looted: true } }, spawnedChunks: ['0,0', '1,0'] });
  state.player.skills.crafting = 23;
  state.objectives.looted = 3;
  state.particles = [{ x: 1, life: .2 }]; state.messages = [{ text: 'temporary', at: 1 }];
  state.render = { fps: 60 }; state.nearby = { id: 'door:12,7' }; state.paused = true;
  const before = JSON.stringify(state), envelope = serialize(state), loaded = deserialize(JSON.stringify(envelope));
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(state), before, 'serialization must not mutate live state');
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.player, state.player);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.zombies, state.zombies);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.worldChanges, state.worldChanges);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.spawnedChunks, state.spawnedChunks);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.objectives, state.objectives);
  for (const key of ['particles', 'messages', 'render', 'nearby', 'paused']) assert.equal(key in envelope.data, false);
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.particles, []); assert.deepEqual(loaded.messages, []); assert.equal(loaded.paused, false);
  loaded.player.inventory[0].qty = 99;
  assert.equal(state.player.inventory[0].qty, 1);
});

test('invalid JSON, future versions, nonfinite health and duplicate actors are rejected', () => {
  assert.throws(() => deserialize('{broken'), /JSON/);
  const future = serialize(makeState()); future.version = 99;
  assert.throws(() => deserialize(future), /version 99/i);
  const malformed = makeState(); malformed.player.health = NaN;
  assert.throws(() => serialize(malformed), /invalid number/i);
  assert.throws(() => serialize(makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie(), makeZombie()] })), /unique/);
  const polluted = JSON.stringify(makeState()).replace('"stats":{', '"stats":{"__proto__":{},');
  assert.throws(() => deserialize(polluted), /unsafe property/i);
});

test('localStorage fallback snapshots eagerly, orders saves, and clears durably', async () => {
  const store = new Map();
  const previous = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'localStorage');
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', { configurable: true, value: {
    getItem: key => store.get(key) ?? null, setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, value), removeItem: key => store.delete(key),
  } });
  try {
    assert.equal(await loadGame(), null);
    const state = makeState(); state.player.health = 81;
    const pending = saveGame(state); state.player.health = 12; await pending;
    assert.equal((await loadGame()).player.health, 81);
    assert.equal(getSaveStatus().backend, 'localStorage');
    state.player.health = 55; const first = saveGame(state);
    state.player.health = 62; const second = saveGame(state);
    await Promise.all([first, second]); assert.equal((await loadGame()).player.health, 62);
    await clearSave(); assert.equal(await loadGame(), null);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(store.get(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY)).deleted, true);
    await Promise.all([clearSave(), saveGame(makeState())]);
    assert.equal((await loadGame()).player.health, 100, 'a later new game supersedes a clear marker');
    store.set(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, '{broken');
    await assert.rejects(loadGame(), /JSON/);
  } finally {
    if (previous) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'localStorage', previous);
    else delete globalThis.localStorage;
  }
});

test('storage failures are reported and imported files are validated before use', async () => {
  await assert.rejects(saveGame(makeState()), /storage is unavailable/i);
  assert.ok(getSaveStatus().error);
  const file = { size: 100, text: async () => JSON.stringify(serialize(makeState())) };
  assert.equal((await importSave(file)).player.health, 100);
  await assert.rejects(importSave({ size: 21 * 1024 * 1024, text: async () => '{}' }), /20 MB/);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { updateNeeds } from '../systems/needs.js';
import { updateWeather } from '../systems/weather.js';
import { attack, updateCombat } from '../systems/combat.js';
import { makeState, makeZombie, emptyWorld } from './fixtures.mjs';

test('needs deplete with time, sprinting costs more, and depleted needs cause bounded damage', () => {
  const idle = makeState(), sprint = makeState();
  sprint.player.moving = true; sprint.player.running = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { updateNeeds(idle, 1); updateNeeds(sprint, 1); }
  assert.ok(idle.player.thirst < 82 && idle.player.hunger < 86);
  assert.ok(sprint.player.thirst < idle.player.thirst);
  assert.ok(sprint.player.stamina < idle.player.stamina);
  const depleted = makeState(); depleted.player.hunger = depleted.player.thirst = 0; depleted.player.bleeding = 40;
  updateNeeds(depleted, 10); assert.ok(depleted.player.health < 100);
  updateNeeds(depleted, 100000); assert.equal(depleted.player.health, 0); assert.equal(depleted.dead, true);
  for (const k of ['health', 'hunger', 'thirst', 'energy', 'stamina', 'bleeding', 'infection']) assert.ok(depleted.player[k] >= 0 && depleted.player[k] <= 100);
});

test('paused/dead simulations freeze and day rollover handles midnight', () => {
  for (const flag of ['paused', 'dead']) {
    const state = makeState({ [flag]: true }), before = JSON.stringify(state);
    updateNeeds(state, 60); updateWeather(state, 60); updateCombat(state, 60);
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(state), before);
  }
  const a = makeState({ time: 1439 }), b = makeState({ time: 1439 });
  updateWeather(a, 1); updateWeather(b, 1);
  assert.equal(a.day, 2); assert.equal(a.time, 4); assert.deepEqual(a.weather, b.weather);
});

test('melee damages in front, respects walls and cooldown, and counts a kill once', () => {
  const state = makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie({ health: 10 }), makeZombie({ id: 3, x: 6.7 })] });
  const first = attack(state, emptyWorld);
  assert.equal(first.hit, true); assert.equal(first.killed, 1); assert.equal(state.player.kills, 1);
  assert.equal(state.zombies[1].health, 100, 'target behind survivor is outside swing');
  assert.equal(attack(state, emptyWorld).hit, false);
  assert.equal(state.player.kills, 1);
  const blocked = makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie({ x: 8.6 })] });
  assert.equal(attack(blocked, { isBlocked: x => x >= 8 && x < 8.4 }).hit, false);
  assert.equal(blocked.zombies[0].health, 100);
});

test('ranged attacks require and consume ammunition, generate noise, and clear feedback', () => {
  const state = makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie({ x: 12.5 })] });
  state.player.equipped = 'pistol'; state.player.inventory.push({ id: 'pistol', qty: 1 });
  assert.match(attack(state, emptyWorld).message, /ammunition/);
  state.player.inventory.push({ id: 'ammo', qty: 1 });
  assert.equal(attack(state, emptyWorld).hit, true);
  assert.equal(state.player.inventory.some(i => i.id === 'ammo'), false);
  assert.ok(state.noise >= 30); assert.ok(state.particles.length > 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) updateCombat(state, 1 / 60);
  assert.equal(state.particles.length, 0); assert.equal(state.player.attackCooldown, 0);
});

test('enemy strikes have cooldowns and cannot damage when current collision blocks attacks', () => {
  const state = makeState({ zombies: [makeZombie({ mode: 'attack', canAttack: true })] });
  updateCombat(state, 1 / 60); const health = state.player.health;
  assert.ok(health < 100);
  updateCombat(state, 1 / 60); assert.equal(state.player.health, health);
  state.zombies[0].attackCooldown = 0; state.zombies[0].canAttack = false; state.player.hurtCooldown = 0;
  updateCombat(state, 1 / 60); assert.equal(state.player.health, health);
});
